16
nūlakaṅkaḷ
muṉṉuri
“eṇṇeṇpa ēṉi eḻutteṉpa ivviraṇṭuṅ
kaṇṇeṉpa vāḻum uyirkku.”
makkaḷiṭiyē kalviyip parappuvataṟku nūlakaṅkaḷum, vācaka cālikaḷum ciṟantaṉavākum. mikamika muṟkālam mutal makkaḷ kalviyip parappuvataṟkup peritum muyaṉṟu vantārkaḷ. muṟkālattil puttakaṅkaḷi āyirakkaṇakkil orē kālattil acciṭak kūṭiya accup poṟikaḷ aṟiyap paṭātiruntamiyāl nūlkaḷ kiyiṉāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. akkālattil nūlkaḷ mika aritiṟ kiṭippaṉavāyum atika viliyuṭiyaṉavāyu miruntaṉa. ākavē, celvarkaḷē peritum nūlkaḷik kūlikoṭuttup paṭi eṭuttum vili koṭuttu vāṅkiyum payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. potu makkaḷukkuk kalvi virivurikaḷ (piracaṅkaṅkaḷ) mūlam aṟivuṟuttap paṭṭatu. itaṉālēyē ‘kaṟṟaliṟ kēṭṭal naṉṟu’ eṉṉum mutumoḻi eḻuntatu. muṉṉōr kalviyip parappuvatil evvaḷavu karuttuk koṇṭiruntārkaḷ eṉpatu palarukkut teriyātu. namatu nāṭṭilum piṟa nāṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭa paḻiya nūlakaṅkaḷiṉ varalāṟṟip payilvatāl nām kalvi vaḷarccikku nūlakaṅkaḷ evvaḷavu iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa eṉpatip paṟṟi aṟintukoḷḷutal kūṭum; iṉṟum pala nūlakaṅkaḷi yum vācakakālikaḷiyum niṟuva ūkkam koḷḷutalum kūṭum. in nūlil aṭaṅkiya karuttukkaḷ perumpālum āṅkila nūlkaḷilum, āṅkilat til veḷivarum tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭukaḷilu miruntu tiraṭṭap peṟṟavi.
ceṉṉi,
30.9.1948 na.ci. kantiyā
nūlakaṅkaḷ
tōṟṟuvāy
iṟṟikku āṟāyiram allatu ēḻāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ makkaḷiṭiyē nākarikam vaḷarcciyaṭintiruntiruntatu. ekiptu mēṟku āciyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ makkaḷ eḻuta aṟintiruntārkaḷ. itaṉi an nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭitta paḻam poruḷkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭu aṟikiṉṟōm. intiya makkaḷum aiyāyiram allatu āṟāyiram āṇṭukaḷukku muṉ eḻut teḻutum muṟiyi aṟintiruntārkaḷ. eṉpatic cintu veḷipputi poruḷ ārāycciyiṟ kiṭitta paḻam poruḷkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷa eḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭu aṟikiṉṟōm. ekiptilē āṟāyiram āṇṭu kaḷiṉ muṉ nūlakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. mēṟku āciyāvil aiyāyiram āṇṭu kaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa nūlakaṅkaḷ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. intiyāvil at tuṇip paḻaṅkālattil nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntamiyip paḻam poruḷ ārāycci mūlam aṟiyamuṭiyavilli. intiya, mēṟku āciya, ekiptiya paḻiya nākarikaṅkaḷ peritum orēvakiyaṉavāyiruntamiyā lum ak kālattil in nāṭukaḷukkiṭiyē pōkkuvarattu iruntu van tamiyālum mikap paḻiya nūl niliyaṅkaḷ intiya nāṭṭilum iruntaṉa eṉak koḷḷutal tavaṟuṭiyatākat tōṉṟamāṭṭātu. varalāṟṟuk kālat til intiya nāṭṭil kāṇappaṭṭa nūlniliyaṅkaḷip paṟṟi ārāyiṉ, ak kālattilē kalvinili intiyāvil evvaḷavu ōṅkiyirunta teṉ pati nām uyttu aṟintukoḷḷalām. ak kālam vaḻaṅkiya nūlkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa maṟintu pōṉamikkuk kāraṇattiyum viḷaṅkik koḷḷalām.
intiya makkaḷ eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷkaḷ.
alakcāntariṉ paṭi eṭuppukkālattil intiya makkaḷ pūrjāmarap paṭṭiyil eḻutiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu kēṭṭiyas eṉṉum urōmaṉ varalāṟṟāciriyar ki.pi.nālām nūṟṟāṇṭiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷār. ak kālap puttacamaya nūlkaḷum pirāmaṇamata nūlkaḷum itik kuṟittup palliṭaṅ kaḷiṟ kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷaṉa. marappaṭṭikaḷ iraṇṭari aṭi nīḷamum oru cāṇ akalamum uṭiyaṉavāka naṟukkappaṭṭu aḻuttam uṇṭākum poruṭṭu eṇṇey taṭavappaṭṭaṉa. ēṭukaḷ naṭuvilē tuḷi iṭappaṭṭu oṉṟiṉmītu oṉṟāka vittuk kayiṟṟil kōkkappaṭṭaṉa. kācumīrattiluḷḷa paṇṭitarkaḷiṉ nūl niliyaṅkaḷilum, oricāvilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum ivvaki nūlkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tuṇi
aracāṅka āvaṇaṅkaḷum, taṉippaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ āvaṇaṅkaḷum naṉku aṭittut tayārikkappaṭṭa tuṇiyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu āntira nāṭṭup paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṉum kaṇakkeḻutum puttakaṅ kaḷic ceykiṟārkaḷ. tuṇiyiṉ mītu puḷiyam vitip paciyit taṭavi ataṉ mēl kari pūcappaṭṭatu. eḻuttukkaḷ cuṇṇāmpuk kucciyiṉāl eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ivvakik kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷ yecelmīr (Jesalmir) mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
marappalakikaḷ
puttakurumār, māṇākkar pārttup payilum paṭi tamatu pōtaṉi kaḷi marappalakiyil eḻutiṉārkaḷ eṉṟu vināyakapiṭakam eṉṉum nūl kūṟukiṉṟatu. nākapāṉa eṉṉum cāka (Saka) aracaṉ kālattil kaṭaṉ campanta māṉa āvaṇaṅkaḷ marap palakikaḷil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu paṭṭiyaṅ kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. vaḻakku muṟipāṭukaḷ marappalakikaḷil cuṇṇāmpiṉāl eḻutappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉak kārttiyāyaṉar (ki.mu.400) kūṟiyuḷḷār. aracak kaṭṭaḷikaḷ macipūcappaṭṭa marappalakiyil eḻutap paṭṭaṉaveṉṟu taṇṭi eṉṉum camakkirutap pulavar kūṟiyuḷḷār. ivvaki marap palaki oṉṟu acāmil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. vaṭamēṟku ellip puṟaṅkaḷil vāḻum vaṟiya makkaḷ camaya campantamāṉa nūlkaḷi marap palakiyil cuṇṇāmpiṉāl eḻutukiṉṟārkaḷ.
ōlikaḷ
eḻutuvataṟkup paṉiyōli peritum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. intiyāvilum mattiya āciyāvilum aṇmiyil kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷāl ki.pi. nālām nūṟṟāṇṭil ōli payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu. puttarukkup piṉ mutaṉmutaṟ kūṭiya puttacapi nikaḻccikaḷ paṉi ōliyil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa eṉṉum kaṉṉaparamparic ceyti hiyaṉticiyāṅ eṉṉum cīṉa yāttirikaṉ kālattil vaḻaṅkiṟṟu. eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttum ōlikaḷ mutalil ularaviṭappaṭṭaṉa; piṉpu avittut taṇṇīril ūṟa viṭappaṭṭaṉa; piṟaku veḷiyē eṭuttuk kāya viṭappaṭṭapiṉ kalliṉāl allatu caṅkiṉāl aḻuttañceyyappaṭṭaṉa. piṉpu avi takunta aḷavukku naṟukkappaṭṭaṉa. perumpālum ōlic caṭṭaṅkaḷil nīḷam oru aṭimutal mūṉṟu aṭi variyum akalam oṉṟē kāl aṅkulam mutal nālu aṅkulam variyum iruntaṉa. ivvēṭukaḷiṉ mītu maciyiṉāl allatu eḻuttāṇiyiṉāl eḻutappaṭṭatu. eḻuttāṇi yiṉāl eḻutiya eḻuttukkaḷ karipūcik karumiyākkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvakic caṭṭaṅkaḷ oṉṟiṉ mītu oṉṟāka aṭukki naṭuvil tuḷiyiṭṭuk kayiṟṟi ṉāl kōkkappaṭṭu mēlum kīḻum marac caṭṭaṅkaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa.
tōlum tantamum
paḻaṅkāla makkaḷ eḻutuvataṟkut tōlip payaṉpaṭuttiṉārkaḷ. mattiya āciyāvil tōlil eḻutappaṭṭa puttakaṅkaḷ kaṇṭu piṭikkap paṭṭaṉa. acuttamuṭiyatu eṉṉum kāraṇattiṉāl tōl atikam payaṉ paṭuttappaṭavilli. tantam mika aritāka eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. tantac caṭṭaṅkaḷil eḻutiya nūl oṉṟu parmāvil kiṭittuḷḷatu.
ulōkattakaṭukaḷ
ulōkat takaṭukaḷ paḻaṅkālattil eḻutuvataṟkup peritum payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. vīṭṭu nikaḻccikaḷum araciṉar naṉkoṭikaḷum ulōkat takaṭukaḷil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu cātakak katikaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ivvaki ulōkat takaṭu oṉṟu takka cīlattiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. paṭṭippurōlu eṉṉum iṭattil puttakōyil aḻipāṭu oṉṟi akaḻnta pōtu veḷḷi itaḻkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa kiyeḻuttuppaṭi oṉṟu kaṇṭu eṭukkappaṭṭatu. ilaṇṭaṉ nūtaṉa poruṭ kāṭcic cāliyil veḷḷi mulām pūcappaṭṭavum veḷḷittakaṭu mēl iṭappaṭṭavum paṉi ōli kaḷil eḻutappaṭṭavumāṉa nūlkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṟkālattil cepput takaṭukaḷ eḻutap peritum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. mavuriya aracar kālattil cepput takaṭukaḷil araca kaṭṭaḷikaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. putta camayak koḷkiyiṉarākiya pukaḻ peṟṟa kaṉiṣka eṉṉum cakkaravartti camayanūlkaḷic ceppu ēṭukaḷil eḻutivikkumpaṭi ceytāṉ eṉa hiyaṉticiyāṅ kūṟiyuḷḷāṉ. ariya ilakkiya nūlkaḷum ceppu ēṭukaḷil eḻutivikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpataṟkuc cāṉṟukaḷ uḷḷaṉa. tāṟṟappakkā kuṭumpattiṉar ivvāṟu poṟippitta ilakkiya campantamāṉa nūlkaḷ tiruppati ālayattil uḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟu parmāvilum ilaṅkiyilum kāppāṟṟappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ ilaṇṭaṉ nūtaṉa poruṭkāṭcic cāliyil uḷḷaṉa. cepput takaṭṭu itaḻkaḷ oru pakkattil tuḷiyiṭṭuc cempu vaḷiyattil kōkkappaṭṭaṉa. eḻuttukkaḷ paḻutaṭiyāmal irukkumpaṭi itaḻkaḷiṉ ōraṅkaḷ uyaramākkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
karuṅkallum ceṅkallum
karuṅkal, ceṅkal eṉpavikaḷum eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. intiya nāṭṭil palavakik kaṟkaḷ eḷitil kiṭikkak kūṭiyavi. ivi kaḷil aṟakkoṭikaḷum, naṉkoṭikaḷum eḻuti vikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvaki eḻuttukkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭa kaṟkaḷi intiyā muḻumiyilum nām kāṇalām. acōka cakkaravarttiyum muṉṉōr vaḻakkip piṉ paṟṟiyē taṉatu kaṭṭaḷikaḷi malikaḷilum kaṟṟūṇkaḷilum poṟittār. ilakkiya campantamāṉa nūlkaḷ cilavum kaṟkaḷiṟ poṟikkappaṭṭaṉa. ivvaki nūlkaḷ cila akappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nālām vikkiramaṉ eṉṉum aracaṉālum avaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉip pulavar cōmatēvarālum ceyyappaṭṭa nāṭakamoṉṟu ivvāṟu poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ajmīr (Ajmere) eṉṉum iṭattil ciṉa talapurāṇac curukkaṅkaḷ cilavum ivvāṟu poṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avi irāca puttāṉāvil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. puttamatat toṭarpāṉa cūttiraṅkaḷ poṟikkappaṭṭa ceṅkaṟkaḷ pala vaṭa mēṟku ellip puṟaṅkaḷiṟ kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
intiyāvilē muṟkālattil kākitam mika arumiyākap payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil kākitam payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa teṉpataṟku ātāramuṇṭu. 14ām nūṟṟāṇṭil eḻutappaṭṭa kākitak kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷ māḷavam kūrccaram mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭittaṉa.
nūlkaḷ eḻuta mi payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu
eḻutuvataṟku maci payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu purāṇaṅkaḷil maci pāttirā, maci pāṇṭā, maci kūpā mutaliya coṟkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ki.pi.5ām nūṟṟāṇṭil vāḻnta nirccas (Nearchus) eṉpār intiya makkaḷ marap paṭṭikaḷilum tuṇiyilum maciyāl eḻutuvatip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭ ṭuḷḷār. iṉi nūl niliyaṅkaḷ evvāṟu amikkappaṭṭaṉa eṉpatip paṟṟip paṭippōm.
nūlakaṅkaḷ aminta vaki
vēta kālattil nūlakaṅkaḷ tēvippaṭavilli. ak kālattil kalvi, camayat toṭarpākavum, kurukkaḷ vakuppiṉarukku māttiram uriya tākavum iruntatu. toḻil toṭarpāṉa kalvi parampari muṟiyāka vantatu. ak kālattil kalvikkuriyavarkaḷā yirunta kurumār, oruvar colla maṟṟavar kēṭṭu maṉappāṭañceytalākiya muṟiyiṉāl kalviyip parappiṉārkaḷ. itaṟkiṭiyil cātikkaṭṭuppāṭu valuvaṭintatu. paṭippup pirāmaṇariṉ taṉiyurimi peṟuvatāyiṟṟu. maṟṟa vakuppiṉar avar avarkaḷukku aṉumatikkappaṭṭa kalviyi māttiram payiṉṟaṉar. immuṟi nīṇṭakālam naṭipeṟṟu varuvatāyiṟṟu. appoḻutu vēta kāla ilakkiyaṅkaḷ atikappaṭṭaṉa; kaliyum viññāṉamum vaḷarcci yaṭintaṉa. muṟkālattiṟpōla ellāvaki nūlkaḷiyum oruvaṉāl payilvatu muṭiyāmal iruntatu. maṟṟavarkaḷukkut taṉatu kalviyi aḷikka maṉamillātirunta vittuvāṉ ippoḻutu iruvakit tuṉpaṅ kaḷukku uḷḷāṉāṉ. taṉkāla nūlkaḷippaṭippataṟku aṉumatikkappaṭā tirunta vakuppiṉarum payilumpaṭi eḻuti vippatu oṉṟu; maṟṟatu tali muṟi talimuṟiyākak kāppāṟṟappaṭṭu vantavikaḷuḷ oruvaṉāl māttiram niṉivil vittukkoḷḷamuṭiyātavikaḷi maṟantu pōkumpaṭi viṭutal. atiṭṭavacamāka eḻuti vikkum muṟiyē kiyāḷap paṭṭatu. eḻutum muṟi naṉṟāka aṟiyappaṭṭiruntatu. avarkaḷ tamatu karuttukkaḷi nūlāka eḻutiṉar. paṭittavarkaḷiṭiyē periya māṟṟam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. nūlkaḷi eḻuti vippatu camayak kaṭamiyākak koḷḷap paṭṭatu. itu kāraṇamākavē intiyāvil nūlakaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa. nūlkaḷ atikamāyiṉa. avikaḷik kāppāṟṟuvatu mukkiyam eṉak karutappaṭṭatu. puttakaṅkaḷik kāppāṟṟi vippataṟkuk kōvilkaḷ ciṟanta iṭaṅkaḷākak karutappaṭṭaṉa. ivvāṟu puttakaṅkaḷ cērttuvikkappaṭṭa iṭaṅkaḷ caracuvati paṇṭāraṅkaḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. puttar kālattil nūlakaṅkaḷ potu vāka eṅkum kāṇappaṭṭaṉa.
kalikkaḻakaṅkaḷ
ciṟappākac cila iṭaṅkaḷil mikap pala nūlkaḷ cērttu vikkappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. avi kōvilkaḷ, kalvi kaḻakaṅkaḷ, maṭaṅkaḷ, araṇmaṉikaḷ eṉpaṉa. paḻiya kālattum mattiya kālattum pulavarkaḷ kalvik kaḻakaṅkaḷi liruntu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa māṇavarukkuk kalvi kaṟpittārkaḷ. makka ḷiṭiyē tōṉṟiyirunta kalvi kaṟkum ārvattiṉāl pala nūlniliyaṅ kaḷi amikka vēṇṭiya kaṭṭāyam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. puttacamayam ōṅki yirunta kālattil pala tuṟikaḷil kalvi payila vēṇṭiyiruntatu. makāyāṉa camayan tōṉṟiyapōtu mikap pala ilakkiyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟalāyiṉa. oppiṭṭup payilutal kāraṇamākap piṟamatak kōṭpāṭukaḷum kaṟpikkap paṭṭaṉa. nālantā, vikkiramacīlam, oṭatapuri mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil ariya nūlkaḷ aṭaṅkiya nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. 12ām 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭu kaḷil mukammatiya varalāṟṟu nūlāciriyarkaḷ eḻutiya kuṟippukkaḷil innūl niliyaṅkaḷip paṟṟiya kuṟippukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
puttarukku muṟpaṭṭa kālam mutal ovvoru kōviliyuṭiya pakutikaḷ tōṟum kalvik kaḻakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. āṇṭiṉ cila kālaṅkaḷil pulavarkaḷ aṅkuk kūṭiṉārkaḷ eṉa vaciṭṭar, kutamar, pōtāyaṉar mutaliya paḻiya nīti nūlāciriyarkaḷ kūṟiyuḷḷārkaḷ. avvāṟu avarkaḷ kūṭiyatu putiya caṭṭaṅkaḷic ceytal, caṭṭaṅkaḷi mīṟuvatāl uṇṭākum piṇakkukaḷit tīrttu vittal eṉpavikaḷukkāka vākum. ik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ ciṟappākac cila paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷiṟ kūṭiṉa. itaṟkuk kāraṇam av viṭaṅkaḷil periya nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntamiyē. kōviṟ pakutikaḷi luḷḷa nūlakaṅkaḷukku aracarum celvarum naṉkoṭi aḷittārkaḷ.
kātikā(Ghatika) eṉap peyar peṟṟa kalvik kaḻakaṅkaḷ, kiṟittu vukkup piṉ cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil kalvikku mattiya iṭaṅkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉa. kātikā eṉṉum kalvik kaḻakaṅkaḷil camaya campantamāṉa tarkkaṅkaḷ naṭipeṟṟaṉa. kōvilōṭu campantappaṭṭirunta kaḻakaṅkaḷil palavakik karumaṅkaḷ ārāyappaṭṭaṉa. kātikā eṉṉum kaḻakaṅkaḷ kalvi campantamāṉa karumam oṉṟil māttiram karuttuc celuttiṉa. kaṭampa araca parampariyi nāṭṭiya mayūracarmaṉ, tarukkattil tērcci aṭiyum poruṭṭuk kātikā eṉṉum kalvik kaḻakaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟukkuc ceṉṟāṉ. kiḻakkuc cāḷukkiya paṭṭiyamoṉṟu āntira nāṭṭil acāṉupuratti lirunta kātikāvip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟatu. iraṇṭām naracimma vākaṉ eṉṉum pallava aracaṉ kāñciyil oru kaḻakatti amittāṉ. tarukkat til veṟṟiyaṭintavarkaḷukkup paṭṭaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. kaḻakaṅ kaḷukku naṉkoṭikaḷum māṉiyaṅkaḷum aḷikkappaṭṭaṉa. kātikā eṉṉum kaḻakaṅkaḷil nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. itu nicām irācciyat til nāki eṉṉum iṭattil kiṭitta paḻam poruḷkaḷāl naṉku valiyuṟukiṉṟatu. ki.pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil poṟikkappaṭṭa cāḷukkiya aracariṉ paṭṭayaṅkaḷ ‘kākita cāli’ yip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṭṭiyattiṟ kuṟippiṭappaṭṭa vācakacāli, āṟu nūl niliya mēṟ pārviyāḷarāl (Librarians) naṭattappaṭṭatu. nūl niliyam irunta kaṭṭaṭamum kaṇṭu piṭittup pātukākkappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
tamiḻc caṅkam
kātikā eṉṉum kaḻakaṅkaḷ potukkalviyil karuttic celuttiṉa. teṉṉintiya caṅkam ilakkiyaṅkaḷ campantamāṉa kalviyi vaḷarttatu. caṅkam eṉpatu pulavarkaḷum kaṟṟavarkaḷum cērnta capi. nalla ilak kiyaṅkaḷ ac capiyāl ēṟṟukkoḷḷappaṭṭaṉa. maturiyiliruntu āṭci purinta pāṇṭiya aracar atip paripālittārkaḷ. caṅkam aracariṉ ātara vil iruntamiyāl caṅkattil mikap periya nūlniliyam niṟuvappaṭ ṭiruntatu. mukammatiyar āṭcik kālattil ṭilliyilum ākrāvilum kalvik kaḻakaṅkaḷ pala iruntaṉa. ovvoru kaḻakattilum nūlniliyamum iruntatu. ākavē, caṅkattil periya nūl niliyam iruntateṉak kūṟutal tavaṟākātu. innūl niliyattil tokuttu vikkappaṭṭirunta pāṭal kaḷiṉ tokuppē pattuppāṭṭu, eṭṭuttoki eṉṉum peyarkaḷuṭaṉ vaḻaṅkum caṅka nūlkaḷākum.
puttamaṭaṅkaḷ
muṟkāla vikārikaḷum caṅkirāmaṅkaḷum kalvi vaḷarcci campanta māṉa atika vēlikaḷic ceytaṉa. putta tarumaṅkaḷip piṉpaṟṟiya varkaḷ paḻiya paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷil maṭaṅkaḷ palavaṟṟi amittārkaḷ. avikaḷil putta kurumār taṅyiruntu putta camayattip parappi vantārkaḷ. mēṟku intiyāvilē vallapāy nāṭṭu aracaṉ oruvaṉ ki.pi. 6ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭil tuttāvilirunta putta maṭattukku nūlkaḷ vāṅkum poruṭṭu aḷitta naṉkoṭiyip paṟṟiya cāṉṟu oṉṟu kiṭittuḷḷatu. ivvāṟu vili koṭuttu vāṅkappaṭṭavi allātaṉavum, puttakurumārāl pārttup paṭi eḻutappaṭṭaṉavumākiya kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷil mikap pala puttamaṭa nūl niliyaṅkaḷil iruntaṉavātal vēṇṭum.
ciṉa maṭaṅkaḷ
putta matattiṉarip pōlavē ciṉa matattiṉarum ciṉattuṟavikaḷ taṅkiyirukkum upacirāyā eṉṉum maṭaṅkaḷik kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ. putta camayattiṉariṉ vikāri, caṅkirāmaṅkaḷip pōlavē im maṭaṅkaḷ camaya vaḷarcciyiṉ poruṭṭup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. potumakkaḷ im maṭaṅ kaḷukku naṉ koṭi aḷittārkaḷ. puttakaṅkaḷip paṭi eḻutum celaviṉ poruṭṭum palar naṉkoṭi aḷittirukkiṟārkaḷ. maṭaṅkaḷil vāḻnta tuṟavikaḷ paṭi eḻutiya nūlkaḷōṭu kūli koṭuttup paṭi eḻutappaṭṭa kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷum cērntu nūlakaṅkaḷiṉ nūlkaḷ mikap perukiṉa. iṉṟum mēṟku intiyāviluḷḷa upacirāyākkaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. āmatāpāttil māttiram uḷḷa nūlakattil “āvāyaka cūttiram” eṉṉum ciṉa nūliṉ kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷ nāṉūṟu kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
kōvilkaḷ
vikāri, upacirāyākkaḷ pōlavē intiyariṉ kōvilkaḷ iruntaṉa. kōvilkaḷ kalvikku mattiya iṭaṅkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉa. aṅkup potumakkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttum poruṭṭu nūlakaṅkaḷ niṟuvappaṭṭiruntaṉa. kupta aracar kālattil kōvilkaḷ mikap pukaḻ oṅki viḷaṅkiṉa. mukammati yar vaṭanāṭṭiṉ mītu paṭi eṭuttapōtu intiya kalikaḷ teṉṉintiyā vilum takkāṇattilum ceṉṟu otuṅkiṉa. kōvilkaḷ ak kalikaḷik kāppāṟṟi vittup piṉpu avikaḷi vēkamākap paravac ceytaṉa. kalvi campantamāṉa ceytikaḷi viḷakkum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. teṉṉintiya kōvil amippil pala maṇṭapaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. naṭaṉa maṇṭapam, purāṇa maṇṭapam, virivuri (viyākkiyāṉa) maṇṭapam eṉpaṉa avaṟṟuṭ cila. virivuri maṇṭapattil ilakkaṇam kaṟpikkappaṭṭatu. purāṇa maṇṭapattil purāṇam payilappaṭṭatu. ovvoru kōvilkaḷilirunta āciriyar māṇavarkaḷip paṟṟiyum avar kaḷiṉ kaṭamikaḷip paṟṟiyum paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. tirupuvaṉattiṟ kiṭitta paṭṭiyamoṉṟu aṅkup pala āciriyarkaḷum 360 māṇavar kaḷum iruntārkaḷ eṉṟu kūṟukiṉṟatu. eṇṇāyiram, tirumukkūṭal mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum ivvakiyāṉa amippukaḷ iruntaṉa. tirumuk kūṭalil oru kalācāli, ōr uṇavuccāli, oru maruntuccāli irun taṉaveṉṟu paṭṭiyaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. āntira tēcattilē cirīcilam , tirākcarāma, tantaram, tiripurāntakam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷilum iv vaki amippukkaḷiruntaṉa. ik kōvilkaḷil palvaki nūlkaḷaṭaṅkiya nūlakaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. nūlakaṅkaḷukku nūlkaḷi utavuvatu celvariṉ kaṭami eṉṟu purāṇaṅkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru kōvililum nūl nili yaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉṟu nām tuṇitaṟku mēṟkūṟiyavi cāṉṟukaḷākum.
maṭaṅkaḷ
kōvilkaḷ potu muṟiyāṉa kalviyip parappiṉa. caṅkarar, irāmaṉucar, matvar pōṉṟavarkaḷ cārpil tōṉṟiya maṭaṅkaḷil antanta matattalivarkaḷiṉ koḷki campantamāṉa kalvi kaṟpikkappaṭṭatu. ivikaḷōṭu pācava,cittaṉiya matak koḷkikaḷip parappum maṭaṅkaḷum eḻuntaṉa. ciriṅkēri, tuvāraki, pūri, kāci, kāttiyavār, pāṭāri mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil caṅkarar maṭaṅkaḷi amittār. mēlkōṭu, maṉṉārkōyil akōpilam mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil irāmaṉucar maṭaṅkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. matvar uṭuppiyil ēḻumaṭaṅkaḷit toṭakkiṉār. mātvariṉ koḷkiyip piṉpaṟṟuvōr piṟkālaṅkaḷil pala kiḷi maṭaṅkaḷik kaṭṭiṉar. pācava matattiṉar karnāṭakattil pala maṭaṅkaḷit tōṟṟuvittaṉar. 12ām 13ām nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil tākala (Dahala) nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum kōlaki (Golagi) maṭattiṉar teṟkē vantu cōḻa, kēraḷa aracaruṭiyavum āntira nāṭṭuk kākattiyaruṭiyavum ātaravip peṟṟuk kāḷamukam eṉṉum civamatattiṉ oru vakik koḷkiyip parappiṉar. ivarkaḷ mantaram, puṭpakiri, tiripurāntakam, tirupparaṅkuṉṟam, maturi, ceyyūr, tēvikāpuram mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil maṭaṅkaḷi amittaṉar. im maṭaṅkaḷil vētāntak koḷki toṭarpāṉa kiyeḻuttuc cuvaṭikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pellakamvi(Balagamvi)yiluḷḷa kōṭiyā (Kadiyad) maṭattil vētāntam, kumārā, pāṇiṉi, cākatāyaṉar mutaliyōriṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷ, taricaṉaṅkaḷ, yōkam, purāṇam, itikācam,tarmacāttiram mutaliya pāṭaṅkaḷ māṇava rukkuk kaṟpikkappaṭṭaṉa. paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷ pōṉṟu payaṉaḷitta maṭaṅ kaḷil nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa eṉpatu uṇmiyākum. tiruvāṭutuṟi, tiruppaṉantāḷ pōṉṟa civa maṭaṅkaḷilum paḻiya nūlkaḷ tokuttu vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa.
araṇmaṉikaḷ
muṟkāla intiya aracar kalviyi ātarittu vantārkaḷ. atu avarkaḷiṉ kaṭamikaḷuḷ oṉṟāka viruntatu. kaṉiṣka, harṣa mutaliya putta matappaṟṟuṭiya aracarkaḷum kalviyi ātarittārkaḷ. kupta aracar kālattil kalvi, camaya viḻippukaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. appoḻutu pulavar kaḷukku atika ātaravu uṇṭākiyiruntatu. kuptar kālappaṭṭiyaṅkaḷ perumpālum aracarkaḷukku vaḻaṅkappaṭṭa koṭikaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭukiṉṟaṉa. aracaṉuṭiya perumi avaṉ celvattiṉ aḷavukku irukkavilli; avaṉ araṇmaṉiyi alaṅkaritta pulavarkaḷiṉ eṇ aḷavukku iruntatu. mattiya kāla intiya varalāṟu pulavarkaḷiṉ ātaravip paṟṟiya varalāṟākavē irukkiṉṟatu. vaṭanāṭṭil mukammatiyar paṭi eṭuppu iruntapōtu intiya kalikaḷ takkāṇam, kācumīram, nēpāḷam mutaliya nāṭukaḷil tañcam pukuntaṉa. aracar palar pulavarkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉārkaḷ. kalviyiṟ ciṟanta palar aracar araṇmaṉiyil iruntārkaḷ. ki.pi. 1000 mutal 1500 variyil takkāṇam kalvikkup poṟkālamāka viḷaṅkiṟṟu. pōcaṉ araṇmaṉiyilum cāḷukkiya aracar araṇmaṉiyilum kāṇap paṭṭa nūlakaṅkaḷik koṇṭu aracar araṇmaṉikaḷil nūlakaṅkaḷ irun taṉaveṉṟu tuṇiyalām. araṇmaṉiyiluḷḷa kalvi maṇṭapattil pula varkaḷ pala karuttukkaḷi vātittaṉar; veṟṟiyāḷarukkuc caṉmāṉaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkappaṭṭaṉa. vicayanakara araṇmaṉip pulavarākiya cirināta eṉpavar poṉmuḻuk kāṭṭappaṭṭār. kalvi maṇṭapaṅkaḷil vātaṅkaḷ purivataṟku nūlakaṅkaḷ iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa. paḻiya nūlakaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭum paṭṭiyaṅkaḷ cilavē kiṭittuḷḷaṉa. mukammatiya paṭi eḻuccik kālaṅkaḷil nērnta aḻivu vēlikaḷāl avi maṟintaṉavākalām. mokalāya cakravartti oruvaṉ tāṉ tiṉamum kuḷikkum vennīri ēṭu kaḷi erittuc cuṭac ceytal vēṇṭumeṉak kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ. pāskara camkiti eṉṉum nūl nūlakaṅkaḷil nūlkaḷ evvāṟu vikkappaṭruttal vēṇṭumeṉak kūṟukiṉṟatu. nūlakam aḻakāka amikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭiṭat til iruttal vēṇṭum. tuṇiyāl cuṟṟik kayiṟṟiṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭapiṉ nūlkaḷ irumput taṭṭukaḷ mītu nīḷap pakkamāka oṉṟiṉ mītu oṉṟāka aṭukkappaṭutal vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu amikkappaṭṭa nūlakaṅkaḷ mēṟ pārvikkāraṉiṉ kavaṉippil iruntaṉa. nūlkaḷip pattiramākak kāppāṟṟuvatu māttiramallāmal paṭippavarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya utavi aḷippatum avaṉ kaṭamiyāka viruntatu. nūlakatti mēṟpārppavaṉ palavaki nūṟkalviyilum tēṟiyavaṉāka viruntāṉ.
intu nūlakaṅkaḷ
puttar kālattukku muṉ pala nūlakaṅkaḷum kalācālikaḷum iruntaṉa.
takkacīlam: puttar kālattukku muṉ intiyāvil viḷaṅkiya periya kalācālikaḷuḷ oṉṟu takkacīlattil iruntatu. kutamaputtar mutaliyōr ik kalācāliyilēyē kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. artta cāttiram ceyta kuṭaliyarum ik kalācāliyilēyē payiṉṟār. jappāṉ, koriyā, kirīs mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntu vanta māṇavarum ikkalā cāliyiṟ kalvi payiṉṟaṉar. nūlkaḷip paṭiyeḻutum poruṭṭup peritum piṟa nāṭṭār iṅkut taṅkiyiruntārkaḷ. takkacīlattil nūlakamum kalācāliyum ki.mu.600 mutal ki.pi. 40 variyil iruntaṉa.
kāci: takka cīlattukku aṭuttapaṭiyiluḷḷatu kāci. itu puṉita iṭa mākak koḷḷap paṭṭamiyāl, iṅkup pala vittuvāṉkaḷum māṇavarum taṅkiyiruntaṉar. iṉṟum kāciyil kalvipayilum māṇavar uyarvuṭiyavar kaḷākak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru paṇṭitariṭattum palanūlkaḷ iruntaṉa. māṇavar avikaḷip paṭi eḻutip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. ivvāṟu kāciyiliruntu paṭi eṭuttukkoṇṭu varappaṭṭa kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷ pala āntira nāṭṭil uḷḷaṉa. kuṟikkappaṭṭa oru kaliyil tērcci peṟuva taṟku oru māṇavaṉukkup paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷ ceṉṟaṉa.
kācumīram: kāciyip pōlavē kācumīramum kalvikku iruppiṭamā yiruntatu. kācumīrattil cāratāpīṭam eṉṉum pēr pōṉa kalvikkaḻakam oṉṟu iruntatu. itu tamiḻc caṅkattip pōla nūlkaḷiṉ takutikaḷi nōkki avikaḷukkut tamatu ēṟṟukkoḷḷutali aḷittatu. kācumīrat til iṉṟum ciṟanta kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. paḻiya cāratāpīṭam periya nūlakamāka viḷaṅkiṟṟu.
nāṭṭilirunta nūlakaṅkaḷ: kalviccālikaḷ, taṉippaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ vīṭukaḷallāta paṭṭiṉaṅkaḷ kōvilkaḷilum nūlkaḷ cērttu vikkap paṭṭaṉa. pīcappūr avvaki iṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟu. mukammatiyar kālattiṉ muṉ atu vittiyāpuram eṉṉum peyar peṟṟuk kalviyi vaḷarttu vantatu. mēṟkuc cāḷukkiya aracaṉākiya kaliyāṉ iṅku aḻakiya kaṭṭaṭa moṉṟi amittāṉ. nūlkaḷ cērttup pātukāttu vikkappaṭṭa kaṭṭaṭattiṉ aḻipāṭu iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvaki aḻipāṭu kāṇappaṭum iṉṉoru iṭam nākar kōṭs. pirōp taklāk eṉṉum cultāṉ ivviṭattic cūṟiyāṭiṉāṉ. civalāmukiyil mikap pala intu nūlkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa veṉṟu kēḷviyuṟṟa cultāṉ aṅkuc ceṉṟu ciṟanta nūlkaḷit terintuteṭuttu avikaḷip pāracīka moḻip paṭuttuvittāṉ.
aracariṉ araṇmaṉikaḷ: aracarkaḷiṉ araṇmaṉikaḷ kalviyiyum nūl niliyaṅkaḷiyum vaḷarkkum iṭaṅkaḷāka viruntaṉa. kaṟṟāri ātarippatu intu aracariṉ kaṭamiyākaviruntatu. paḻaṅkālap pulavarkaḷ varalāṟukaḷāl evvāṟu pulavarkaḷ araṇmaṉikaḷi alaṅ karittārkaḷ eṉṟum, vātaṅkaḷil veṟṟi peṟṟār evvāṟu caṉmāṉikkap paṭṭārkaḷ eṉṟum aṟikiṉṟōm. pala nūlkaḷ aracariṉ ātaravu peṟṟu eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. aracariṟ palar pulavarkaḷāka viḷaṅkiṉar. avarkaḷ tamatu kālattum, tamakku muṉṉum tōṉṟiya ilakkiyaṅkaḷit tiraṭṭi vit tiruntārkaḷ. paḻiya nūlakaṅkaḷip paṟṟic cila cāṉṟukaḷē uḷḷaṉa. irācciyaṅkaḷōṭu nūlakaṅkaḷiyum mukammatiyar aḻittamiyē itaṟkuk kāraṇam. mattiya kāla nūl niliyaṅkaḷuṭ ciṟantatu pōca rācaṉuṭiyatu.
iṉṟu intiya aracarkaḷiṉ araṇmaṉikaḷil nūlakaṅkaḷ kāṇap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi talimuṟi talimuṟiyākat toṭarntu varuvaṉa. pikkanēr, jammu, micūr, tañcāvūr, ceyappūr nēpāḷam mutaliya iṭaṅ kaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum nūlniliyaṅkaḷē itaṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭukkaḷākum. jōṭpūr(Joput) araṇmaṉi nūlakattil 1800 kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷum pala acciṭṭa nūlkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. pikkanēril 2000 kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa. nēpāḷattil 5000-kku mēṟpaṭṭa kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷ uṇṭu. tañcāvūr nūl niliyam kācumīra nūl niliyattukku oppāṉatu. tañcāvūr nūl niliyam 16ām nūṟṟāṇṭil nāyakka aracarāl ārampikkappaṭṭatu. ivvaracar nūṟu āṇṭukaḷākap pala nūlkaḷic cērttār. tañcāvūri veṟṟi koṇṭa aracarum pala putiya nūlkaḷic cērttu vittaṉar. intiyāviṉ ellāp pakutikaḷiliruntum tiraṭṭi iṅkē vikkappaṭṭa kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷiṉ eṇṇikki 18000. in nūlakattiṉ matippu ēḻari ilaṭcam eṉa matikkappaṭṭuḷḷatu. nicām irācciyattilum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilum talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka varum taṉippaṭṭavarkaḷiṉ nūl niliyaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
puttamata nūlakaṅkaḷ
puttamatam ōṅkiyiruntapōtu kalvi campantamāṉa niliyaṅkaḷ mika ātaravu peṟṟiruntaṉa. putta camayak koḷkikaḷ piṟanāṭukaḷiṟ paraviṉamiyāl putta camayattit taḻuviya āyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ intiya nāṭṭukku yāttiri ceytārkaḷ. kaṟṟavarkaḷ mikak kuravikkap paṭṭamiyāl uyartarak kalvikku atika matippiruntatu. itaṉāl pala viṭaṅkaḷil kalācālikaḷum nūl akaṅkaḷum tōṉṟiṉa. yāttirikar akkālak kalvi muṟiyip paṟṟik kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷārkaḷ. putta tuṟavikaḷ camayac caṭṭaṅkaḷiyum nīticcaṭṭaṅkaḷiyum palakikaḷil eḻutiṉārkaḷ. celvarkaḷiṉ kuṭumpac ceytikaḷ puttakaṅkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nūlakaṅkaḷum kalviccālikaḷum uḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷilēyē puttamatam paraviyiruntatu. nālantāvil kalvi aracariṉ ātaravu peṟṟiruntu, piṟkāla kuptar aṅku maṭaṅkaḷ amittu avikaḷukku māṉiyam vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. hiyaṉticiyāṅ eṉṉum cīṉa yāttirikaṉ kālattil aracaṉ nālantāk kalācālikku nūṟu kirāmaṅkaḷiṉ varuvāyi māṉiyamaḷittāṉ. iṟciṅ (Itsingh) eṉṉum cīṉayāttirikaṉ nālantāviliruntu pala camakkiruta nūl kaḷic cēkarittāṉ; hiyaṉticāṅ 650 kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷik koṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ. aḻipāṭukaḷi veṭṭic cōtitta pōtu kiṭitta ceppup paṭṭiyamoṉṟil tēvapālā eṉṉum vaṅkāḷa aracaṉ putta camayam campantamāṉa nūlkaḷip paṭi eḻutuvataṟku aḷitta māṉiyattiṉ viparam kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. āyirakkaṇakkāṉa putta tuṟavikaḷ nālantā maṭattil iruntārkaḷ.
vikkirama cīlam: nālantāk kalācāliyil aṉṉiya nāṭṭiṉar vantu taṅkik kalvi payiṉṟārkaḷ. vikkirama cīlam aracarāl amikkappaṭṭatu. aṅku aracariṉ uyarnta karumakkārar pulavarukkup paṭṭam vaḻaṅkiṉārkaḷ. tipettiya varalāṟukaḷ ik kalviccāliyip paṟṟiya pala ceytikaḷi viḷakkukiṉṟaṉa. iṅkuḷḷa kalvic cālikkut tarumapālar eṉṉum aracaṉ ki.pi.9ām nūṟṟāṇṭil taḷamiṭṭāṉ. ik kalācāli nāṉūṟu āṇṭukaḷ naṭipeṟṟatu. itu āṟupēr aṭaṅkiya capiyāl naṭattap paṭṭatu. ic capikkut talivar camaya kuruvākaviruntār. aṅku āṟukalā cālikaḷ iruntaṉa. ovvoṉṟilum 108 āciriyarkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. ak kaṭṭaṭam matilāṟ cūḻappaṭṭu 8000 makkaḷ taṅkak kūṭiya vicālamuṭiyatā yiruntatu. ik kalācālikaḷukku ayalē nūlniliyamoṉṟiruntatu.
namatu nāṭṭilē kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷ cēkarittu nūl niliyaṅ kaḷil vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. anniliyaṅkaḷ pārati paṇṭāram allatu caracuvati paṇṭāram eṉappaṭṭaṉa. avi kōvilkaḷōṭum araṇmaṉi kaḷōṭum iṇikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. pāṉā (kipi.620) tamakkuc contamāka oru nūlakatti amittiruntār. hiyaṉticiyāṅ ērāḷamāṉa nūlkaḷi 20 kutirikaḷil ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉ kipi. 640il vallāpi puratti aṭintapōtu aṅkup periya nūl niliyamiruntatu. an niliyattiṉ pukaḻ cīṉāvil eṭṭiyiruntatu. cittiramati, tiṉamati eṉṉum iraṇṭu putta tuṟavikaḷ cīṉāvukku aḻikkappaṭṭārkaḷ. ciramaṇapuṇ ṇiyō pāyā eṉpavar 1500 nūlkaḷi intiyāviliruntu cīṉāvukku eṭuttuc ceṉṟār. ki.pi. 11ām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiya pōcaṉiṉ araṇmaṉiyil periya nūl niliyamiruntatu. cayacimhatēva cittarāca eṉṉum cāḷukkiya cakkaravartti māḷavatti ki.pi. 1140il veṉṟapōtu aṉkilvāṭ paṭṭiṉattukku (Anhilvad patan) ataṉi eṭuttuc ceṉṟāṉ. kucarāttiya cāḷukkiyar kālattil pala nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. emacantirāccāriyārum avar māṇavarum pala nūlkaḷic cēkarittup paṭi eṭuttuc ciṉa kōviṟ paṇṭāraṅkaḷil vittārkaḷ.
ki.pi. 1200il puttakam paṭi eṭuppatu kaliyāka vaḷarcciyaṭintatu. mukammatiya paṭi eṭuppāḷariṉ aḻivu vēlikkup piṟpāṭum kampēyi luḷḷa ciṉa nūl niliyattil 30,000 kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷum tañcāvūr nūl niliyattil 12000 nūlkaḷum irukkap pūlar (Buhler) kaṇṭār. 1186-kkum 1400-kku miṭiyil intiyāvil periya aḻivu vēli naṭi peṟṟatu. ak kālattil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa nūl niliyaṅkaḷ aḻintu pōyiṉa. avikaḷiliruntu maṟintupōṉa nūlkaḷ ilaṭcak kaṇakkil ākalām. cīṉar acciṭum vakiyi ki.mu.202il kaṇṭupiṭittārkaḷ. airōppāviṉ yōvaṉ kūtaṉpark (Johan Gotenberg) eṉpavaṉ accup poṟiyik kaṇṭupiṭittāṉ. curuḷ vakiyil mutal acciṭappaṭṭa puttakam ki.pi. 868il veḷiyāyiṟṟu. itu kūtaṉpark pipiḷi acciṭu vataṟku aṟunūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muṟpaṭṭa nikaḻcci. 
intiyāvukku acciyantiram 1566ām āṇṭu cepṭampar mātam pōrttukkēyarāl mutaṉ mutal koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu. atu irākkōl (Rockol) eṉṉumiṭattiluḷḷa cēyiṉpōl kalā cāliyil amikkap paṭṭatu. civāci makārācā ōr accup poṟiyi amittār. atil vēli ceyvikka muṭiyāmal iruntamiyāl avar ati 1674il viṟṟār.
1712il ṭāṉiya pātirimār airōppaviliruntu oru accup poṟiyik koṇṭu vantu taraṅkampāṭiyil nāṭṭiṉārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pōrttuk kēya moḻiyil pala nūlkaḷi veḷiyiṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kiṟittuva mata campantamāṉa tamiḻp puttakam oṉṟit tamiḻil veḷiyiṭ ṭārkaḷ. intiya moḻiyil acciṭappaṭṭa mutaṟputtakam ituvē. ivvaccu niliyam 1715il pipiḷiṉ putiya ēṟpāṭṭi veḷiyiṭṭatu.
ikkāla nūlakaṅkaḷ
nūlakaṅkaḷ kalvikaṟṟa vittuvāṉkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttuvataṟku māttiram uriyateṉa oru kālattil karutappaṭṭatu. ippoḻutu avi paṭittavark kum paṭikka virumpukiṉṟavarkkum uriya iṭamākak koḷḷappaṭukiṉṟatu. 
ilaṇṭaṉ mānakariluḷḷa nūlakam ēṉi nūlakaṅkaḷi viṭac ciṟappu vāyntatu. aṅku 20,00,000 acciṭṭa nūlkaḷum 56,000 kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. maṟṟiya nūlakaṅkaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭuvaṉa pōṉṟa ciṟiya nūlkaḷ ik kaṇakkil aṭaṅkuvaṉavalla. avikaḷiyum cērppiṉ nūlkaḷiṉ eṇ 50,00,000 variyilākum. iṅkilāntil acciṭappaṭum nūlkaḷil 3,400 paṭikaḷ āṇṭutōṟum cērkkappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa.
kiyeḻuttup paṭikaḷ kiṟittuvukku muṉ mūṉṟām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal iṉṟu variyum uḷḷavi. ivikaḷuḷ pipiras tāḷil eḻutappaṭṭa kirēkka nūlkaḷ ciṟappuṭiyaṉa. ilaṇṭaṉ poruṭkāṭcic cāliyiluḷḷa nūlakattil ceṉṟu nūlkaḷip paṭippataṟku oruvaṉ 21 vayatukku mēṟpaṭṭavaṉāyiruttal vēṇṭum; avaṉiṭattil aṉumatic cīṭṭum iruttal vēṇṭum.
kalkattāviluḷḷa camakkiruta kalācāliyil 1652 camakkirutak kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷum 2769 camakkiruta accu nūlkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. aṅku ciṉa matak koḷkikaḷ toṭarpāṉa kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷum uḷḷaṉa. 1781il toṭaṅkappaṭṭa arāpiya nūl niliyattil 731 acciṭṭa nūlkaḷum, 143 (mūlam) kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷum 151 paṭi eḻutiya nūlkaḷum uḷḷaṉa.
tippuc cultāṉiṉ nūlakattil 2000 kiyeḻuttu nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa.
amerikkāvil pala nūl niliyaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. 1000 periya nūl kaḷukku mēluḷḷa nūlniliyaṅkaḷ 5,383 variyil 1900il amerikkā viliruntaṉa. 1910il 10000 variyiliruntaṉa.
kāṅkiras nūl niliyam 1800il vāṣiṅṭaṉil ārampikkappaṭ ṭatu. iti 1814il āṅkila pōrvīrar tī mūṭṭi erittārkaḷ. piṉpu 1851il putiya nūlaka moṉṟu toṭakkappaṭṭatu. aṅku 20000 nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa. avi tīkku iriyāyiṉa. ataṉ piṉ tokukkappaṭṭavi 26,00,000 nūlkaḷ. 
iṉṟuḷḷa nūl niliyak kaṭṭaṭam 1897il amikkappaṭṭatu. ataṉ parappu mūṉṟari ēkkar. atil nāṟpati laṭcam nūlkaḷi vikka lām. atiṉ vili nilam uṭpaṭa 70,00,000 ṭālar. ituvē ulakam muḻumiyilum uḷḷa nūlakak kaṭṭaṭaṅkaḷiṟ periyatu.
kaṉṉimarā nūlakam
kaṉṉimarā nūlakam ceṉṉiyil eḻumpūril nūtaṉa poruṭkāṭcic cālik kaṭṭiṭattiluḷḷatu. iṅku uttēcamāka oru ilaṭcam nūlkaḷ variyil uḷḷaṉa. iṅku palatuṟi nūlkaḷ uṇṭu. ākavē māṇavarum, āciriyarum, ārāycciyāḷarum in nūlakattip payaṉpaṭuttuvar. in nūlakam potumakkaḷukkut tiṟantu viṭappaṭṭirukkiṉṟatu. nāṉūṟu mutal ainnūṟu pēr tiṉam in nūlakattukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar. iṅku nūlkaḷē yaṉṟip pala nāḷ. tiṅkaḷ, vāra veḷiyīṭukaḷum varavaḻikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paḻiya paṭikaḷ puttaka vaṭivil kaṭṭi vikkappaṭṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. in nūlakat tukku aṅkattavarāvataṟku irupatu rūpā kaṭṭaṇam celutta vēṇṭum. aṅkattiṉiṉṟu vilaka virumpiṉāl ap paṇattit tirumpap peṟṟuk koḷḷalām. aṅkattavarkaḷ nūlkaḷi iraval peṟṟuc cellalām. tiruvallikkēṇiyil palkali kaḻaka nūl niliyamoṉṟuḷḷatu. iṅkum kaṉṉimarā nūl niliyattiluḷḷa nūlkaḷ aḷavu uṇṭu. itu potumakka ḷukkut tiṟantuviṭappaṭavilli. paṭṭatārikaḷ kalācāli māṇavarkaḷē in nūlakattiṟ payaṉkoḷvar. iṉṉōr nūlakam aṭiyār tiyāca pisiṭ caṅkattiṉar kaṭṭaṭattiluḷḷatu. iṅkum ariya pala nūlkaḷ uḷḷaṉa.
ayal nāṭṭup paḻiya nūlakaṅkaḷ 
ekiptu
ekiptilē eṭiv eṉṉum iṭattil nūlakam oṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭatu. an niliyattiṉ aṟikaḷ oṉṟil nūl niliyattiluḷḷa puttakaṅkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ niriyāka eḻutappaṭṭa pipiras curuḷ oṉṟu kāṇappaṭṭatu. in nūlakam ki.mu.4000 variyil amikkappaṭṭatu. ekiptilē varalāṟu eḻuti vikkum vaḻakkup paḻamiyuṭiyatu. parōvā eṉṉum ekiptiya araca paramparikku muṟpaṭṭa aracariṉ varalāṟukaḷ toṭarpāka eḻutik kāppāṟṟappaṭalāyiṉa. aracariṉ varalāṟukaḷi eḻutum pulavaṉ paṭi eṭuppuk kālaṅkaḷil aracarōṭu pōrkkaḷañ ceṉṟu veṟṟikaḷiṉ taṉmikaḷi eḻutiṉāṉ. ki.mu.2500 variyil varalāṟu eḻuvatu ciṟanta kaliyākak karutappaṭṭatu. ki.mu. 2500 mutal ekiptiya pulavarkaḷ taṅkaḷ aracari varicippaṭutti eḻuti avarkaḷirunta kālattiyum kuṟippiṭṭārkaḷ. nikaḻccikaḷ iṉṉa aracaṉ kālattil iṉṉa āṇṭu eṉakkuṟippiṭappaṭṭaṉa.
cumēriyā
ekiptiya nūlakattukku aṭutta paṭiyil mutalām cārkaṉ ak kāṭṭil niṟuviyirunta nūlakam paḻamiyuṭiyatu. ki.mu.2700il cumēriyāvil periya nūl niliyaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiyiruntaṉa. tellō eṉṉum nakaril oṉṟiṉmēl oṉṟāka oḻuṅkupaṭutti aṭukkappaṭṭa 30,000 kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. ki.mu.2000 variyil cumēriya varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ kaḻintakāla varalāṟukaḷi eḻuti vittārkaḷ. iv varalāṟṟil pāpilōṉiya araca parampariyi viḷakkum pakutikaḷ vantuḷḷaṉa.
pāpilōṉiyā
pāpilōṉiyāvil tellō, ilākāṣ, nippūr mutaliya iṭaṅ kaḷil nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. nūlkaḷ cāṭikaḷil oḻuṅkāka iṭṭut taṭṭukaḷil vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. an nūlakaṅkaḷ aḻintu pōyiṉa. pōrcippā eṉṉum iṭattilirunta nūl niliyam mikap periyatu.
acīriyā
acurpāṉippāl (ki.mu.673) eṉṉum acīriya aracaṉ periyanūl niliyamoṉṟi niṟuviṉāṉ. an niliyattilirunta nūlkaḷ pōrcippā, kūtā, akkāṭ, ūr, erek, ilārcā, nippūr mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷi lirunta nūlkaḷip pārttup paṭi eṭukkappaṭṭavi. acurpāṉippāliṉ araṇmaṉiyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa 30,000 kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ pāpilōṉiya makkaḷiṉ vāḻkki muṟiyip paṟṟi aṟivataṟku ātāramāyuḷḷaṉa. teṉ ciriyāvil kaḷimaṇ ēṭukaḷ aṭaṅkiya nūlniliyamoṉṟu kaṇṭu piṭikkappaṭṭatu. itaṉ kālam ki.mu.1300 variyil.
alakcāntiriyā nūlniliyam
ekiptuviliruntu pipiras tāḷkaḷ kiṭippataṉ muṉ kirīcil puttakaṅkaḷ mikak kuṟivāyiruntaṉa. ki.mu.7ām nūṟṟāṇṭu mutal pipiras tāḷkaḷ kirīcukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. ataṟku muṉ nūlkaḷ tōl kaḷil allatu marac caṭṭaṅkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭaṉa. ak kālattil pipiras eṉṉum nāṇal nil āṟṟaṅkariyi aṭutta catuppu nilaṅkaḷil maṇṭi vaḷarntatu. kiṟittuvukku mūvāyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ nāṇal taṇṭukaḷip piḷantu nīḷamākac cērttu oṭṭit tāḷ ceyyappaṭṭatu. at tāḷkaḷ curuṭṭi vikkappaṭṭaṉa. kirēkkar pipiras tāḷkaḷip paṟṟi aṟivataṟku nīṇṭa kālattukku muṉ toṭṭu ekiptil avi payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭaṉa.
ki.mu.ēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭil pipiras tāḷkaḷ curuḷ vaṭivil kiricukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. itu avarkaḷ muṉ eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttiya poruḷkaḷiviṭa vāyppuṭiyatāyiruntamiyāl puttakaṅkaḷ atikappaṭṭaṉa. ki.mu. aintām nūṟṟāṇṭil hōmariṉ nūlkaḷum avar kālattiṟkup piṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷum pipiras tāḷkaḷil eḻutappaṭ ṭiruntaṉa. avi cantikaḷil viṟkappaṭṭaṉa. ki.mu.407il eḻutāta oru pipiras curuḷiṉ vili mūṉṟu ṣiliṅ variyiliruntatu. appoḻutu makkaḷ nūl niliyaṅkaḷi vittukkoḷḷa virumpiṉārkaḷ. yūripiṭis (Euripides) eṉpavaṉ mikap pala pipiras curuḷ vaṭivāṉa nūlkaḷi vittiruntāṉ. piḷāṭṭō (lato) nālām nūṟṟāṇṭil tattuva cāttirak kalācāliyi niṟuviyapōtu aṅku oru nūlniliyattiyum amarttiṉār. itaṉilum periya nūl niliyam arisṭōṭṭiliṉ paḷḷik kūṭattil amikkappaṭṭiruntatu.
alakcāntariṉ maraṇattiṉ piṉ (ki.mu.323) avaruṭiya taḷapatikaḷi loruvaṉāṉa tālami ekiptukku aracaṉāṉāṉ. it tālamikkup piṉ vanta aracarum tālami eṉṉum peyarāl aṟiyappaṭṭaṉar. pipiras tāḷkaḷic ceyyum toḻiṟcālikaḷ aracāṅkattiṭam iruntaṉa. ākavē, kirēkkar ekiptiya aracāṅkattiliruntu tamakku vēṇṭiya pipiras tāḷkaḷip peṟa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. akkālattil tālamiyē mikac celvamuṭiyavaṉāyiruntāṉ. avaṉ mattiya tarikkaṭali aṭutta nāṭukaḷiṟ kiṭikkak kūṭiya nūlkaḷi ellām vili koṭuttu vāṅkiṉāṉ. pipiras tāḷkaḷ atikam iruntamiyāl avaṉ paḻiya puttakaṅkaḷip paṭi eṭuttāṉ; putiya puttakaṅkaḷi makkaḷiṭiyē paravac ceytāṉ. alakcāntiriyāvil periya nūlakamoṉṟu niṟuvutal vēṇṭumeṉṉum viruppam avaṉukku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. eṇṇiyāvāṟē avaṉ aṅku mikapperiya nūlakamoṉṟi niṟuviṉāṉ. kirēkka aracarkaḷ in nūlakattukkup pōṭṭiyākat tāmum oṉṟi amikka muyaṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ ekiptiyariṉ tāḷ ceyyum taṉiyurimiyic citikka virumpi oruvaki melliya tōlit tāḷākap payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. āyiṉum kirēkka urōmaṉ ulakattil kiṟittuva kālam variyil pipiras puttakaṅkaḷ eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. alakcāntiriyāvil toṭaṅkappaṭṭa nūl niliyam miyūciyam eṉṉum kōyilōṭu iṇikkappaṭṭiruntatu. miyūciyam eṉṉum col kirēkkil mōce aṉ (moursion) eṉappaṭṭatu. itaṟkup pāṭaṟ peṇ kaṭavuḷiṉ kōyilēṉṟu poruḷ. kirēkkarāl naṭattappaṭṭa ellāc caṅkaṅkaḷiṉ mattiyilum oru teyvam allatu pala teyvaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. pitakōriya viṭutikaḷil ak kūṭṭattukkuriya teyvaṅkaḷiruntaṉa. ateṉcilirunta arisṭōṭṭiliṉ paḷḷik kūṭattil teyvaṅkaḷukkuk kōyiliruntatu. tālami kirēkkak kalvi, viññāṉam campantamāṉa kalvik kaḻakatti amittapōtu avaṉ ataṉik kōyilōṭu toṭarpupaṭuttuvatu iyalpēyākum. avaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉiyil iruntavarkaḷuḷ atikāramuṭiya oruvaṉ kirēkki ṉiṉṟum tappi ōṭicceṉṟavaṉum arisṭōṭṭiliṉ māṇavaṉumākiya ṭemeṟṟiyas eṉpavaṉ.
tālamiyāl toṭakkappaṭṭa vēli avaṉatu makaṉ tālami pilāṭelpas(Ptolemy Philadephus ki.mu.285-247) kālattil muṟṟup peṟṟatu. ivaṉukku vilaṅkunūl, ilakkiyam eṉpavikaḷil mikka viruppam iruntatu. nūlakatti mēṟ pārppavaṉ kurumāruḷ talivaṉāyiruntāṉ. kirēkka nāṭṭiluḷḷa tattuva cāttirikaḷ ellōrum ik kalvik kaḻakattiṉ uṟuppiṉarāyiṉar. pilāṭalpas maraṇamāṉapōtu nūlakattil 4,00,000 kalanta curuḷkaḷum, 90,000 kalappillāta curuḷkaḷumiruntaṉa. ‘kalanta’ eṉpavikaḷil oṉṟukku atikamāṉa nūlkaḷ variyappaṭṭirun taṉa. ki.mu.50il, nūlakattilirunta nūlkaḷiṉ eṇ 7,00,000. alakcāntiriyā vil iṉṉoru ciṟiya nūlakamumiruntatu. atil 42,800 curuḷkaḷ iruntaṉa.
alakcāntiriyāvilirunta nūlkaḷukku eṉṉa viḷintatu eṉṟu teriyavilli. ki.mu.47il cīcar ekiptiya kappalkaḷukkut tī vittapōtu an nūlakam erintu pōyiṟṟu eṉṟu nampappaṭukiṉṟatu. ciṟiya nūlakam kiṟittuva matattiṉarāl ki.pi.391il aḻikkappaṭṭatu. ki.pi.642il amaru eṉṉum mukammatiya taḷapati alakcāntiriyā nūlniliyattukkut tīyiṭṭāṉ.
urōm
urōmil mutal nūlakam ki.mu.168il niṟuvappaṭṭatu. atu mace ṭōṉiyā aracaruṭiya nūl niliyattiliruntu veṟṟip poruḷākak koṇṭu varappaṭṭa nūlkaḷik koṇṭu amikkappaṭṭatu. ākastas eṉpār palaṉataṉ aṭṭōviyaṉ eṉṉum peyaruṭiya iraṇṭu nūl niliyaṅkaḷi amittār. kāṉstānṭiṉ picāntiyattil oru nūl niliyatti amittār.
urōmil taṉippaṭṭavarkaḷ tamatu māḷikikaḷil nūl nili yaṅkaḷ vittiruntaṉar. cilariṭattil āyirak kaṇakkāṉa nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa. iḷiyapiḷiṉi eṉpār eḻutiya nūlkaḷil iru nūṟṟukku mēṟpaṭṭa nūlkaḷ mēṟkōḷākak kāṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avikaḷuḷ oṉṟēṉum iṉṟu kāṇappaṭavilli.
cīṉā
cīṉāvil aracāṅka nūl niliyaṅkaḷum piṟa nūl niliyaṅkaḷu miruntaṉa. cīṉac cakkaravartti oruvaṉ varalāṟṟāciriyarkaḷ eḻutiya nūl kaḷi ellām tīyiliṭṭuk koḷuttumpaṭi kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ. cāttira campantamāṉa cila nūlkaḷ erikkāmal taṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. taṭi ceyyappaṭṭa nūlkaḷ aracāṅka nūlakattil vikkappaṭṭaṉa. araciṉar aṉumati peṟṟu māṇavar avaṟṟip paṭikkakkūṭiyatākaviruntatu. akkāla nūlkaḷ mūṅkiṟ caṭṭaṅkaḷil eḻutappaṭṭavi. ākavē, avi mikavum pāramuṭi yaṉa. puttakaṅkaḷi erikkātu kāppāṟṟat tuṇintavarkaḷ pala tolli kaḷukkuḷḷāṉārkaḷ. pala nūlkaḷ kippaṟṟappaṭṭaṉa. 400 pēr maraṇa taṇṭaṉi aṭintaṉar. kaṉpiyūciyas eḻutiya nūlkaḷic cilar maṉap pāṭañ ceytiruntārkaḷ. maṟṟavarkaḷ kēṭṭu maṉappāṭañceyyumpaṭi avarkaḷ avikaḷic coṉṉārkaḷ. avvaracaṉ iṟantapiṉ nūlkaḷ maṟupaṭiyum paravalāyiṉa; āṉāl, avikaḷil pala tavaṟukaḷ nuḻintaṉa.
cīṉariṉ aracāṅka nūl niliyattil 3,123 ilakkiyaṅkaḷ, 2,705 tattuva cāttiraṅkaḷ, 1,318 pāṭal nūlkaḷ, 2,668 kaṇita nūlkaḷ, 869 maruntu nūlkaḷ 790 pōr nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa. 18ām nūṟṟāṇṭil pulavar pāṭal kaḷit tokukkumpaṭi mañcu aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ. 2,300 pulavarkaḷ pāṭiya 48,900 pāṭalkaḷ aṭaṅkiya muppatu nūlkaḷ tokukkappaṭṭaṉa. appoḻutu nūlakattil 54,000 nūlkaḷ iruntaṉa.
pākṭāṭ
paḷḷivācalkaḷ mucilim makkaḷiṉ kalik kaḻakaṅkaḷākaviruntaṉa. aṅku naṭakkum virivurikaḷik kēṭka ellā iṭaṅkaḷiliruntum māṇākkar vantu kūṭiṉar. camayam, tattuvam, ñāṉam, maruntu, kaṇitam pōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ paṟṟiya virivurikaḷ naṭattappaṭṭaṉa. arāpi moḻi vaḻaṅkum nāṭukaḷil āṅkāṅkirunta pērpōṉa āriciyarkaḷ tāmē muṉ vantu virivurikaḷi nikaḻttiṉārkaḷ. tiṟamiyuṭiya āciriyarkaḷukku virivuriyik kēṭkum makkaḷ ciṟiya ūtiyamaḷittārkaḷ. arāpi moḻiyiliruntu ilattiṉil moḻipeyarkkappaṭṭa kaṇitam viññāṉam, vāṉa cāttiram, maruntu, kirēkka tattuva cāttiram mutaliyavikaḷiyē mattiya kāla airōppā aṟintiruntatu. arapu moḻiyiliruntu ivvaki yil vanta nūlkaḷ patiṉāṟām nūṟṟāṇṭaḷavil paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷil payilappaṭṭaṉa. cāttira campantamāṉa kirēkka nūlkaḷ perumpālum arapumoḻiyil tiruppappaṭṭiruntaṉa. pākṭāṭṭil pala nūl niliyaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. maṉum (Manum) eṉṉum kalipā pākṭāṭṭil amitta viññāṉa maṇṭapattil periya nūlakamum vāṉa ārāycci ceyyum uyarnta kaṭṭaṭamum (observatory) iruntaṉa.
muṟkāla nūl niliyaṅkaḷiṉ ilakku
muṟkālattil nūlkaḷ aritiṟ kiṭippaṉavāyiruntamiyāl avi iṟantu paṭātu nili peṟutaṟ poruṭṭu ōriṭattiṟ tokuttu vikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. an nūlkaḷ tokuttu vikkappaṭṭirunta niliyaṅkaḷ ikkāla vācakacālikaḷ pōṉṟu payaṉaḷittaṉa eṉṟu kūṟutal iyalātu. niliyaṅkaḷil perumpālum paḻiya nūlkaḷē iruntaṉa. avikaḷiliruntu nūlkaḷ iraval koṭukkappaṭavilli. ikkāla nūl niliyaṅkaḷiṉ nōkkam muṟṟilum vēṟākavuḷḷatu. potumakkaḷ nūlkaḷip payiṉṟu aṟivu vaḷarcciyuṟa vēṇṭumeṉpatē ikkāla nūlkaḷiṉ nōkkamākum.
namatu nāṭṭiṉ kalvi nili
namatu intiya nāṭṭil nāṟpatu kōṭi makkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉar. ivar kaḷil nūṟṟukkup patiṉmūṉṟu pērē kalviyaṟivuṭiyavarkaḷāyiruk kiṉṟaṉar. ēṉiya 87 pērum eḻuta vācikkat teriyātavarkaḷāyirukkiṉ ṟaṉar. mēṟkut tēcaṅkaḷil nūṟṟukku mūṉṟu pērē kalviyaṟivillātavar kaḷāyirukkiṉṟaṉar. paṭittavarkaḷukkum paṭiyātavarkaḷukkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu maṉitarukkum vilaṅkukaḷukkumuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu pōlvatu eṉat tiruvaḷḷuvar kūṟiyuḷḷār.
“vilaṅkoṭu makka ḷaṉiyar ilaṅkunūl
kaṟṟārō ṭēṉi yavar”
kalvi yaṟivillāmiyiṉālēyē makkaḷ namatu nāṭṭil mākka ḷākavum piṟa nāṭukaḷil kalviyaṟivuḷḷamiyāl makkaḷākavum vāḻ kiṉṟaṉar.
kalviyip paravac ceyvataṟkēṟṟa vaḻivakikaḷ
1“nakaraṅkaḷilum kirāmaṅkaḷilum aṅkaṅkēyuḷḷa makkaḷiṉ takutikkum aḷaviṟkum ēṟṟavāṟu palatiṟappaṭṭa nūlkaḷit tokuttu vittu vēṇṭiyōrkku vēṇṭuñ camayattu eḷitiṉutavi yaṟivip paravac ceyvatē nūl niliyap piracārattiṉ mukkiya nōkkam. aṟivu peruki vaḷartaṟku nūlkaḷik kāṭṭilum ciṟanta karuvi illi eṉṟē kūṟalām. āciriyarkaḷ pāṟ kaṟpatu oru ciṟitaḷavākavē eppōtum iruttal kūṭum... kalviyeṉappaṭuvatu kaṟkum māṇavaṉ nūlkaḷit tāṉē cintittu ārāyntu tuṇivataṉ payaṉākavē amiyumeṉpatu yāvarkkum oppa muṭintatām. ākavē,piṟarutavi vēṇṭātu ōtiyuṇar vataṟkuriya nūlkaḷ makkaḷiṭiyē paravap parava, kalviyum paravi añ ñāṉamum akaṉṟoḻiyu meṉpatu kūṟavēṇṭā”.
“potumakkaḷaṉivaratu uḷḷattiyum kavarumāṟu nakaram, ūr, ciṟṟūr mutaliya palaviṭaṅkaḷilum nūl niliyaṅkaḷ pala amikkappeṟutal vēṇṭum. makkaḷiṉ viruppattiyum cukariyattiyum takutiyiyum nōkki avarkaḷukku ēṟṟa puttakaṅkaḷik koṭukkavēṇṭum. avar kaḷukku nūlkaḷil kavarcci uṇṭātaṟ poruṭṭu avaṟṟiṉ nayaṅkaḷi vivaraṇap pattirikikaḷ mūlam veḷiyiṭutal vēṇṭum. parantupaṭṭa aṟiviṉ palvēṟu pakutikaḷip paṟṟip putupputu nūlkaḷ veḷivara veḷivara avaṟṟiyellām potumakkaḷiṭiyē parakka vitittal vēṇṭum. kalviyaṟivu iṉṟiyamiyātatākumeṉpati vaṟpuṟuttik kūṟi, ataṉiyaṭitaṟkuc cātaṉamākuṅ karuvi nūlkaḷik kuṟittu upan niyācaṅkaḷ ceytu varal vēṇṭum. curuṅkakkūṟiṉ araciyaliṉ neṟip paṭṭa oru nāṭṭiṟ palatiṟattāṉum uttama vāḻvu vāḻtaṟku vēṇṭum. kalviyaṟi vaṉittiyum nūlkaḷ mūlam utavi vara ellā vakiyāṉum muyaṟci ceyvatē nūl niliyaṅkaḷ mēṟkoḷḷutaṟkuriya taṉipperuṅ kaṭamiyā yuḷḷatu.”
“innūl niliyaṅkaḷ palatiṟattaṉavāyiruttalē takkateṉpatu veḷippaṭi. kuḻanti mutal mutiyōr variyilumuḷḷa aṉivarkkum kalviyūṭṭutaṟkeṉa nūl niliyaṅkaḷ āyirakkaṇakkāka ēṟpaṭuttap peṟutal mika avaciyam. ivikaḷēyaṉṟik kalvit tuṟikaḷ palavaṟṟiṟkut taṉittaṉiyēyuriya nūl niliyaṅkaḷum mikap palavāka niṟuvappeṟutal iṉṟiyamiyātatu. mēlum, paṟpala kalvit tuṟikaḷilum ārāyccik keṉat taṉit taṉiyāyaminta nūl niliyaṅkaḷ pala tāpikkap peṟutalum takkatēyākum. piṟkūṟiya iraṇṭu vaki nūl niliyaṅkaḷum carvakalā caṅkaṅkaḷālum kalācālikaḷālum niṟuvap peṟutaṟkuriyaṉaveṉpatu kūṟāmalē amiyum.”
“mēlē vivarittaṉa pōṉṟa nūl niliyaṅkaḷ nāṭeṅkaṇum niramputal vēṇṭum. namatu nāṭṭilē nīṭittuk kuṭikoṇṭirukkum aṟiyāmiyik kāltarikka voṭṭātu veṭṭutal vēṇṭum. aṟivuc cuṭariṉ pēroḷi namatu nāṭeṅkum parantu vīcutal vēṇṭum. aṟiviṉ mūlamāy oṟṟumi mikutal vēṇṭum. aṟivu ciṟantu oṟṟumi mikku namma varkaḷ nalliṉpa neṟiyil niṟṟal vēṇṭum. ittakiya nōkkaṅ koṇṭē nūl niliyap piracāram nikaḻntu varukiṉṟatu.”
oru kuṟi
namatu moḻiyil cila ilakkaṇa ilakkiyaṅkaḷum camaya nūlkaḷumē yuḷḷaṉa. in nūlkaḷ ilakkiya muṟiyil mika mikac ciṟappuṭiyaṉa veṉpatu uṇmiyē. ik karuttiṉip pāratiyārum,
“yāmaṟinta pulavarilē kampaṉip pōl
vaḷḷuvarpōl iḷaṅkō vip pōl
pūmitaṉil yāṅkaṇumē piṟantatilli
uṇmi veṟum pukaḻcci yilli
ūmiyarāyc ceviṭarkaḷāy kuruṭarkaḷāy
vāḻkiṉṟōm orucoṟ kēḷīr
cēmamuṟa vēṇṭumeṉil teruvellām
tamiḻmuḻakkañ ceḻikkac ceyvīr”
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉāl naṉku uṇarttiyuḷḷār.
mēlnāṭṭu moḻikaḷil paṟpala aṟivuttuṟikaḷil cevviya nūlkaḷ veḷi vantuḷḷaṉa. ivvāṟu piṟa moḻikaḷil veḷivarum ariya nūlkaḷ perumpālum āṅkilattil moḻi peyarkkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ataṉāl āṅkila moḻi aṟivu nūlkaḷiṉ kaḷañciyamāka viḷaṅkukiṉ ṟatu. makkaḷ aṟivut tuṟiyil payilavēṇṭiya nūlkaḷ āṅkila moḻiyil iruppatāl āṅkilam payilum kaṭṭāyam uṇṭākiṉṟatu. āṅkila naṉ makkaḷ tam moḻiyil evvāṟu aṟivu nūlkaḷip perukkik koṇ ṭārkaḷō avvāṟē nāmum piṟamoḻikaḷiluḷḷa aṟivu nūṟporuḷ kaḷit tamiḻil eḻuti vittal vēṇṭum.
“piṟanāṭṭu nallaṟiñar cāttiraṅkaḷ1
tamiḻ moḻiyiṟ peyarttal vēṇṭum
iṟavāta pukaḻuṭiya putunūlkaḷ
tamiḻmoḻiyil iyaṟṟal vēṇṭum
maṟivāka namakkuḷḷē paḻaṅkatikaḷ
colvatilōr makimi illi
tiṟamāṉa pulamiyeṉil veḷināṭṭōr
ati vaṇaṅkac ceytal vēṇṭum.” - pāratiyār
“tamiḻarkkut tamiḻmoḻiyiṟ cuvaṭiccāli
carvakalā cāliyip pōl eṅkum vēṇṭum
tamiḻililāp piṟamoḻinūl aṉittum nalla
tamiḻākki vācikkat tarutal vēṇṭum
amutampōl centamiḻiṟ kaviti nūlkaḷ
aḻakiyavām urinaṭiyil aminta nūlkaḷ
cumicumiyāyc cēkarittup palkalicēr
tuṟituṟiyāyp pirittaṭukki vittal vēṇṭum.”
- pāratitācaṉ

aṟivukkaṭṭurikaḷ
muṉṉuri
innūliṟ kāṇappaṭum kaṭṭurikaḷ paṟpala āṅkila nūlkaḷilum tiṅkaḷ veḷiyīṭukaḷilum kāṇappaṭṭa karuttukkaḷit tiraṭṭit tamiḻi leḻutappaṭṭavi. ivi pal poruḷaṉavākavum poruṭ ceṟivuṭiyaṉa vākavum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟamiyiṉ, kaṭṭuri varivōrukkum, pāṭamākap payilvōrukkum perum payaṉpaṭat takkaṉaveṉpatu ematu karuttu.
na.ci. kantiyā
1. pēcum paṭattiṉ kati
palar pēcum paṭakkāṭciyip pārttu makiḻkiṉṟaṉar. atu iciyum kūttum atikamuḷḷatāka allatu tūratēcak kāṭcikaḷik kāṭṭuvatāka allatu tiṭukkiṭum nikaḻccikaḷuṭiyatākavirukkalām. nām cila maṇi nēratti makiḻcciyākak kaḻippataṟkup pēcumpaṭak kāṭci ēṟṟatu.
nām ciṟitu nēram pārttu makiḻkiṉṟa ip paṭattip piṭittu muṭippa taṟku evvaḷavu pirayāci eṭukkappaṭṭatu eṉṟu aṟivatu namakku viyappi aḷikkum. oṉṟari maṇinēram ōṭikkoṇṭirunta paṭatti ēṟakkuṟiya irupattunāṉku naṭikarkaḷum, mēṟpārppavarkaḷ cilarum, paṉṉiraṇṭukku mēṟpaṭṭa paṭampiṭippavarkaḷum, maṟṟum vēliyāḷarum patiṉeṭṭu mātaṅkaḷil allatu iraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷil piṭittu muṭittiruppārkaḷ.
pēcumpaṭam piṭippataṟku mutalil vēṇṭiyatu oru kati. atu pēr peṟṟa oru kaṟpaṉik kati allatu pēcumpaṭa naṭippukku eṉṟu putitāka eḻutappaṭṭa oru katiyākavirukkalām. paṭam piṭippataṉ muṉ evvakik kāṭcikaḷ amikkappaṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu tīrmāṉikkappaṭavēṇṭum; iṉṉiṉṉa pakutiyi iṉṉavar naṭippatu eṉṟum, avarkaḷ pēca vēṇṭi yaṉa naṭikka vēṇṭiyaṉa evi evi eṉṟum eḻuti vikkappaṭutal vēṇṭum. 
pēcumpaṭam piṭippatil mukkiyamuṭiyavar paṭamutalāḷi. avar naṭikkiṉṟavarkaḷic campaḷattukku amarttukiṉṟavarāvār. paṭattukku vēṇṭiya kati āyattamāṉavuṭaṉ evvaḷavu paṭappiṭippu niliyattil (Studio) piṭippatu eṉṟum evvaḷavu veḷiyiṟ piṭippatu eṉṟum tīrmāṉa mākavēṇṭum. ivaṟṟuṭ cila kāṭcikaḷ imayamaliyilum, cila cakārā vaṉān tarattilum, cila kirīṉlāntil uṟipaṉi mūṭiya veḷikaḷilumāka virukka lām. ākavē paṭamutalāḷi iyaṟkiyāṉa ik kāṭcikaḷ allatu iyaṟki pōlat tōṉṟum cila kāṭcikaḷi āyattañ ceytal vēṇṭum.
ilaṇṭaṉ allatu holivūṭṭiliruntu atika campaḷam peṟum naṭikarkaḷic cakārā, kirīṉlāntu mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuk koṇṭuceṉṟu paṭampiṭittal mikap paṇac celavuṭiyatākum. vēliyiṉṟip payaṇañ ceyyum naṭikarkaḷukkup pala vāraṅkaḷukkuc campaḷaṅ koṭukka vēṇṭi nērum. ivvāṟu celavu ceytu piṭikkappaṭum paṭaṅkaḷ tiriyil cila nimiṭaṅkaḷ ōṭuvaṉavē. ākavē paṭamutalāḷi ittollikaḷi ōr upāyattāl vellukiṟāṉ. paṭamutalāḷi, naṭikarkaḷic cakārā, kirīṉlāntu mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷukkup pōkkuvataṟkup patil paṭampiṭikkum cilari avviṭaṅkaḷukku aṉuppukiṟāṉ. naṭikarkaḷiṉ naṭippip paṭam piṭippataṟkuc cila mātaṅkaḷiṉ muṉ paṭam piṭippōr puṟappaṭṭuc celkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ aṅkuḷḷa malikaḷ vaṉāntaraṅkaḷ paṉiyuṟinta veḷikaḷ pōṉṟu akkāṭciyil varavēṇṭiyavaṟṟip paṭam piṭik kiṟārkaḷ. avaṟṟip paṭam piṭittukkoṇṭu avarkaḷ tamatu iṭattukkut tirumpukiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ paṭaṅkaḷ caripārkkappaṭṭa piṉ ak kāṭcikaḷukkuriya naṭippu varumvariyil avi otukkivikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
paṭam piṭikkum niliyattil periya kaṇṇāṭit tiri uṇṭu. ik kāṭcikaḷil oṉṟu tōṉṟa vēṇṭiya pakutivarumpōtu ivaṟṟiloṉṟu kaṇṇāṭit tirikkup piṉpakkattiluḷḷa kaṇṇāṭi viḷakku veḷiccam mūlam kaṇṇāṭit tiriyiṟ kāṭṭappaṭum. pēcum paṭam piṭikkum “kāmirā” muṉpuṟattil irukkum. naṭikar kaṇṇāṭit tirikku etirē niṉṟu naṭippar. kaṇṇāṭittirikkup piṉṉālirukkum kaṇṇāṭi viḷakku mali, vaṉāntaram ,uṟipaṉi,veḷi mutaliya kāṭcikaḷik kaṇṇāṭittiriyil kāṭṭum. muṉṉāliruntu naṭippip patiyavikkum kāmirā kaṇṇāṭit tiriyil māṟi māṟit tōṉṟum kāṭcikaḷiyum, patiya vikkiṟatu. ivvāṟu naṭikar malippakkaṅkaḷ, kuḷirmikunta uṟipaṉi veḷikaḷ, vaṉāntaraṅ kaḷukkup pakkattil niṟpavarākak kāṭciyaḷikkumpaṭi ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉar. uṇmiyil avarkaḷ avviṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟavarkaḷallar.
nāṭakattip paṭampiṭippataṉ muṉ atika vēli ceyyavēṇṭi yavar aḻakuk kaliyi mēṟpārppavar. ivar paṭattiṉ aḻakukkali campantamāṉavaṟṟiṟ kavaṉañceluttuvar. iṭaṅkaḷiṉ kāṭcikaḷi ōḻuṅku paṭuttutal, palavaki alaṅkāraṅkaḷ, nāṟkāli mutaliya taḷavāṭaṅkaḷ , toṅkaviṭum alaṅkāraṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟukkup poṟuppāḷi ivarē. iv vēlikaḷip paṟṟi ivar naṉku aṟintiruttal vēṇṭum. vikṭōriyā irāṇi kālattip paṟṟiya naṭippil irumpu nāṟkālikaḷ iṭam peṟu māṉāl atu tavaṟākum.
uṭikaḷip paṟṟi mēṟpārppavarum mukkiyamuṭiyavarāvar. ivar perumpālum peṇṇākaviruppar. ivar periya mutaluṭiyavarum paṟpala iṭaṅkaḷukkuriya palakāla uṭikaḷip paṟṟi aṟintavarumāyirut tal vēṇṭum. teriyātavaṟṟi ivar aṟintukoḷḷa vēṇṭum. pala vaki naṭikarkaḷ aṇintukoḷḷum uṭikaḷukku ivarē poṟup pāḷiyāvār. periya paṭa niliyaṅkaḷil vakivakiyāṉa uṭikaḷit tippataṟkup pala vēliyāḷariruppar.
naṭippi mēṟpārppavar mutaṉmiyāṉavaruḷ oruvar. ivar camūkamāyirātu ekkāṭciyum paṭam piṭikkappaṭamāṭṭātu. ivar taṉatu maṉatil niṉippatu pōlavē naṭikar appakkam allatu ippakkam tirumpuvatu, aḻuvatu, cirippatu pōṉṟavaṟṟi naṭikkiṉṟaṉar. nalla naṭikar mēṟpārppavar colvatik kavaṉittu naṭippavarāvar.
paṭampiṭippatil campantappaṭāta iṉṉoruvar olippativu ceypavarāvar. ivaratu vēli paṭattil varum pāṭṭu, caṅkītam. pēccup pōṉṟavaṟṟi ellām paṭa ‘pil”mil patiyavippatu.
potuvākak kāṭcikaḷ paṭam piṭikkappaṭumpōtu olikaḷum patiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila camayaṅkaḷil ivvāṟuceyvatu kaṭiṉamākum. appoḻutu kāṭcikaḷ paṭam piṭikkappaṭṭa piṉ olikaḷ taṉiyē patiyappaṭṭu iraṇṭum oṉṟāka iṇikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pēcumpaṭam piṭikkum oru paṭaniliyattip pārttāl nām viyappaṭivōm. aṅku ellā iṭaṅkaḷum collamuṭiyāta oḻuṅkīṉa mākat tōṉṟum. periya paṭa niliyaṅkaḷil orē muṟiyil iraṇṭu allatu mūṉṟu vevvēṟu katikaḷ paṭam piṭikkappaṭum. oru iṭattil cāntiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa kōyil mukappum, iṉṉoru iṭattil cātāraṇa aṭuppaṅkarik kāṭciyum kāṇappaṭalām. paṭam piṭikkum niliyattil vēli ceyyum vēliyāḷar, paṭampiṭippōr,veḷiccam campantamāṉa karuvikaḷukkup poṟuppāyuḷḷōr, olippativu ceyyum oliperukki kaḷil vēli ceyvōr uḷḷē celvataṟku āyattamāyirukkum naṭikar mutaliyōr kāṇappaṭuvar. 
iṟutiyil aṅku viṟuviṟuppākap paṭam piṭikkappaṭum. aṅku paṭam piṭippōriṟ cilar kāmirākkaḷōṭum, cilar atika oḷi koṭukkum viḷakkukaḷōṭum, olippativōr mikirāpōṉ eṉṉum oliperukki kaḷōṭum, olipatiyum karuvikaḷōṭum niṟpar. olipativōr avar kaḷukkup pakkattē niṟpar. mēṟpārppavar (iṭirakṭar) taṉatu kiyil maṇik kūṭṭōṭu oru nāṟkāliyil iruppar.
oṉṟu, oruvaṉ taṉatu kātaliyic cantikkum kāṭciyākalām. iruvarum cantittu muttamiṭukiṟārkaḷ. mēṟpārppavar muttamiṭum nērattiṉ aḷavik kaṭikārattil pārkkiṟār. muttamiṭumvaki mēṟ pārppavariṉ viruppattukku ēṟṟatāyirāviṭil allatu oruvari oruvar cantikkumpōtu makiḻccikkuṟi tōṉṟāviṭil akkāṭci maṟupaṭiyum paṭam piṭikkappaṭum. iṉṉōru kāṭci oru peṇ taṉatu tāyōṭu vīṭṭu aṟi yiliruntu tēnīr parukikkoṇṭiruppatu ākalām. peṇ mika āṟutalāka uṇṭu koṇṭiruntāl allatu vayatu pōṉa tāy mika nākarika muṟi yākat tēnīr aruntiṉāl akkāṭci mēṟpārppavar tiruptippaṭum vaki yil tēnīr parukumvari tirumpat tirumpap paṭam piṭikkappaṭum.
nām paṭattil kāṇum kāṭcikaḷ variciyākap paṭam piṭikkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu niṉittal kūṭātu. oru nīṇṭa paṭaccuruḷil nūṟṟukkaṇak kāṉa tuṇṭup paṭaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. ivi taṉittaṉiyē piṭikkappaṭṭu iṟutiyil oṉṟu cērkkappaṭṭavi. ittuṇṭup paṭaṅkaḷ paṭapparicōtaka rāl pārviyiṭappaṭum. acciṭa vēṇṭiya pakutikaḷip pattiriki āciriyar oruvar pārviyiṭṭuk koṇṭiruppatu pōlap paṭap paricōta karum tiṉam eṭukkappaṭum paṭaṅkaḷik katiyōṭu ottup pārppār. tuṇṭuppaṭaṅkaḷ katiyōṭum, iṭaṅkaḷiṉ iyaṟkik kāṭcikaḷōṭum naṭippukkaḷōṭum ottiruntāl ēṟṟaṉa eṉṟu oppukkoḷḷappaṭum. paṭap paricōtakar oppuk koḷḷāta pakutikaḷ maṟupaṭiyum paṭam piṭippataṟku mēṟpārppavariṭam aṉuppappaṭum.
paṭam piṭikkum niliyaṅkaḷil naṭikariṉ paṭaṅkaḷ viyappāṉa nāṭu kaḷiṉ iyaṟkik kāṭcikaḷiṭiyē niṟpatāka av viyaṟkik kāṭci kaḷik kaṇṇāṭit tiriyil kāṭṭappaṭum paṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu eṭuk kappaṭukiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu muṉ kūṟiṉōm. ivvāṟu kaṇṇāṭit tiriyil kāṭṭappaṭum paṭaṅkaḷ “piṉpuṟak kāṭci” (kṣayuma cīṭitanuviṭin) eṉap paṭum. immuṟi paṭampiṭikkum kumpaṉikaḷukku mikap payaṉuṭiyatu. oru peṇṇum āṇum paṭakiliruntu āṟṟil vēkamākac cellum paṭa moṉṟu piṭikka vēṇṭiyirukkiṟateṉṟu vittukkoḷḷuvōm. iv vāṟu paṭam piṭikka vēṇṭiyiruntāl paṭam piṭikkum paṭakukku muṉṉāl iṉṉoru paṭaku vēkamākac cella vēṇṭiyirukkum. avvāṟu nikaḻiṉ naṭikaṉum naṭikiyum pēcum pēccukkaḷik kēṭka muṭiyātapaṭi iyantirat tiṉ iriccal maṟittuviṭum. “piṉpuṟak kāṭcip paṭam” eṉṉum muṟiyiṉāl ittolliyi vellalām. paṭam piṭippōr cilar paṭakil pōvar. pōkum pōtu avar nīriyum karikaḷiyum aṅku kēṭkum olikaḷiyum pativu ceytukoṇṭu celvar. veḷiyil ceyya vēṇṭiya vēli ivvaḷavē. maṟṟiya vēlikaḷ paṭam piṭikkum niliyattiṟ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉum muṉṉum āṭak kūṭiyatākac ceyyappaṭṭa nāṟkāliyil kaṇṇāṭit tirikku muṉṉāl vaḷḷam vik kappaṭukiṟatu. paṭakukku muṉṉāl naṭikaṉum naṭikiyum irukkiṟārkaḷ. āṟṟil piṭikkappaṭṭa paṭaṅkaḷ piṉpuṟattiliruntu tiriyil kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. muṉṉālirukkum paṭaku muṉṉum piṉṉum āṭukiṟatu. tammip piṉ toṭarntu varum iṉṉoru paṭakip pārppatupōl naṭiki tirumpip pārkkiṟāḷ. avarkaḷ oruvarōṭu oruvar pēcukiṉṟaṉar. avaḷ vēkamākap paṭaki ōṭṭumpaṭi avaṉiṭam collukiṟāḷ. ap poḻutu piṉṉālirukkum kaṇṇāṭit tiriyiluḷḷa paṭaṅkaḷ vēkamāka ōṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu piṭikkappaṭṭa paṭattip pārttāl uṇmi yākavē paṭaku āṟṟil celvatu pōlavē tōṉṟum.
pēcum paṭaṅkaḷi amippavar kiyāḷum upāyaṅkaḷ pala. iravil allatu iṉṉoru nāṭṭil pukivaṇṭi celvatākak kāṭcikaḷ kāṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi ceyaṟkip pukivaṇṭi oṉṟiyum cittarikkappaṭṭa iyaṟkik kāṭcikaḷiyum vittup paṭam piṭikkum niliyattil piṭikkappaṭṭaṉavākum.
katiyil varum oruvaṉ irumpuk kampiyāl taliyil aṭikkap paṭuvatiyō kaṇṇāṭip puṭṭiyiṉāl eṟintu viḻuttappaṭuvatiyō kaṇṭu nām kalakkamaṭiyavēṇṭiyatilli. irumpuk kampi eṉpatu kākitakkūḻiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭatu. eṟintavuṭaṉ uṭintupōkiṟa kaṇṇāṭippuṭṭi meḻukiṉāl ceyyappaṭṭatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil katiyil varum peṇ āṟṟil muḻukit tattaḷippatik kaṇṭum nām kuḻappa maṭiya vēṇṭiyatilli. āṟṟuk kāṭci kaṇṇāṭit tiriyil kāṭṭap paṭṭa paṭamākalām. peṇ niṟkum taṇṇīr paṭaniliyattil oru toṭṭi yiluḷḷa nīrākalām.
2. kalleṇṇey
mōṭṭār vaṇṭiyiṟ payaṇañceyvōr kalleṇṇiyiṉ mukkiyatti aṟivar. 
“peṭrōl” eṉappaṭum iv viyappāṉa eṇṇey eṅkiruntu kiṭikkiṟatu? itip paṟṟiya varalāṟu iṅku kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu.
kalleṇṇey eṉpatu oruvaki iyaṟkip poruḷ; atu uṟpattiyākkappaṭuvatilli. atu ōraḷaviṟ payaṉpaṭuttak kūṭiya nili yil ivvulakiṉ cila pakutikaḷiṟ kiṭikkiṉṟatu. mekcikkō eṇṇey viḷiyum nāṭu. urumēṉiyāvilum iṉṉum ulakiluḷḷa pala iṭaṅkaḷi lum nilattiṉ āḻattiluḷḷa paḷḷaṅkaḷil eṇṇillāta kālamāka kalleṇṇey niṟintu kiṭakkiṉṟatu. ikkūṟṟu viyappākat tōṉṟa lām. mōṭṭār vaṇṭiyi ōṭṭum eṇṇey ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷ nilattiṉ kīḻ maṟintu kiṭantatu. aṇmiyilēyē makkaḷ nilattiṉ ivveṇṇeyt tēkkaṅkaḷit tiṟantu eṇṇeyi eṭuttu ataṉi eri poruḷākap payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉar.
varuṅkāla eriporuḷ eṇṇeyākum. nāḷukku nāḷ atika eṇṇey payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. āḻnta kaṭaliṟ cellum periya kappalkaḷ nilakkari viṟakik koṇṭu ōṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. ippoḻutu avi eṇṇeyāl ōṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ākāyap paṭiyum pōrk kappal kaḷum kalleṇṇeyiṉāl iyaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. eṇṇey illāviṭil iṉṟu ceyyum pala kāriyaṅkaḷi nām ceyya muṭiyātu. āṇṭutōṟum pala kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa kalaṉ eṇṇey tariyiṉ kīḻiruntu eṭukkap paṭṭuk kaṭal vaḻiyāka āyirakkaṇakkāṉa milkaḷukkappāl koṇṭu pōkappaṭukiṟatu. 
eṇṇey eṅkum kāṇappaṭumāyiṉ nilattil oru kiṇaṟṟit tōṇṭi ataṉi eḷitil peṟṟuviṭalām. eṇṇey ulakiṉ cila iṭaṅ kaḷil māttiram kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu.
ivvaki iṭaṅkaḷiloṉṟu kirkak (Kirkuk). iṅku eṇṇey eppaṭik kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa teṉpatiyum, 1,150 mil vaṉāntarattuk kūṭāka atu eppaṭik kaṭaṟkarikkuk koṇṭu varappaṭṭateṉpatiyum ārāyvōm.
kirkak eṉpatu makkaḷ vāḻa virumpat takka iṭamaṉṟu. itu payir pacciyaṟṟatum niḻalillātatum nelliṭṭāl neṟporiyum kāṅki yuṭiyatumākiya nāṭu. 1927ām āṇṭiṉ muṉ itu veḷiyulakattukku aṟiyappaṭāta iṭamāka viruntatu; payir pacciyaṟṟiruntapōtum iṅku eṇṇey kiṭikkiṉṟatu. 1927ām āṇṭil iṅku eṇṇeyk kiṇaṟu akaḻvataṟku aṉumati īrāk peṭrōliyam kumpaṉiyārukkuk kiṭittatu.
eṇṇeyk kiṇaṟu tōṇṭuvatu eṉpatu eṇṇey ūṟṟil muṭṭum variyil oru kuḻāyi nilattuḷ iṟukkutal. poṟivallār (eñciṉiyarkaḷ) 3000 aṭi āḻattukku nilattit tuḷikka vēṇṭi yiruntatu. eṇṇey kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa orē nāḷil ivvaṉāntarap paṭṭiṉam mika mukkiyamuṭiyatāka māṟiṟṟu. īrāk peṭrōliyam kumpaṉiyeṉpatu pirāṉciya, āṅkila amerikka campantamuṭiyatu.
pālastīṉamum ciriyāvum muṟiyē iṅkilāntu pirāṉcu eṉṉum iru nāṭukaḷiṉ āṇikkuṭpaṭṭiruntaṉa. kirkak aṇmiyil irukkumāṉāl pālastīṉattiluḷḷa keypāvum (Haifa) ciriyāviluḷḷa tiripoliyum eṇṇey tēkkivippataṟku ēṟṟavi. kirkak keypāvi liruntu 530 mil tūrattilum tirippoli 620 mil tūrattilum iruntaṉa. ittūrattiṉ perumpakuti marañceṭi yaṟṟatākavum taṇṇīraṟṟa tākavum naccup pāmpukaḷum tēḷkaḷum uṟivatākavum petōṉiyar (Bedonians) eṉṉum alintu tiriyum kāṭṭuc cātiyiṉar vāḻvatākavu miruntatu.
ivvāṟiruntum etāvatu ceyya vēṇṭiyiruntatu. mikap payaṉuṭiya eṇṇeyi vaṉāntarattil payaṉiṉṟik kiṭakkaviṭa muṭiyāma liruntatu. vaṉāntarattuk kūṭāka iraṇṭu niri kuḻāykaḷ iṭuvatāka muṭivu ceyyappaṭṭatu oru niri kirkakkiliruntu keypāvukkum maṟṟatu kirkakki liruntu tirippolikkum celvaṉa. eṇṇeyi etirpārttirukkum tēkkaṅkaḷukku ikkuḻāykaḷ vaḻiyāka eṇṇey celuttappaṭa kūṭiyatākaviruntatu. 1,150 mil nīḷamuḷḷa urukkuk kuḻāykaḷ patikka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. itu evvaḷavu periya vēli!ivvaḷavu nīṇṭa tūrattukku muṉ orupōtum kuḻāykaḷ pōṭappaṭavilli. iv vēliyit toṭaṅkuvataṉ muṉ kuḻāykaḷ iṭavēṇṭiya pātiyit tīrmāṉittaṟku nilaaḷavikkārar palar ceṉṟaṉar.
ivvēli muṭintatum kuḻāykaḷi iṭuvōr ceṉṟaṉar. ivarkaḷ pātiyiṉ nīḷattukkuk kuḻāykaḷip pōṭṭukkoṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. avaṟṟip putikkavō poruttavō illi. ivvēlikaḷ ivar kaḷiṉ piṉṉāl ceṉṟa iṉṉoru kūṭṭattiṉarukku viṭappaṭṭaṉa. mika nīṇṭa tūram vaṉāntaramāka iruntatu. uccikkāla veyil 120 pākiyil kāyum. aṅku kuḷirnta pāṉaṅkaḷ kiṭiyā; taṇṇīr taṅkattilum pārkka aritiṟ kiṭippatāka viruntatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil maṇpuyalkaḷ vīci vēliyāḷariṉ kūṭāraṅkaḷi maṇalāl mūṭiviṭuvaṉa pōl payamuṟut tiṉa. ik kāṟṟukku etirākap pātukāppukaḷ illi. cila camayaṅkaḷil kāṟṟup pala nāṭkaḷ vīcum. kāṟṟu niṉṟatum kūṭāraṅkaḷiṉ pāti maṇṇāl mūṭuṇṭum uṇavum tuṇiyum maṇṇil maṟintum kiṭantaṉa. iyantiraṅ kaḷuḷ maṇ pukuntuviṭuvatāl vēli toṭaṅkumuṉ avaṟṟik kaḻaṟṟittuṭikka vēṇṭiyiruntatu.
maṇpuyalkaḷuḷ akappaṭṭavarkaḷukkallatu itaṉ apāyatti aṟiya muṭiyātu. mārikālattil vēliyāḷarukku vēṟuvakiyāṉa tuṉpaṅkaḷ nērntaṉa. ellā iṭaṅkaḷum veḷḷakkāṭāka iruntaṉa. iravil poṟukkamuṭiyāta kuḷir vīciṟṟu. ivvaki vaṉāntarattil nila aḷavikkārarukkup piṉṉāl periya mōṭṭār vaṇṭikaḷ nāṟpataṭi nīḷamuḷḷa kuḻāykaḷi ēṟṟikkoṇṭu ceṉṟaṉa. aṭiyāḷam ceyyap paṭṭa pāti vaḻiyē paḷḷattilum mēṭṭilum kuḻāykaḷ iṭappaṭṭaṉa. ivvaki vēliyil avarkaḷ tikiracu yupirātacu eṉṉum iru āṟukaḷiyum kaṭakkavēṇṭiyiruntatu. ivvāṟu iṭappaṭṭa kuḻāykaḷ nilattil putikkappaṭa vēṇṭiyiruntaṉa. 1150 mil tūrattukku akaḻikaḷi kiyiṉāl akaḻvatu muṭiyātiruntatu. ākavē akaḻitōṇṭum iyantiraṅkaḷ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. ivviyantiraṅkaḷ akaṉṟa urukkuc cakkaraṅkaḷil ceṉṟaṉa. iyantiraṅkaḷ iraṇṭaṭi akalamum āṟaṭi āḻamumuṭiya akaḻiyit tōṇṭiṉa. nāḷoṉṟukku oru mil nīḷamuḷḷa akaḻiyit tōṇṭa muṭintatu. akaḻi tōṇṭum iyantiraṅkaḷil piṉ ceṉṟōr nīṇṭa kuḻāykaḷip porutti akaḻiyiliṭṭu maṇṇip paravi mūṭiṉar. kuḻāykaḷiṉ varici paḷḷat tiliruntapōtu eṇṇey mēṭṭiliruntu kīḻē cellakkūṭiyatāka viruntatu. pala iṭaṅkaḷil pāti mēṭṭu nilaṅkaḷukkūṭākac cella vēṇṭiyiruntatu. appoḻutu eṉṉa nērntatu? eṇṇey mēṭṭukkuc cella māṭṭātu. kuḻāppāti mēṭṭuc carivukku vanta pōtu aṅkē eṇṇeyi iyantirattiṉāl mēlē celuttum niliyam (Phming Station) amikka vēṇṭiyiruntatu. itu mēṭu allatu kuṉṟiṉ uccikku eṇṇeyic celutti ataṉi maṟupakkattukku ōṭac ceyyum.
kirkakkiliruntu keypāvukkum tiripolikku miṭiyiluḷḷa kuḻāppātiyil iyantirattāl eṇṇeyi uyara ēṟṟum niliyaṅkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ pala vaṉāntarattil maṟintirukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟ ṟukkum nākarika ulakattukku miṭiyiluḷḷa toṭarpu tantik kampiyum kampiyillāt tantiyumākum. ovvoru niliyattilum poṟivallārum utaviyāḷarkaḷum kuḻāyp pātiyip pātukākka āyutam tāṅkiya cila kāvaṟ kārarumiruppar.
āyutan tāṅkiya kāvalāḷar alaṅkārattukkāka aṅku irukka villi. pāti iṭappaṭukiṉṟa kālattum petōṉiyar eṉṉum kāṭṭuc cātiyiṉar vēliyāṭkaḷip payamuṟuttiṉar. ivvāṟu periya kuḻāyp pāti muṭikkappaṭṭu kirkakliruntu tiripolikkum keypāvukkum 1150 mil vaṉāntaraṅkaḷuk kūṭāka eṇṇey koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭatu.
ippātiyip pōṭṭu muṭikka 10,000,000 pavuṇ celavāyiṟṟu. ip periya vēliyil 10,000 pēr īṭupaṭṭiruntārkaḷ. kaṇṇukkup pulappaṭāmal vaṉāntara maṇalil kiṭakkum ikkuḻāyppāti vaḻiyāka āṇṭil 4,000,000 toṉ eṭi eṇṇey koṇṭu pōkappaṭukiṟatu. ivvaḷavu atika eṇṇey veḷiyēṟuvataṉāl eṇṇeyk kiṇaṟukaḷ vaṟṟiviṭumeṉṟu cilar niṉikkalām. avi vaṟṟamāṭṭā. āṇṭutōṟum eṇṇey veḷḷam kirkakkiliruntu taṭiyiṉṟic ceṉṟukoṇṭiruk kiṟatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil petōṉiyar caṭutiyākak kuḻāykaḷik kiḷaṟi uṭittuviṭuvar. eṇṇey veḷiyē pōvatu kiṭṭiya niliyattukkut teriya vantatum aṅku niṉṟum poṟivallārkaḷum āyutam tāṅkiya kāvalāḷikaḷum ceṉṟu paḻutic carippaṭuttuvar. poṟivallār kuḻāyi luṇṭāṉa uṭippip paḻutu pārkkumpōtu āyutan tāṅkiyavarkaḷ avviṭattil iṉṉum tirikiṉṟa kāṭṭuc cātiyiṉarit turattuvar. icciṟiya paṭi maṟupaṭiyum tamatu niliyattukkut tirumpivarukiṉṟatu; kuḻāykaḷ vaḻiyē eṇṇey vaḻakkampōl ōṭat toṭaṅkukiṟatu.
3. intiyāvil paḻaṅkālak kalvi muṟi
vētakālattil māṇavaṉ āciriyariṭattil kalvi payilat toṭaṅ kiṉāṉ. upanayaṉam eṉṉum kiriyiyi naṭattiyapiṉ avar avaṉi māṇakkaṉāka oppukkoṇṭār. kiriyi mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷ naṭipeṟṟatu.
itaṉāl āciriyar māṇavaṉukku iraṇṭāvatu piṟappuk koṭup pavarākak karutappaṭṭatu. upavītattukkup piṉ māṇavaṉ piramacāri vāḻkkiyil nuḻintāṉ.
piramacārikku vitikkappaṭṭa vāḻkki muṟikaḷip piṉpaṟṟi avaṉ oḻuka vēṇṭum. avaṉ taruppip pullāṟ ceyta arippaṭṭi yum kaṟuppu māṉ tōlum aṇintu nīṇṭa talimayir uṭiyavaṉāy kālimāli eṉṉum iru poḻutukaḷilum tī vaḷarppataṟkuc camittuk koṇṭu vantāṉ. taṉṉaṭakkam, tavam, picci ēṟṟal pōṉṟu vitikkap paṭṭuḷḷa oḻukkaṅkaḷiyum ivaṉ kikkoṇṭāṉ. piramacārikaḷip pōla āciriyaṉiṭattiṟ kalvi payilum piramacāriṉikaḷākiya peṇkaḷu miruntaṉar.
cila camayaṅkaḷil tantiyē āciriyaṉākaviruntāṉ. kalvi kaṟkum kālam perumpālum paṉṉiraṇṭu āṇṭukaḷākaviruntatu. māṇākkaṉ āciriyaṉ illattilēyē avaṉōṭu taṅkiyiruntāṉ. piramacāriyākiya māṇavaṉ āciriyaṉukkākap picci ēṟkac ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉuṭiya maṟṟoru vēli tī vaḷarttal, āciriyaṉiṉ vīṭṭu vēlikaḷik kavaṉippatu, āṭumāṭukaḷi mēyppatu eṉpaṉavum māṇavaṉuṭiya kaṭamikaḷākaviruntaṉa. āciriyaṉum māṇavaṉum nilattil iruntārkaḷ. pāṭaṅ kēṭkumpōtu māṇavaṉ piṉpuṟamāka allatu muṉ puṟamākac cāyntu niṟṟal kūṭātu.
āciriyariṭattil uyarnta oḻukkamum āṉma ñāṉamum iruttal vēṇṭum. takutiyāṉa māṇavaṉ varumpōtu avar taṉakkut terinta vaṟṟi maṟiyātu avaṉukkuk kaṟpittal vēṇṭum; māṇavaṉ ēṟkat takutiyākavuḷḷa karuttukkaḷi māttiram avaṉukkuc collutal vēṇ ṭum; maṟṟavikaḷic collutal kūṭātu. āciriyar oru pāṭattit tammāl kaṟpikka iyalātu eṉṟu kaṇṭāl māṇavaṉi iṉṉoru āciriyariṭa maṉuppalām. tām kalvi kaṟpikkakkūṭiya māṇavar palarip peṟuvatu āciriyariṉ viruppamākaviruntatu. tām kaṇṭaṟinta uṇmikaḷ tamakkup piṉpum nili peṟa vēṇṭumeṉpatu avarkaḷ karuttu. māṇavar kalvi kaṟpikkakkūṭiya nallāciriyarit tēṭit tūra tēcaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉar. māṇavar kēḷvi kēṭka āciriyar viḷaṅka vittal pōṉṟa muṟiyil kalvi payilappaṭṭatu. peṇkaḷukkuk kalvi maṟukkappaṭavilli. upa niṭataṅkaḷil kalviyiṟ ciṟanta pala peṇkaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ kāṇappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
piṟkālattil ciṟuvarukku aintu vayatil kalvi toṭakkappaṭṭatu. piṉ kuṭumi vittalum ataṉpiṉ upanayaṉan tarittalumākiya kiriyi kaḷum ceyyappaṭṭaṉa. nāl varuṇattārukkum upanayaṉam uriyatu eṉṟu pōtāyaṉar kūṟiyuḷḷār.
kāliyil āciriyar eḻumuṉṉum cūriyaṉ tōṉṟuvataṉ muṉṉum māṇavaṉ eḻuntiruttal vēṇṭum. eḻuntapiṉ kuḷittal vēṇṭum. kuḷikkum pōtu nīril viḷiyāṭutal kūṭātu; aciyāmal nīntalām; oru nāḷil mūṉṟu muṟi kuḷittal vēṇṭum; kuḷittapiṉ niṉṟa nili yil kaṭavuḷit tutittal vēṇṭum; pūmāli aṇital kūṭātu. vācaṉip poruḷkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttutal, uṭalil eṇṇey pūcikkoḷḷutal ,kaṇṇukku mi tīṭṭutal, ceruppu, kuṭi, vaṇṭikaḷip payaṉpaṭuttutal kūṭātu; pakalil nittiri koḷḷutal icikkaruvikaḷip payaṉpaṭut tutal, pāṭutal, āṭutal ākātu.
māṇavaṉ āciriyarukkuk kīḻppaṭital vēṇṭum; āciriyarirup patilum tāḻnta ācaṉattil iruttal vēṇṭum. āciriyar pāṭam kaṟpikka varumpōtu avar pātaṅkaḷi vaṇaṅka vēṇṭum. āciriyar coṉṉa tum paṭikka ārampikka vēṇṭum. avarukku nērākak kāli nīṭṭutal ākātu. paṭuttiruntukoṇṭu āciriyarik kūppiṭutal kūṭātu. āciriyar paṭuttiruntāl māṇavaṉ iruntukoṇṭu maṟumoḻi collalām. āciriyar niṉṟāl ivaṉum eḻuntu niṉṟu maṟumoḻi collutal vēṇṭum; cerupput tarittukkoṇṭu allatu taliyi mūṭikkoṇṭu āciriyari aṇukutal kūṭātu; āciriyarukku atika tūrattilum atika kiṭṭattilum iruttal kūṭātu. āciriyarukku muṉṉāl toṇṭiyi mūṭikkoḷḷutal, cuvaril cāytal, kāli nīṭṭutal, tupputal, cirittal, koṭṭāvi viṭutal, neṭṭi muṟittal kūṭātu.
pāṭam toṭaṅkumpōtum muṭiyumpōtum māṇavaṉ āciriyariṉ pātaṅkaḷil vaṇaṅkutal vēṇṭum. āciriyar iru eṉṟatum kiḻakku allatu mēṟku mukamāka āciriyarukku valappuṟattil iruttal vēṇṭum. ōyntirukkum pōtu paṭittavaṟṟil cantēkamāṉavaṟṟic cintittal vēṇṭum.
muṟkālattil peṇkaḷukkuk kalvi maṟukkappaṭṭirukkavilli eṉpataṟku eṭuttukkāṭṭāka kōṣā , urōmācā, ulōpāmuttirā, vicuvāmittirā mutaliya peṇ iruṭikaḷiṉ peyarkaḷ irukku vētattiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. peṇkaḷ piramavātiṉi, cattiyōpāku eṉa iruvakip paṭṭārkaḷ. piramavātiṉiyic cērntavar upanayaṉam akkiṉi kāriyaṅkaḷ, vētakkalvi, picci ēṟṟal mutaliyavaṟṟukkuriyar. cattiyōpākuvic cērntavar maṇañceytukoḷvataṉ muṉ upanayaṉam ceytukoḷḷutal vēṇṭum. muṟkālattil peṇkaḷ upanayaṉam, vētakkalvi, kāyattiri mantiram ōtal mutaliyavaṟṟukku arukarāyiruntārkaḷ eṉṟu iyamaṉ kūṟiyuḷḷār.
pirāmaṇa māṇavar pirāmaṇarallāta āciriyariṉ kīḻ kalvi payilvatum iyalpāka viruntatu. camāvarttaṉam eṉṉum kiriyiyiṉāl kalvi muṭivaṭiyappeṟṟatu. appoḻutu māṇavaṉ piramacārikku aṭi yāḷamākiya tāṭiyiyum, nīṇṭa mayiriyum maḻittukkoṇṭu naṇpar kaḷāl koṭukkappaṭṭa vācaṉip poruḷkaḷi uṭampil pūcikkoṇṭu, piramacārikku aṭiyāḷamākavirunta taṇṭam pōrvi mutaliyavaṟṟi āṟṟil eṟintāṉ. nīrāṭiya piṉ avaṉ putiya āṭi aṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇintukoṇṭu tēril allatu yāṉiyil ēṟi avvūriluḷḷa vittuva capikkuc ceṉṟu, tāṉ oru vittuvāṉ eṉṉum niliyil camukamaḷittāṉ.
kalvi, āciriyar colla māṇavar kēṭṭuc colli maṉappāṭañ ceyyum muṟiyāka viruntatu. vētam, ilakkaṇam, nīti mutaliya pāṭaṅkaḷip paṭippataṟkut taṉippāṭacālikaḷ iruntaṉa. āciriyaṉ māṇavaṉiṉ kuṟikaḷi maṟikkum kuṭiyip pōlaviruntāṉ.
āciriyi, upāttiyāyiṉi upāttiyāya āccāriyā eṉap paṭṭāḷ. vētaṅkaḷil valla peṇkaḷumiruntaṉar. peṇkaḷukku irāṇuvap payiṟciyum aḷikkappaṭṭatu. cāktiki eṉṉum collukku īṭṭi piṭitta peṇ eṉpatu poruḷ.
kuṭumi vikkum caṭaṅku muṭintatum, ciṟuvarukku eḻuttum kaṇakkum kaṟpikkappaṭavēṇṭumeṉṟu kuṭaliyar kūṟukiṉṟār. irāca kumāraṉiṉ kalvi avaṉatu 16-vatu āṇṭil muṟṟuppeṟṟatu. piṉpu avaṉ maṇañ ceytu koḷḷavēṇṭum. avaṉuṭiya kalvikaḷuḷ oṉṟu taṇṭanīti (āṭci puriyum kalvi). maṇamāṉa piṉpum avaṉ yāṉiyēṟṟam, kutiriyēṟṟam paṭippayiṟci mutaliya pōrkkalviyik kaṟṟuk koḷḷutal vēṇṭum.
muṟkālattil kirāmattukkuk kirāmam paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷ amittu māṇavarukkuk kalvi payiṟṟum oḻuṅku irukkavilli. tiṇṇip paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷ iruntaṉa. tiṇṇip paḷḷik kūṭak kalvi muṭintatum uyartarak kalviyi virumpiṉōr āṅkāṅku pukaḻpeṟṟu viḷaṅkiya āciriyarkaḷiṭam ceṉṟu pāṭaṅ kēṭṭaṉar. putta, camaṇa mataṅkaḷ paraviyapōtu putta vikāraṅkaḷilum , camaṇap paḷḷikaḷilum putta, camaṇa kurumārāl māṇavarukkuk kalvi kaṟpikkappaṭṭatu. immuṟiyip piṉpaṟṟik kiṟittuva kōvilkaḷilum kalvic cālikaḷ uṇṭāyiṉa. ikkalvi muṟiyiṉ vaḷarcciyē iṉṟiya paḷḷik kūṭaṅkaḷākum.
4. pukiyiliyiṉ kati
cēr vālṭar irali eṉpavariyum avaratu pukiyili pukikkum cuṅkāṉippaṟṟiyum ellārum kēḷvippaṭṭirukkalām.
ivar amerikkāviliruntu iṅkilāntukkut tirumpic ceṉṟapiṉ tamatu paṭikkum aṟiyiliruntu cuṅkāṉil pukiyiliyi vittup pukittuk koṇṭiruntār. avar muṉṉorupōtum pukippiṭippatik kaṇṭirāta vēlikkāraṉ avaratu taliyic cūḻntu puki varuvatik kaṇṭāṉ. pukiyiliyip paṟṟiyum ataṉip pukikkum cuṅkāṉip paṟṟiyum aṟiyāta avaṉ iraliyil tīppiṭittiruppatāka niṉittu oru vāḷiyil taṇṇīr koṇṭuvantu taṉatu ecamāṉiṉ talimītu ūṟṟiṉāṉ.
cērvālṭar iraliyē mutaṉmutal pukiyiliyi airōppāvukkuk koṇṭu ceṉṟavarāvār. pukiyiliyippaṟṟiya varalāṟu amerikkāvi luḷḷa kāṭṭuc cevvintiyariṭiyē ārampamākiṉṟatu. pukiyili pukikkum paḻakkam avarkaḷiṭiyē ullācam eṉpatu pōlirātu vēṟu vakiyil iruntatu. cevvintiyar pukiyiliyi mikap puṉitamuḷḷa ceṭiyākak karutiṉar. cevvintiyaṉuṭiya mika matippukkuriya poruḷ avaṉuṭiya cuṅkāṉ. avaṉ taṉatu vāḻnāḷ muḻumiyilum ataṉi uṭaṉkoṇṭu tirintāṉ. maraṇakālattil atu avaṉōṭu putikkap paṭṭatu.
kalviyaṟivuṭiya cevvintiyaṉoruvaṉip pukiyiliyiṉ toṭakkattip paṟṟik kēṭṭāl avaṉ aṟivu muṟiyāṉatum viyap paḷiyātatumākiya maṟumoḻi kūṟuvāṉ. iralikālattu vīraṉoruvaṉ vēṟu vakiyāṉa kati kūṟiyiruppāṉ. mikap paḻaṅkālattil iṉṟiyilum pārkka ulakam mika naṉṟāka viruntapōtu tēvati oṉṟu ivvulakip pārkka vanta katiyip paṟṟi avaṉ kūṟuvāṉ. ciṟitunēram alintu tirinta piṉ at tēvatikku nittiri vantatu. atu kāṭṭil neruppuk koḷuttivittuviṭṭu ataṉ pakkattē paṭuttu nittiri koṇṭatu. appoḻutu it tēvatikkup pakiyāṉa iṉṉoru tēvati ataṉ pakkattāl kāṭṭuvaḻiyē pōykkoṇṭiruntatu. atu neruppukkup pakkattē naṉku nittiri koḷḷum tēvatiyik kaṇṭu ataṟkut tīṅkiḻikka niṉittatu. atu etiriyiṉ nittiriyik kuḻappātapaṭi melleṉat tavaḻntu vantu ataṉ tali nerupputtaṉal mītu kiṭakkum paṭi uruṭṭiviṭṭu ōṭicceṉṟatu. neruppuc cuṭutaliṉālum mayirkaḷ eriyum oliyiṉālum viḻitteḻunta tēvati payattiṉāl kāṭṭukkūṭāka ōṭic ceṉṟatu. ōṭumpōtu kāṟṟu vīciyatāl ataṉ erikiṉṟa mayir amerikkāviṉ oru pakkattiliruntu maṟupakkam variyil viḻuntatu. eṅku mayir viḻuntatō aṅku atu vērviṭṭup pukiyilic ceṭiyāka vaḷarntatu. ivvāṟu cevvintiyar pukiyiliyip paṟṟiya katiyik kūṟuvar.
pukiyiliyip pōlavē cevvintiyaṉiṉ pukiyili pukikkum cuṅkāṉum puṉitamuṭiyatu. periya tēvati toṭakkattil pukiyili pukittatu eṉṟu muṟkālac cevvintiyar nampiṉar. periya tēvati mutaṉmutal cuṅkāṉil pukiyili pukittatāka cevvintiyar nampivarum iṭatti iṉṟiyap pirayāṇi oruvaṉ pārkkalām. ivviṭattukku “cuṅkāṉil pukiyili pukitta pāṟi” eṉṟu peyar. nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷāka itu amerikkāviṉ mikap puṉita iṭamāka iruntu varukiṉ ṟatu. amerikka vīrar tamatu vāḻnāḷil ippāṟikkuc ceṉṟu atiliruntu puṉitamuḷḷa civanta kalli eṭuttut tamatu pukiyili pukikkum cuṅkāṉic ceytaṉar.
cevvintiyariṉ viḻākkaḷil cuṅkāṉ mutaṉmiyāṉatu. ovvoru kulattāriṭattum tattam kulattukkuriya cuṅkāṉ iruntatu. ik kulattiṉariṉ taliyārikaḷ kūṭṭam iccuṅkāṉiṉṟi naṭipeṟamāṭṭātu. kūṭṭam toṭaṅkuvataṉmuṉ cuṅkāṉ oruvar kiyiliruntu oruvar kikku māṟṟappaṭṭatu. ovvoru puṉitamuḷḷa vīraṉum pukiyi oru muṟi vāyniṟiya uṟiñciṉāṉ. cevvintiya pōr vīrar tamatu taliyik kaḻukiṉ iṟakukaḷāl alaṅkarippar; kūṭārattukkumuṉ eriyum neruppic cūḻntu vaṭṭamākaviruppar. cuṅkāṉi oruvariliruntu maṟṟavarukku māṟṟuvar. camātāṉa uṭaṉpaṭikkiyip paṟṟi ālōcippataṟkuk kūṭṭam kūṭumāyiṉ etirikaḷiṉ taliyārikaḷum uṭaṉiruntu camātāṉac cuṅkāṉip pukippar.
ekkāraṇam paṟṟik kūṭṭaṅkūṭiṉālum paricuttamāṉa cuṅkāṉ aṅkiruntatu. cuṅkāṉillātu evvakik karumamum muṭivu ceyyappaṭa māṭṭātu.
cēr vālṭar irali iṅkilāntukkup pukiyiliyikkoṇṭu ceṉṟapiṉ pukiyili celvariṉ ullācamāka iruntatu. pukiyili mutaṉmutal iṅkilāntukkuk koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭapōtu oru avuṉcu pukiyiliyiṉ vili irupattintu ciliṉāka viruntatu. ākavē pukiyilic celavik ceṭṭup paṭuttuvataṟkākac cevvintiyaruḷ naṭipeṟṟatu pōṉṟa vaḻakkamum aṅku uṇṭāyiṟṟu. palar oru iṭattil cantittu oru cuṅkāṉil pukiyiliyi nirappi neruppu mūṭṭiṉar; piṉṉar ellōrum cila taṭavi pukiyi uṟiñcumpaṭi ataṉi oruvar kiyi liruntu maṟṟavar kikku māṟṟiṉar. atu iṉṟiya makkaḷ pārttup parikacikkattakkatākaviruntirukkum. ikkāla āṭavarum makaḷirum pukikkum atika pukiyiliyi muṟkālattavar pārttāl viyap paṭivar. iṉṟu amerikkāvil māttiram oru āṇṭil 110,000,000,000 veṇ curuṭṭukaḷ pukikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. 
nām ellōrum vērciṉiyāp pukiyiliyip paṟṟik kēḷvip paṭṭirukkiṟōm. vērciṉiyā veṉpatu amerikkāviluḷḷa ōriṭam. itu oru kālattil āṅkilariṉ kuṭiyēṟṟa nāṭāka viruntatu. iṅkilāntiṟ pukikkappaṭum pukiyili muḻuvatum oru kālattil vērciṉiyāvi liruntu ceṉṟatu. muṟkālattil vērciṉiyāvil pukiyili payiriṭu vōr celvarāka viruntaṉar. avarkaḷ periya nilaṅkaḷil kaṭṭappaṭṭa vicālamāṉa māḷikikaḷil vāḻntārkaḷ. nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa aṭimikaḷ avarkaḷiṭattiliruntu vēli ceytārkaḷ. patiṉēḻu patiṉeṭṭām nūṟ ṟāṇṭukaḷil ērāḷamāṉa pukiyili iṅkilāntil iṟakkumatiyāyiṟṟu.
maṟṟa nāṭukaḷil pukiyilic ceṭi payiriṭappaṭuvataṉ muṉ vērciṉiyāp pukiyiliyilum pārkkak kuṟinta viliyiṟ kiṭikkum poruṭṭu iṅkilāntil pukiyilic ceṭi payiriṭappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟu ceytal piriṭṭiṣ aracāṅkattukku atika paṇam tīrviyākak kiṭit tatu. evvaḷavu pukiyili veḷiyiliruntu vantatō avvaḷavukku atu atika celvamaṭintatu. itē vakik koḷki iṉṟum intiyāvilē tiruvitāṅkūr irācciyattil iruntu varukiṉṟatu. aṅku pukiyilic ceṭiyip payiriṭuvatu caṭṭavirōtamāṉa ceyal eṉak koḷḷappaṭum.
iṅkilāntil payiriṭappaṭṭuḷḷa pukiyilic ceṭikaḷi ellām aḻittu viṭutal vēṇṭum eṉṉum caṭṭam kuromvel kālattil koṇṭu varappaṭṭatu. pukiyili payiriṭuvōr ic caṭṭattukkuk kīḻp paṭiyavilli. ākavē tamatu caṭṭatti naṭimuṟiyiṟ koṇṭu vara oru kūṭṭam pōrvīrari kuromvel ēviṉār. itu pukiyili payiriṭu vōrik kōpam mūṭṭiṟṟu. avarkaḷ araciṉar paṭiyi etirkka āḷ tiraṭṭiṉārkaḷ. uḷnāṭṭuk kalakam mūḷavilli. kuromvel taṉatu vīrarit tiruppi aḻittukkoṇṭār. avvāṇṭup pukiyilic cey kikkup piṉ avarkaḷ ataṉi payiriṭutal kūṭātu eṉṟu avarkaḷukku aṟivikkappaṭṭatu. accaṭṭattukku makkaḷ kīḻppaṭiyavilli. patiṉēḻām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ piṟpakutiyaḷavil iṅkilāntil pukiyilic ceyki niṉṟatu.
ik kālattil mūṉṟu vakip pukiyili payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu. amerikkāvil vērciṉiyāp pukiyiliyum, āciyā, āppirikkāvil turukkiya pukiyiliyum, pāracīkattil kāram kuṟinta pukiyiliyum payiriṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru avuṉcu niṟiyil 40,000 pukiyili vitikaḷ variyil uṇṭu. vitikaḷi mēṭiyiliṭṭu uṇṭākkiya kaṉṟukaḷ piṭuṅki naṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. pukiyilic ceṭiyiṉ ellā ilikaḷum puki yiliyāka varuvatilli. ceṭikaḷil pūkkaḷ tōṉṟiyatum pūkkaḷ kiḷḷi eṟiyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṉ talippu muṟikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. nalla ilikaḷ māttiram ceṭiyiliruntu muṟṟaviṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. evvakikkup puki yiliyip payaṉpaṭutta vēṇṭumō ataṟku ēṟpa ilikaḷ kūṭavum kuṟiyavum viṭappaṭum. cuṅkāṉ allatu veṇcuruṭṭup payaṉukkāyiṉ paṉṉiraṇṭu ilikaḷ variyil viṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. curuṭṭup payaṉuk kāyiṉ irupatu ilikaḷ variyil viṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
talippu muṟittu aintu allatu āṟu vāraṅkaḷiṉ piṉ ilikaḷ muṟṟi veṭṭi eṭuppataṟkup patamāka virukkum. mika viliyēṟiya puki yilikaḷ muṟṟa muṟṟa oṭittu eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ilikaḷ ularntapiṉ taraṅkaḷākat teriyappaṭṭup piṭikaḷākak kaṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
pōrccukkēyar kiḻakku nāṭukaḷukkup pukiyili vitikaḷik koṇṭu ceṉṟu pukiyili payiriṭum muṟiyi makkaḷukku aṟivit tārkaḷ. intiyā, ilaṅki, malāyt tīvukaḷ, pilippiṉ tīvukaḷil puki yili atikam payiriṭappaṭukiṟatu. curuṭṭu, pīṭi, veṇcuruṭṭu, mūkkuttūḷ vakikaḷil pukiyili payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. itu tāmpūlattōṭum cērttuk koḷḷappaṭukiṟatu. cilar itaṉi vāyiliṭṭuk koḷvar.
pukiyiliyil nikōṭiṉ eṉṉum oruvaki nañcu uṇṭu. itaṉ upayōkam tīmi viḷivippatu eṉa maruntu nūlār kūṟuvar. curuṭṭu, cikareṭ piṭippatu nākarika capip paḻakkaṅkaḷuḷ oṉṟu eṉavum, ivaṟṟip payaṉpaṭuttum paḻakkamillātavar nākarikap paḻakkamillātār eṉavum koḷḷum karuttukaḷum nilavukiṉṟaṉa. ulakam muḻumiyilum oru āṇṭil muppatu ilaṭcam toṉ pukiyili viḷikiṟatu. iṅkilāntil pukiyili campantamāṉa tīrviyāl āṇṭu oṉṟukkuk kiṭippatu 75,000,000 pavuṇākum. intiyāvil cilar itip piramamūli eṉak kūṟukiṉṟaṉar. tamiḻ nāṭṭup pulavaroruvar “pukiyili viṭu tūtu” eṉa oru nūlum ceytuḷḷār. atu makō upāttiyāyar u.vē.cāmināta aiyaravarkaḷāl acciṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. atil ip piramamūli tēva ulaki liruntu pūvulakukku vanta varalāṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
5. nallaperiya paḻakkaṅkaḷip peṟavaki
āyiram āyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ ivvulakam muḻumiyilum kāṭu iruntatu. aṅku kāṭṭu maṉitar vāḻntaṉar.
kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷ, kāṭṭu maraṅkaḷ, kāṭṭup pūkkaḷ, kāṭṭup paḻaṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉavum aṅku kāṇappaṭṭaṉa. maṉitaṉāl payiriṭṭu vaḷarkkappaṭum tāvaraṅkaḷ oṉṟum irukkavilli.
ak kāla maṉitariṉ uṭali mayir mūṭiyiruntatu. am mūrkkamāṉa maṉitar tām koṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟicciyiyuṅ, kāṭṭut tēṉiyum uṇṭu vantaṉar. makkaḷ ciṟitu ciṟitāka nākarikamaṭintapōtu tamakkuk kiṭitta paḻaṅkaḷi uṇṭum, tāmkoṉṟa vilaṅkukaḷiṉ iṟicciyip pucittum vāḻum vāḻkkiyi virumpavilli. kāṭṭup paḻaṅkaḷ atikam kiṭittapōtum vēṭṭikkārarukku vēṭṭi vilaṅku kiṭittapōtum avarkaḷ tiruptiyaṭintu vantārkaḷ. paḻaṅkaḷ kiṭiyātapōtum vēṭṭikkārarukku vilaṅkukaḷ kiṭiyātapōtum avarkaḷ paṭṭiṉi yiruntu paciyāl iṟantu pōka vēṇṭiyiruntatu.
namatu muṉṉōril putticālikaḷ cilar kūṭiyiruntu tamakku uṇavukaḷ eppoḻutum kiṭikkak kūṭiya pala vaḻi vakikaḷi ālōcittārkaḷ; avarkaḷ cila kāṭṭu vilaṅkukaḷip piṭittu vaḷarttārkaḷ; kāṭṭiluḷḷa tāvaraṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟiyum naṭṭu vaḷarttārkaḷ. avarkaḷ mutal mutal naṭṭu vaḷartta tāvara vaki kāṭṭuppuṟkaḷ. cila puṟkaḷiṉ vitikaḷ kaṟkaḷiṉiṭiyē vittu arittu māvākkappaṭṭapōtu uṇpataṟku ēṟṟaṉavāka viruppati avarkaḷ aṟintārkaḷ. piṉṉar avarkaḷ kāṭṭut tāṉiyaṅkaḷ cilavaṟṟi vitittu aṟuvaṭi ceytārkaḷ.
ik kati uṇmiyillāta kaṟpaṉik katipōlat tōṉṟalām. tāṉiyam ivvāṟu payiriṭappaṭṭirukkalām. tāṉiyaṅkaḷiṉ toṭakkam kāṭṭuppuṟkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟatu. ivvāṟē nāmuṇṇum kāykaṟikaḷ paḻaṅkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ toṭakkamum oru kālattil vaḷarnta marañceṭikaḷukku allatu paḻaṅkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟatu.
kāṭṭil vaḷarnta oru ceṭi payiriṭappaṭṭapōtu atu pala iṉaṅkaḷāka māṟiṟṟu eṉṟu aṟivatu mika viyappākum. orē iṉat tāvaram vevvēṟu veppaniliyuṭiya iṭaṅkaḷilum, ceḻumiyuṭiyavum ceḻumiyaṟṟavumāṉa iṭaṅkaḷilum payiriṭappaṭumpōtu vevvēṟu iṉaṅkaḷākap perukukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟu nām uṇṇum āppiḷ paḻaṅ kaḷik koṭukkum maraṅkaḷ puḷippup paḻam kāykkum oruvaki āppiḷ marattiliruntu vantavi. kāṭṭil vaḷarum marañceṭikaḷukkum payiriṭap paṭum marañceṭikaḷukku miṭiyil atika vēṟupāṭu uṇṭu. maṉitaṉuṭiya utaviyiṉṟi iyaṟki ivvēṟupāṭṭic ceyya muṭiyātu.
muṟkālattil maṉitaṉ ivvēṟupāṭṭic ceyya muyaṉṟu vanta muṟi “terivu” eṉappaṭum. muṟkālattil vāḻnta payiriṭuvōṉoruvaṉ oru ceṭiyiṉ paḻattilum pārkka nalla cuvi aḷitta iṉṉoru atē iṉacceṭiyi virumpiyirukkalām. “terivu” eṉappaṭuvatu immuṟiyē yākum. payiriṭuvōṉ orē iṉacceṭikaḷ palavaṟṟi nōkkuvāṉ. avaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu yātō kāraṇattiṉāl maṟṟac ceṭikaḷilum pārkka nalla paḻaṅkaḷ koṭuppati avaṉ kāṇpāṉ. avaṉ nalla kaṉikaḷik koṭāta ceṭikaḷi aḻittu viṭṭu nalla kaṉikaḷ koṭukkum ceṭikaḷi uṇṭākkuvāṉ. ivvakiyāṉa terivu iṉṉumorumuṟi toṭaṅkum. eppoḻutum nalla ceṭikaḷi aḻittuviṭutalāl payiriṭuvōṉ oru kālattil parumiyum cuviyumuḷḷa kaṉikaḷik koṭukkum ceṭikaḷi uṇṭākkiviṭuvāṉ.
ik kālam tāvaraṅkaḷi uyarnta kuṇam aṭiyac ceyyum muṟi, terivōṭu kalappuk karukkoḷḷac ceytal ākum. (Cross fertilization). im muṟiyāka nalla iyalpukaḷi uṇṭākkumpōtu payiriṭuvōṉ orē tāvara vakiyil pala iṉaṅkaḷik kaṇṭaṟivāṉ. avaṟṟuḷ ellāvaṟṟi lum pārkkac ciṟappāṉa kaṉikaḷi utavum iraṇṭu ceṭikaḷit terivāṉ. oru ceṭi cuviyillāta periya paḻattik koṭuppatāyirukkalām. iraṇṭāvataṉ kaṉi maṟṟiya kaṉikaḷilum pārkkac cuviyuṭiyatāy parumiyiṟ ciṟitāyirukkalām. ippoḻutu ceṭikaḷi uṇṭākkuvōṉ ivvirukaṉikaḷiṉ kuṇaṅkaḷuṭiya oru ceṭiyi uṇṭākka virumpu kiṉṟāṉ. ivvāṟu uṇṭākkuvataṟku avaṉ ceṭikaḷik kalappuk karukkoḷḷacceytal vēṇṭum.
kalappukkaru eṉpatu eṉṉa eṉṟu nīvir aṟiya virumpalām. ovvoru tāvaramum āṇ pū, peṇ pū eṉṉum iruvakip pūkkaḷip pūkkiṉṟatu. āṇ pūviluḷḷa makarantam peṇ pūviṟpaṭāviṭil ceṭi oru pōtum kāykka māṭṭātu. ivvēliyit tēṉīkkaḷum paṟakkum pūccikaḷum ceykiṉṟaṉa. iru ceṭikaḷiṉ paṇpukaḷum orē ceṭiyil amiyumpaṭi putiya ceṭiyi uṇṭākka virumpukiṉṟavaṉ oṭṭaka mayirk kucciyiṉāl oru ceṭiyiṉ āṇ pū makarantatti maṟṟac ceṭiyiṉ peṇ pūvuḷ taṭaviviṭukiṉṟāṉ. ituvē kalappukkaru uṇṭākkutal eṉappaṭum. ivvāṟu kalappuk karukkoṇṭu kāytta paḻattiṉ vitikaḷiliruntu muḷikkum ceṭikaḷ iraṇṭu ceṭikaḷiṉ kuṇamumuṭiyaṉavāka virukkum.
tāvaraṅkaḷip payiriṭupavaṉ ivviraṇṭu ceṭikaḷaḷavil tirupti yaṭiyamāṭṭāṉ. avaṉiṭattil kāykāykkum nāṉku orē iṉamaraṅkaḷ niṟkalām. avaṉ an nāṉku ceṭikaḷilum kāykkum paḻaṅkaḷiṉ kuṇamuṭiya oru ceṭiyi uṇṭākka niṉikkalām. ākavē avaṉ kalappukkaru uṇṭākka muyalalām. avaṉ mutalil ivviraṇṭākak kalappukkaru uṇṭākac ceytu nāṉku ceṭikaḷiṉ kuṇamuṭiya iraṇṭu ceṭikaḷi uṇṭākkuvāṉ. piṉpu ivviraṇṭiliruntu oru ceṭiyi uṇṭākkuvāṉ. mēl nāṭukaḷil cuviyum parumiyumuṭiya nalla paḻaṅkaḷikkoṭukkum ceṭikaḷum, periya katirkaḷi īṉum tāṉiya vakikaḷum im muṟiyiṉālēyē peṟappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
6. paṭṭuppuḻu vaḷarttal
paṭṭu oru vakik kampaḷip puḻuviliruntu kiṭikkiṟatu. ciṟuvar peṇkaḷ āṇkaḷ aṇikiṉṟa aḻakiya paṭṭāṭikaḷ ellām kampaḷip puḻu taṉṉic cuṟṟip piṉṉum kūṭṭiliruntu kulittu eṭukkappaṭum nūliliruntu ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
toṭakkattil paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷ cīṉāvil vaḷarkkappaṭṭaṉa. āyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ veḷiyulakiluḷḷōr paṭṭu eṉṉa eṉṟum atu eppaṭik kiṭikkiṟateṉṟum aṟiyātiruntaṉar. cīṉā mikap paḻiya nāṭu. cīṉamakkaḷ mikap paḻaṅkālattilēyē nākarikamaṭintiruntārkaḷ. makkaḷ airōppiya nāṭukaḷil vilaṅkukaḷiṉ tōli uṭuttiya kālattil cīṉap peṇkaḷ viliyuyarnta paṭṭāṭikaḷi aṇintārkaḷ.
