ak kālattum pala nūṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉum paṭṭup puḻukkaḷi vaḷarkkum muṟi irakaciyamāka iruntu vantatu. paṭṭuppuḻu vaḷarppu cīṉa nāṭṭiṉ ariya celvamāka iruntu vantatu. paṭṭuppuḻu vaḷarttalil cakkaravarttikaḷum, cakkaravarttiṉikaḷum karuttuk koṇṭiruntārkaḷ. 4500 āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ vāḻnta cīliṅ eṉṉum cīṉac cakkaravarttiṉi taṉatu nērattiṉ pakutiyip paṭṭuppuḻu vaḷarppatil evvāṟu celaviṭ ṭāḷeṉap paḻiya cīṉa nūlkaḷ kūṟukiṉṟaṉa.
pala nūṟāṇṭukaḷākap paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷi vaḷarkkummuṟi maṟi vāka iruntu vantatu. airōppā paṭippaṭiyē nākarikamaṭintatu; airōppā vukkum kiḻakku nāṭukaḷukkumiṭiyil vāṇikam naṭippeṟṟatu. cīṉāviliruntu vāṅkappaṭum paṭṭunūliliruntu urōmi nāṭṭup peṇkaḷ aṇiyum aḻakiya āṭikaḷ neyyappaṭṭaṉa. āṉāl cīṉāvi allāta maṟṟiya nāṭukaḷukkup paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷip paṟṟi oṉṟum teriyāmaliruntatu. palar ivvirakaciyatti aṟiyamuyaṉṟu vantārkaḷ; avarkaḷāl aṟiyamuṭiyavilli. ki.pi. 550-l airōppiyar paṭṭu eṉpatu eṉṉa eṉṟu aṟintaṉar. piṉṉar irupāracīka tuṟavikaḷ ivvirakaciyattic cīṉāviliruntu aṟintu ceṉṟārkaḷ.
it tuṟavikaḷ cīṉāvil vāḻntapōtu paṭṭup puḻukkaḷiyum avaṟṟi vaḷarkkum muṟikaḷiyum paṟṟi evvakiyilō aṟintārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ paṭṭuppūcciyiṉ muṭṭikaḷi vittiruppati cīṉa makkaḷ aṟintiruntāl ivarkaḷ orupōtum veḷiyēṟi yirukkamāṭṭārkaḷ. ivarkaḷ cīṉāviviṭṭuk koṉsntāntiṉōppiḷukkuc ceṉṟapōtu cila paṭṭuppuḻu muṭṭikaḷit tamatu ūṉṟu kōlkaḷukkuḷ maṟittu vittukkoṇṭu ceṉṟārkaḷ. ivvāṟu 1400 āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ koṇṭu cellappaṭṭa muṭṭikaḷiṉiṉṟum perukiyaviyē iṉṟu mēṟku nāṭukaḷil paṭṭu uṟpattikkāka vaḷarkkappaṭum kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa paṭṭup puḻukkaḷākum.
airōppāvil paṭṭu uṟpatti evvāṟu toṭaṅkiṟṟu? paṭṭuppuḻuk kaḷi vaḷarppōr cīṉar ceytatu pōlavē immuṟiyip pala nūṟāṇṭukaḷ irakaciyamākak kāppāṟṟi vantaṉar. ippoḻutu paṭṭup puḻu vaḷarkkum muṟi irakaciyamaṉṟu. iṉṟu paṭṭuppuḻu vaḷarkka virumpum evarum avaṟṟi vaḷarttup paṭṭu uṟpatti ceytukoḷḷalām.
paṭṭuppūcciyiṉ muṭṭikaḷ cilavaṟṟi vāṅkiṉāl nāmē evvāṟu ammuyaṟciyit toṭaṅkavēṇṭumeṉap pārppōm. muṭṭi kaḷi vāṅkumuṉ aṟiyavēṇṭiya iraṇṭoru karumaṅkaḷuṇṭu. paṭṭup puḻukkaḷukku maṟṟiya ilikaḷilum pārkka mucukkaṭṭic ceṭi ili kaḷil viruppamatikam. paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷi vaḷarkkat toṭaṅkumuṉ vēṇṭiya pōtu ilikaḷ kiṭikkakkūṭiya mucukkaṭṭic ceṭiyik kaṇṭupiṭikka vēṇṭum. paṭṭuppūccikaḷiṉ muṭṭikaḷ mikap pāraṅ kuṟintavi. oru tāṉiya eṭiyil 100 muṭṭikaḷ variyiluṇṭu. oru iṟāttalil 700,000 variyilirukkum. ākavē nī oru tāṉiya eṭi allatu iraṇṭu tāṉiya eṭi muṭṭikaḷi vāṅkiṉāṟ pōtum.
iṉi vēṇṭappaṭuvatu muṭṭikaḷ porippataṟku vēṇṭiya peṭṭi. uṭuppu vikkum kaṭutāci aṭṭippeṭṭi pōtumāṉatu. muṭṭi kaḷi peṭṭiyiṉ aṭiyil parappi vittu avi porippati etirpārt tiruttal vēṇṭum. muṭṭikaḷ porippataṟku atika veppamillāta iṭam ēṟṟatu. muṭṭi porikkum nāḷ vantavuṭaṉ mucukkaṭṭic ceṭi niṟkum iṭattukkuc ceṉṟu pacci ilikaḷip paṟittu varavēṇṭum. muṭṭikaḷ eṭṭu allatu oṉpatu nāṭkaḷil porikkum.
ippoḻutu iṉṉum cila peṭṭikaḷ vēṇṭiyirukkum. porit tatum paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷ mika virivil vaḷarum. oru peṭṭiyil nūṟu puḻuk kaḷi viṭṭu vaḷarppatu kaṭiṉam. iḷam paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷ uṇṇumpaṭi peṭṭikaḷiṉ aṭiyil mucukkaṭṭi ilikaḷip parappi vikkavēṇ ṭum. muṭṭikaḷ porikka vikkappaṭṭuḷḷa peṭṭikaḷiṉaḷavuḷḷa taṭitta kākitattāḷkaḷ iṉi vēṇṭappaṭum. itil mikac ciṟiya tuḷikaḷ iṭavēṇṭum.
itu etaṟku eṉṟu aṟital vēṇṭum. porittatum puḻukkaḷil ciṟiya ōṭukaḷ oṭṭikkoṇṭirukkum. ittuḷiyiṭṭa kākitam avaṟṟip pōkkikkoḷḷa utavukiṉṟatu. muṭṭikaḷ porikkat toṭaṅkiyatum kākitatti muṭṭikkumēl ciṟitu iṭiveḷi niṟkumpaṭiyum veḷiccam mēlintu tuvāraṅkaḷ vaḻiyākak kīḻē cellum paṭiyum vittal vēṇṭum. ciṟiya paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷ muṭṭiyiliruntu veḷiyē vantatum mēlēyuḷḷa veḷiccattāl kavarappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. uṭaṉē avi melliya tuvāraṅkaḷ vaḻiyāka mēlē nakarntu celkiṉṟaṉa. appoḻutu tuvāraṅ kaḷiluḷḷa viḷimpukaḷ avaṟṟil oṭṭiyirukkum ōṭukaḷi uriñcip pōkkiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. tuvāraṅkaḷ vaḻiyākac ciṟiya puḻukkaḷ kāṇappaṭṭa tum avaṟṟip pacci mucukkaṭṭi ili parappiyuḷḷa putiya peṭṭi kaḷil viṭa vēṇṭum. ippuḻukkaḷ tiṉṉum aḷaviyum cuṟucuṟuppiyum kaṇṭu nām viyappaṭivōm.
paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷ ārōkkiyattōṭu iruntu nalla paṭṭit tara vēṇṭumeṉa virumpiṉāl nām mūṉṟu kāriyaṅkaḷi mutaṉmiyākak kavaṉittal vēṇṭum. iḷampuḻukkaḷukku atika iṭavacati aḷikkavēṇ ṭum. avi irukkum peṭṭikaḷic cuttamāka vittiruttal vēṇṭum. paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷ vaḷarum virivik kaṇṭu nām viyappaṭivōm. porittapōtu avi kālaṅkulap parumiyuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. ippoḻutu avi mutalāvatu tōl uriyak kūṭiya aḷavukkup paruttup putiya nari niṟattōluṭiyaṉavākak kāṇappaṭum. aṭutta iruvāraṅ kaḷil avi iṉṉum mūṉṟu muṟi tōl kaḻaṟṟip putiya tōl uṭi yaṉavāy iraṇṭaṅkulamaḷavu vaḷarntirukkum.
ivvaḷavu virivil vaḷarvataṟkup paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷukku atika uṇavu vēṇṭu meṉpatil viyappu oṉṟum illi. paṭṭuppuḻu kampaḷip puḻuvāka māṟum pōtu kuṟukiyirukkum. kampaḷip puḻukkaḷ iṟutiyil antuppūccikaḷāka māṟukiṉṟaṉa. kampaḷippūcci nilikkum antup pūcci nilikku miṭiyil kūṇṭuppuḻu nili oṉṟuṇṭu. kampaḷip pūcci niliyiliruntu antup pūcci niliyi aṭivataṟku ovvoṉṟukkum taṉittaṉi vīṭu vēṇṭum. ākavē avi akaṉṟa paṭṭuk kūṭukaḷip piṉṉattoṭaṅkum. paṭṭukkūṭu eṉpatu tām kampaḷip pūcci niliyi liruntu antu pūcci niliyi aṭivataṟkup paṭṭup puḻukkaḷ tammi mūṭikkaṭṭiya vīṭākum. ik kūṭukaḷiliruntu paṭṭuk kiṭikkiṟatu.
paṭṭuppuḻu paṭṭi iḻikkum vaki mika viyappāṉatu. ovvoru paṭṭup puḻuviṉ kīḻ utaṭṭilum iraṇṭu ciṟiya tuḷikaḷuṇṭu. puḻuviṉ uṭaluḷ oru vakip paccip poruḷ niṟinta iraṇṭu pikaḷuṇṭu nīḷamāṉa ap pacipporuḷ mañcaḷ niṟamuṭiyatākavum kaṇṇāṭipōl veḷiccattip pukaviṭakkūṭiyatākavumirukkum. kūṭu iḻikkum kālam neruṅkiyatum paṭṭuppuḻu uṇpati niṟuttiviṭukiṟatu. atu paciyit tuvāraṅkaḷ vaḻiyākac celuttukiṟatu. ippoḻutu mañcaṭ paciyil viyappāṉa māṟṟamuṇṭākiṉṟatu. paṭṭuppuḻuviṉuḷ irukkumpōtu atu oṭṭun taṉmiyuḷḷa nīrpporuḷāka viruntatu; kāṟṟōṭu campantappaṭum pōtu atu mañcaḷ niṟap paṭṭu nūlāka māṟukiṟatu. paṭṭuppuḻu ivviraṇ ṭiyum oṉṟākat tirittu vayiramaṭiyac ceykiṟatu. ivvaki nūliṉāl atu taṉatu vīṭṭik kaṭṭat toṭaṅkukiṟatu; nūlāl taṉatu uṭalic cuṟṟukiṟatu. nāṉku nāṭkaḷil paṭṭuk kūṭu muṭivaṭikiṟatu. oru paṭṭuk kūṭṭiluḷḷa nūlik kulittāl atu arimil nīḷa muṭiyatākum.
ippoḻutu nām cuṟucuṟuppāka irukka vēṇṭiya kālam varukiṉ ṟatu. kūṭukaḷiluḷḷa nūlik kulikka vēṇṭumeṉa virumpuvōm. paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷ kūṭukaḷi iḻittu muṭittatum avaṟṟi eṭuttu vennīril pōṭavēṇṭum. ic ceyalāl kūṭṭuppuḻukkaḷ iṟantuviṭum mayakka niliyiliruppatāl avi nōvi uṇaramāṭṭā. avaṟṟukku nōvuṇṭākumeṉṟu nām añca vēṇṭiyatilli.
nūlik kulippatu aṭutta vēliyākum. etiril vāyakaṉṟa pāttirattil kotikkum nīri vittukkoḷḷutal vēṇṭum. ataṟkuḷ āṟu kūṟukaḷip pōṭavēṇṭum. paṭṭu nūlik kulikkak kūṭiyatākac cūṭu paciyi iḷakac ceyyum. nūlkaḷiṉ antaṅkaḷik kaṇṭupiṭittu ivviraṇṭāka muṟukka vēṇṭum. ippoḻutu āṟunūlkaḷukkup patil mūṉṟu nūlkaḷirukkum. immūṉṟu nūlkaḷiyum oru nūlākumpaṭi tirittu oru kaṭṭiyiṟ cuṟṟa vēṇṭum. kaṭṭiyil cuṟṟum variyil kūṭukaḷ vennīril iruttal vēṇṭum.
nūlik kulippatu ilakuvāṉavēliyaṉṟu. oru veṟum nūṟ kaṭṭi, tuḷiyiṭṭa oru taṭi eṉpavaṟṟik koṇṭu muṟukkavum cuṟṟavum kūṭiya iyantiratti nām amittukkoḷḷalām.
nām iṉṉumorumuṟi paṭṭuppuḻukkaḷi vaḷarkka virumpiṉāl nām iraṇṭu tāṉiya eṭiya muṭṭikaḷi vāṅkalām; allatu paṭṭup pūccikaḷiliruntu muṭṭikaḷip peṟalām. paṭṭup pūccikaḷiliruntu muṭṭikaḷip peṟum muṟi mika viyappāṉatu. muṭṭikaḷip peṟa virumpiṉāl nām paṭṭuk kūṭukaḷ cilavaṟṟi vittiruttal vēṇṭum. avaṟṟi veppamuḷḷa iruṭṭaṟiyuḷ vikka vēṇṭum. iraṇṭu vārat tuḷ kūṭukaḷiliruntu antup pūccikaḷ veḷiyēvarum. veḷivantatum avaṟṟi mucukkaṭṭi iliyōṭu peṭṭiyil viṭavēṇṭum. avi paṟantu viṭumeṉṟu payappaṭavēṇṭiyatilli. avi paṟakkamāṭṭā. cilanāṭkaḷuḷ peṇ pūccikaḷ nāṉūṟu mutal ainnūṟu muṭṭikaḷ variyil iṭum. ippoḻutu namakku vēṇṭiya muṭṭikaḷ kiṭikkum. ivaṟṟuṭ cilavaṟṟi nām naṇparukkuk koṭukkalām. itaṉ irakaciyatti avarkaḷukkup paṭippippatu namakkup poḻutu pōkkāka virukkum.
paṭṭiṉ varalāṟu itu. paṭṭuk kūṭukaḷiliruntu ciṟitaḷavu nūl eṭuppatu pōlavē namatu paṭṭu uṭikaḷic ceyyum nūl eṭukkap paṭukiṉṟatu. paṭṭu eṭukkum muṟi mikap paḻiya kālattil iruntatu pōlavē iṉṟum iruntu varukiṟatu.
7. irappariṉ kati
kiṟistōpar kolampacip paṟṟi ellōrum kēḷvippaṭṭirukki ṟōm. ivarputiya ulaku eṉa vaḻaṅkum amerikkāvik kaṇṭupiṭittār.
ivar aṅkuḷḷa ciṟār viyappāṉa uruṇṭik kaṟuppup pantukaḷi vittu viḷiyāṭuvatik kaṇṭār; ciṟuvar avaṟṟi eṟintu ēntik koṇṭiruntaṉar. piṭikkat tavaṟiya pantu nilattil viḻuntu uyiruḷḷa poruḷ pōla mēle eḻuvati avar pārttār. uyiruḷḷa poruḷ eṉa niṉittu ataṉip piṭittup pārttapōtu atu tōlpōṉṟu vayira māṉa oru vakipporuḷāl ceyyappaṭṭiruppati aṟintār.
atu eṉṉavāyirukkalām eṉa aṟiya virumpiya kolampacu ki yiṉāl ciki ceytu atu eṉṉaveṉṟu avarkaḷik kēṭṭapiṉ atu marappāliṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa poruḷeṉavaṟintār. taṉatu nāṭṭukkut tirumpic ceṉṟatum avar itip paṟṟi maṟantuviṭṭār. kolampacu itaṉ payaṉkaḷippaṟṟi aṟintiruntārāyiṉ avar irapparik kaṇṭupiṭittava rākap pukaḻ peṟṟiruppār.
etirkālattil irappariṉ payaṉ evvāṟirukkum eṉpatip paṟṟik kolampacu aṟintirukkavilli. iṉṟu ulakam muḻumiyilum oru āṇṭil 800,000 toṉ irappar payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. kolampacu irapparp pantik kaṇṭataṟku munnūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉ irappar airōppāvukkuk koṇṭupōkappaṭṭatu.
tavaṟāka eḻutiya oru colli allatu oru kōṭṭi aḻippa taṟku nām irapparip payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟōm. airōppāvukku irappar koṇṭu cellappaṭṭapōtu mutalil itē ceyalukkāka atu payaṉ paṭuttappaṭṭatu.
iṉṟu irappar pala karumaṅkaḷukkup payaṉ paṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. cappāttukaḷ, tāḷkaḷ, caṭṭikaḷ, vennīrp puṭṭikaḷ, mōṭṭār vaṇṭi picikkiḷ vaṇṭic cakkaraṅkaḷukku māṭṭum vaḷiyaṅkaḷ, taṇṇīrk kuḻāykaḷ pōṉṟa pala poruḷkaḷ irapparāl ceyyappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. irappa rāl ceyyappaṭum poruḷkaḷip paṟṟi cintittup pārttāl atu vāṇikattōṭu toṭarpuḷḷa poruḷkaḷic ceyvatil evvaḷavu mutaṉmiyuṭiyateṉṟu namakkut tōṉṟum.
irappar eṉpatu oru vaki marattiṉ pāl eṉṟu amerikka makkaḷ kolampaciṭam kūṟiṉārkaḷ. irapparppālik koṭukkiṉṟa pala iṉa maraṅkaḷ uṇṭu. ippāl pārvikkup pacuviṉ pālippōṉṟu nīrt taṉmi yuṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭum. kāyaviṭumpōtu itu iḻupaṭakkūṭiya iṟukiya poruḷāka māṟukiṉṟatu. irappar ceyvateṉṟāl irappar marattik kāyappaṭuttip pāli eṭuttuk kāyaviṭutal eṉṟu nām niṉittal kūṭātu. oru kālattil payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa irappar muḻumiyum kāṭṭil vaḷarum irappar maraṅkaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭṭa pāliliruntu ceyyappaṭṭatu. āppirikkā, amerikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiṉ veppa maṇṭalaṅkaḷil vāḻum makkaḷ ippāli eṭuttu veḷḷiyarukku viṟṟārkaḷ. ippoḻutu avvakiyil peṟappaṭuvatu tēvikkup pōtiyataṉṟu. mōṭṭārc cakkaraṅkaḷukku māṭṭum vaḷiyaṅkaḷ, cappāttukaḷ mutaliya āyirakkaṇakkāṉa poruḷkaḷic ceyvataṟku vēṇṭiya irappar, payiriṭappaṭum maraṅkaḷiliruntu eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu.
palvaki maraṅkaḷ irapparp pālik koṭukkiṉṟaṉa vāyiṉum avaṟṟuḷ oruvaki marattiṉ pāl maṟṟa ellā marappālkaḷiviṭak kuṇattiṉāl ciṟantatu. immarattiliruntu mutal taramāṉa irappar eṭukkappaṭukiṟatu. immarattukku hēviyā(Hevea) eṉpatu peyar. itu pārā(Para) irappar maram eṉṟum aṟiyappaṭum. itu teṉṉamerikkāvi luḷḷa pirēcil kāṭukaḷil vaḷarkiṟatu. kāṭṭil vaḷarum pōtu itu atika pāl koṭukkamāṭṭātu. im marattiliruntu kiṭikkum irappar atika kuṇamuṭiyatāyiruntamiyiṉ im marattiṉ vitikaḷik kaṭalukkappāl koṇṭu ceṉṟu piriṭṭiṣārukkuc contamāṉa kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷil payiriṭa vēṇṭiyiruntatu. avviṭaṅkaḷil ivi naṉku vaḷarattakkaṉa. avaṟṟi at tēcaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭu celvatil oru taṭi ēṟpaṭṭatu.
ivvitikaḷ pirēcil nāṭṭiliruntu ilaṅki, malāyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukku aṉuppappaṭṭaṉa. in nāṭukaḷi aṭintatum vitikaḷ muḷikkamāṭṭātu iṟantuviṭṭaṉa. ivvitikaḷ ciṟitu kālattiṉpiṉ muḷikkamāṭṭā. pirēcil nāṭṭiṉiṉṟum kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷukkuk koṇṭupōka ākum kāla ellikku muṉ avi iṟantupōyiṉa. avvāṟāyiṉum im maraṅkaḷik kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷil uṇṭākkum tiṭṭam kiviṭappaṭavilli. cila vitikaḷi allatu kaṉṟukaḷi ilaṅki allatu malāyāvukku uyiruṭaṉ koṇṭu ceṉṟāl avi avviṭaṅkaḷil naṉku vaḷarum. kiyu(Kew) eṉṉum tāvarat tōṭṭatti luḷḷa aṉupavacālikaḷ ic cikkalāṉa vēliyil muyaṉṟārkaḷ. im marattiṉ vitikaḷ virivil iṅkilāntukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭṭu aṅku nāṟṟiṭappaṭṭaṉa.
vitikaḷ muḷittu vaḷarntaṉa. ik kaṉṟukaḷi uyiruṭaṉ kaṭalukkūṭākak kiḻakkut tēcaṅkaḷukkuk koṇaṭu ceṉṟāl aṅku putiya kittoḻil ēṟpaṭakkūṭiyatāka viruntatu. kaṉṟukaḷ koṇṭu pōkappaṭṭaṉa. avaṟṟuṭ cila piḻittaṉa.
ik kaṉṟukaḷiṉ vitikaḷiṉiṉṟu perukiya maraṅkaḷē ilaṅki malāyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil iṉṟum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. irappar maraṅkaḷ uṇṭākkappaṭṭapiṉ kiḻakku nāṭukaḷil nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉa milkaḷ vicālamuḷḷa irappart tōṭṭaṅkaḷ tōṉṟiṉa.
maraṅkaḷ āṟu vayataṭiyumpōtu pāl veṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. pāl veṭṭuvateṉpatu pālvaṭiyumpaṭi irappar marattiṉ paṭṭimītu oru vari ceyvatu. avvāṟu pāl veṭṭutaṟkut tiṟamiyum aṉupavamum vēṇṭum. cātāraṇa oruvaṉāl pāl veṭṭa muṭiyātu. avaṉ veṭṭi ṉāl veṭṭu atika āḻamillāmal allatu atika āḻamuṭiyatāka virukkum. veṭṭu kaṇakkāṉa āḻamuṭiyatāyiruttal vēṇṭum. veṭṭu āḻamillātiruntāl atika pāl vaṭiyātu. atika āḻamāka veṭṭiṉāl marattil kāyamuṇṭākum. ak kāyam orupōtum muṟṟāka āṟātu.
irappar marattil veṭṭuvateṉpatu oṭuṅkiya vari vaḻiyē pāl vaṭiyumpaṭi ataṉ paṭṭiyil carivāka veṭṭuvatu. ivvoṭuṅkiya vari marattiṉaṭiyiliruntu nilatti nōkkic celkiṉṟatu.
vēṟu varikaḷi veṭṭumpōtu avi nilatti nōkkic ceṉṟu pāl oḻukum periya variyi ēṟakkuṟi 45 pāti carivil cantikkum. pāl, pakkavarikaḷāl nilatti nōkkic cellum nērvarikkuc ceṉṟu aṅku vittirukkum kiṇṇattil viḻukiṟatu. marattil veṭṭappaṭukiṟa vari mītu paṭṭi vaḷarkiṟatu; veṭṭukaḷ mēvuṇṇukiṉṟaṉa. maṟupaṭiyum maṟupaṭiyum varikaḷ veṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. mēlum mēlum pāl vaṭikiṟatu.
pālveṭṭuvatip paṟṟi viḷaṅkikkoḷvataṟku nām irappar marattip paṟṟip potuvāka aṟintirukkavēṇṭum. marattiṉ veḷippuṟattiyum. paṭṭiyiyum paṟṟi ellārukkum teriyum. paṭṭi marattukkup pātukāppāṉa uṟi. marattukkuk kāyamuṇṭākāmal taṭuppataṟku marattukkum paṭṭikkumiṭiyil cavvu pōṉṟa melliya paṭṭiyuṇṭu. itu oru kākitat taṭippaḷaviṉatu. iccavvu pōṉṟa paṭṭi yiṉ uṭpakkattil maram vaḷarkiṟatu; mēṟpakkattil paṭṭi vaḷarkiṟatu.
ākavē, pālveṭṭukiṟavaṉ veḷip paṭṭiyiṉ aṭippākam variyil veṭṭavēṇṭum. ataṟkuk kīḻ veṭṭiṉāl paṭṭi vaḷarkiṉṟa uḷtōl veṭṭuppaṭum;kāyam orupōtum āṟātu. itupaṟṟiyē pālveṭṭuvatu nuṭpamāṉa vēliyeṉṟum atu kavaṉattōṭu ceyyappaṭavēṇṭumeṉṟum koḷḷappaṭum. vaḻakkamākak kāliyil pāl veṭṭappaṭukiṟatu. maṟṟa nēraṅkaḷilum pārkka appoḻutu pāl naṉṟāka vaṭiyum. pāl kiṇṇaṅkaḷil viḻuntavuṭaṉ atu vāḷikaḷil tiraṭṭappaṭṭut tōṭṭattiluḷḷa toṭṭikaḷukkuk koṇṭu pōkap paṭukiṟatu. nīrt taṇmiyuḷḷa ippāl toṭṭikkuḷ ūṟṟappaṭṭapiṉ oruvakik kāṭiviṭṭuk kalakkappaṭukiṟatu. ciṟitunērattil atu veṇmi yāṉa kaṭṭipōl uṟikiṉṟatu. atu tuṇṭukaḷāka veṭṭappaṭṭuk kaḻuvappaṭṭapiṉ uruḷikaḷil iṭṭu arikkappaṭukiṟatu. uruḷikaḷ ataṉi melliya kīlaṅkaḷākac ceykiṉṟaṉa. ik kīlaṅkaḷ kāyntapiṉ cippaṅkaḷākak kaṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉar ivi, ceyyappaṭum kittoḻiṟ poruḷkaḷukkēṟpap pakkuvañ ceyyappaṭṭu āyirak kaṇakkāṉa poruḷkaḷākac ceyyappaṭṭuc cantikaḷiṟceṉṟu viliyākiṉṟaṉa.
8. mikac ciṟiya paṟavikaḷ
ulakil mikac ciṟiya paṟaviyiṉ eṭi iraṇṭu kirāmum, nīḷam iraṇṭu aṅkulamum ākum. oru kirām eṉpatu 15 1/2 tāṉiya eṭi koṇṭa niṟi. ip paṟavi ūṅkārappaṟavi (Humming bird) iṉattic cērntatu.
ivviṉattil mikapperiya paṟaviyiṉ nīḷam 81/2 aṅkulam. ūṅkārap paṟavikaḷil ēṟakkuṟiya 750 iṉaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. ivviṉaṅkaḷ amerikkāvilē vaṭakkē alaskā mutal teṟkē pattakōṉiyā variyil kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi perumpālum atika pūkkaḷum atika veyilumuḷḷa iṭaṅkaḷil vāḻkiṉṟaṉa.
ip paṟavikaḷukku vāṉavilliṉ niṟaṅkaḷuṇṭu. ulakiluḷḷa ep paṟaviyum ūṅkārap paṟaviyiṉ aḻaki eḷitil tāṇṭamāṭṭātu. ūṅkārap paṟavi pūkkaḷiṭiyē paṟantu cellumpōtu irattiṉak kaṟkaḷ pōlavum eriveḷḷikaḷ pōlavum tōṉṟum. ivaṟṟiṉ iṟakkikaḷ acivatip pārkka muṭiyātu. ivi nāḷ muḻuvatum vāṉattil paṟantu pūccikaḷip piṭittukkoṇṭum pūkkaḷil tēṉik kuṭittukkoṇṭum tiriyum. avaṟṟukku nīṇṭa mūkkum nīṇṭa nākkumuṇṭu. ip paṟavikaḷāl piṉpuṟamākap paṟantu cellavum iyalum. ivvāṟu maṟṟap paṟavikaḷ ceyyamāṭṭā. ivi paṟantukoṇṭē pūviṉuḷ taliyi viṭṭu nākki ṉāl tēṉi uṟiñcum.
ip paṟaviyiṉ kūṭu aḻakāṉatu. itu ilikaḷic cilantivali kaḷāl poruttik kūṭukaṭṭi ataṉip pāciyiṉāl mūṭukiṉṟatu. kūṭu kiṇṇam pōṉṟa vaṭivuṭiyatāy marakkiḷikaḷiṉ kavarkaḷiṭiyē kāṇappaṭum. cila paṟavikaḷ kūṭukaḷik koṭikaḷiṉ taṇṭukaḷil niṟkum paṭiyākak kaṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi payaṟṟiṉ parumiyuṭiya iraṇṭu muṭṭikaḷi iṭum.
veppa nāṭukaḷil vāḻum ūṅkārappaṟavi pāṭum. pāṭumpōtu ataṟku uṟcākamum kaḷippum tōṉṟum. avaṟṟuṭ periya iṉaṅkaḷ pāṭa māṭṭā; kīcciṭṭu ōci ceyyum. ūṅkārappaṟavi taṉatu kūṭṭuk arukē varum periya paṟavikaḷiyum payamiṉṟit tākkum. itaṉāl paruntiyum tākkamuṭiyum. itu tōṭṭaṅkaḷil paṟantu cella virumpukiṉṟatu. vīṭṭilirukkum oruvar kiyil pūkkaḷi vittiruntālum vāyiṉ itaḻkaḷiṭiyē carkkariyi vittiruntālum itu payamiṉṟic ceṉṟu pūkkaḷiluḷḷa tēṉiyum itaḻkaḷiṭiyē uḷḷa carkkariyiyum uṇṇum.
oruvaki ūṅkārappaṟavikku vāḷ pōṉṟa mūkku uṇṭu. paṟaviyiṉ nīḷam nāṉku aṅkulam. ataṉ mūkkum itē nīḷamuṭiyatu. cēvaliṉ mūkku itaṉiṉum irumaṭaṅku nīḷamuṭiyatāka virukkum. ip paṟavikaḷiṉ mūkkukku eṭṭāmal oru pūvilum tēṉ irukka māṭṭātu. vēṟu iṉaṅkaḷukku itaṉilum ciṟiya mūkku uṇṭu. cila iṉattiṉ mūkku mēlnōkkiyum cila iṉattiṉ mūkkuk kīḻ nōkkiyum vaḷintirukkum. ūṅkārappaṟavikaḷil periyatu eṭṭu aṅkula nīḷa muṭiyatu. iṟakkikaḷiṉ kuṟukku nīḷam aintu allatu āṟu aṅkula aḷaviṉatu. atu pūviliruntu tēṉi uṟiñcumpōtu ataṉ vāl viciṟipōl virintu oṭuṅkuvatik kāṇalām.
yamēkka(Jamican) ūṅkārap paṟavikku vālilum pārkka nīḷamuḷḷa iraṇṭu iṟakukaḷ vāṟpakkamāka vaḷarntirukkum. avi ataṉ uṭaliṉum pārkka atika nīḷamāṉavi. cilavaṟṟukkuk kālil ceruppup pōl vaḷarnta veḷḷi iṟakukaḷ uṇṭu; cilavaṟṟukkuk kāliccuṟṟi iṟakukaḷ uṇṭu. cila paṟavikaḷiṉ taliyil toppipōla veṇṇiṟa iṟakukaḷ kāṇappaṭum. cilavaṟṟiṉ toṇṭiyil kuñcampōl iṟakukaḷ vaḷarntirukkum. cilavaṟṟiṉ taliyil alaṅkāramāṉa niṟaṅkaḷ tōṉṟum.
intiyā, etiyōppiyā, āstirēliyā mutaliya nāṭukaḷil ūṅkārappaṟavi eṉṟu vaḻaṅkum ciṟu paṟavikaḷ uṇṭu. ivi ūṅkārap paṟaviyiṉattic cērntaṉavalla. ivaṟṟiṉ uṇmiyāṉa peyar tēṉciṭṭu(Sunbird oir honey sucker) itaṉ āṭamparamāṉa niṟam merikka ciṟu paṟavikaḷiṉ niṟatti ottatu. itu pūviliruntu kuḻāy pōṉṟa nākki nīṭṭit tēṉi eṭukkum. ūṅkārappaṟavi ceyvatu pōlavē ituvum ilic carukukaḷiyum pāciyiyum koṇṭu kūṭukaṭṭukiṟatu; cilanti nūlkaḷik koṇṭum mayirkkuṭṭip puḻu uṇṭākkum nūlik koṇṭum ilikaḷip poruttukiṟatu; ilikaḷiṉ muṉikaḷi marat toṭu piṇittuk kūṭṭit toṅkaviṭukiṟatu. kūṭṭiṉ vāyil muṉpuṟat til irukkum. intiyāvil kāṇappaṭum iṉṉoru ciṟiya paṟavi tiyaṟkuruvi(Tailor bird) atu ilikaḷit tittuk kūṭuceyvatāl ataṟku ippeyar vaḻaṅkukiṉṟatu. atu marattil toṅkum ilikaḷi mūkkiṉāṟṟuḷittu mayirkkuṭṭippuḻuk kūṭṭiliruntu eṭukkumnūliṉāl tittu nūl kaḻaṉṟu viṭātapaṭi ataṉ taliyil oru muṭiccu iṭukiṉṟatu. tāṉ uḷḷē pōkavum varavum kūṭiyatāka veḷiviṭṭu ilikaḷit tikkiṟatu. uḷḷē kutirivāl mayir, tumpu pōṉṟavaṟṟi aṭukki vikkiṟatu.
āciyāviluḷḷa mikac ciṟiya paṟavi pūkkotti. (Flower pecker). itaṉeṭi ēḻu allatu eṭṭu kirām variyiluḷḷatu. itu tēṉ ciṭṭip pōlap pañcu, cilantivali mutaliyavaṟṟāl kūṭukaṭṭukiṟatu; marattiṉ mika uyaramāṉa kiḷikaḷil kūṭṭit toṅkaviṭukiṟatu. ākavē ataṉik kāṇpataritu. palākkoṭṭikkuruvi eṉa aṟiyappaṭum iṉṉoru paṟavi intiyā ilaṅki mutaliya iṭaṅkaḷil kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itaṉ parumi palākkoṭṭi yaḷaviṉatātalāl itu ippeyarāl aṟiyap paṭukiṟatu.
9. tēṉīkkaḷ kuḷavikaḷiṉ
viyappumikka vāḻkki
makkaḷ mikap paḻaṅkālam mutal tēṉīkkaḷiṉ payaṉi aṟintu avi cērttu vikkum tēṉi eṭuttu varukiṟārkaḷ. ekiptiyariṉ paḻaṅ kālac ciṟpaṅkaḷil tēṉīkkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kirēkkar tēṉīkkaḷukku melicā eṉṉum peyariṭṭu vaḻaṅkiṉar. kirēkka nūlāciriyarkaḷ palar tēṉīkkaḷip paṟṟi eḻutiyirukkiṉṟārkaḷ. tēṉīkkaḷip paṟṟi naṉku aṟintukoḷḷa muṭiyātapaṭiyāl avarkaḷ avaṟṟip paṟṟik kaṟpaṉik katikaḷ palavaṟṟip puṉintu kūṟiṉārkaḷ.
koṉṟu putikkappaṭṭa eruttu māṭṭiliruntu avi tōṉṟu kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu cilarum, iṟanta ciṅkattiṉuṭaliliruntu piṟakkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu vēṟu cilarum eḻutiyuḷḷaṉar.
18ām nūṟṟāṇṭiṉ iṟutiyil marālṭi (Maraldi) eṉpār kaṇṇāṭiyiṉāl oru tēṉkūṭu ceytār. ak kālam mutal tēṉīkkaḷ viyappumikka vāḻkki varalāṟu aṟiyappaṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.
tēṉīkkaḷum kuḷavikaḷum kūṭṭamāka vāḻum iyalpiṉa. nām ti mātattil oru tēṉ kūṭṭip pārttāl tēṉīkkaḷ atika cuṟucuṟuppuṭiya ṉavākak kāṇappaṭamāṭṭā. kuḷirkālattil avi ari nittiriyiṟ kiṭakkum. vēli ceyyum tēṉīkkaḷ iliyutir kālattil mārikālat tukku vēṇṭiya āyattaṅkaḷ purikiṉṟaṉa. uṇavic cērttu vittapiṉ avi tamatu kūṭṭik kuḷiriṉiṉṟu kāppataṟku vēli ceyyat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avi pūkkaḷiliruntu oruvaki oṭṭuntaṉmiyuḷḷa poruḷi eṭuttuc ciṟu uruṇṭikaḷākac ceytu kūṭṭiluḷḷa veḷi kaḷi aṭikkiṉṟaṉa. oṭṭum poruḷ iṟukit taṇṇīri uḷḷē viṭa māṭṭātu. tēṉīkkaḷ nīri veṟukkiṉṟaṉa.
ivvoṭṭumporuḷ iṉṉoru vakiyilum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭu kiṉṟatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil natti, kuḷavi, cuṇṭeli pōṉṟavi kūṭṭi ṉuḷ pukuntuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. cuṇṭeli pōṉṟavaṟṟiṉ uṭali appuṟap paṭuttuvataṟku avaṟṟukku valimi paṟṟātu. avvuṭali aḻukumpaṭi viṭṭāl tēṉkūṭṭiṉ cukātārattukkup paḻutuṇṭākum. ākavē avi cuṇṭeliyip paciyiṉāl mūṭukiṉṟaṉa. kuḷavi pōṉṟa ciṟiya uyir kaḷiṉ uṭali avi appuṟappaṭuttiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.
tēṉīkkaḷ vaṇṭukaḷi allatu kuḷavikaḷip pōl uṟaṅkik koṇṭirukka māṭṭā. oṉṟi oṉṟu piṭittukkoṇṭu kiṭakkum. tēṉīkkaḷ veppanili atikappaṭum pōtu kūṭṭiṉ ellāppakuti kaḷukkum paṟantu cellum. iṟantupōṉa tēṉīkkaḷiṉ uṭalkaḷ appuṟap paṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. veppanili ēṟṟatāyiruppiṉ peruntoki īkkaḷ ciṟitu tūram paṟantucellum. tēṉīkkaḷ mikac cuttamāṉavi. uṭal nala miṉṟi irukkumpōtum avi kūṭṭi aḻukkuppaṭuttamāṭṭā. māri kālattil ciṟitu tūram paṟappatāl uṭampil taṅkiyirukkum kaḻivup poruḷkaḷ veḷiyēṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa.
paṉittuḷi pūkkaḷiṉ muṟukki aviḻkkum kālattil avi cuṟucuṟuppaṭikiṉṟaṉa. vēli ceyyum īkkaḷ kūṭṭiṉ aḻukkic cuttañceytapiṉ pūkkaḷukkup paṟantu celkiṉṟaṉa;veyil nāṭkaḷil tūrac cella muyalkiṉṟaṉa; caṭutiyil peyyum maḻiyum. vīcum kāṟṟum avi paṟappataṟku iṭiyūṟu viḷikkiṉṟaṉa. tēṉīkkaḷ, kuḷavikaḷiṉ iṟakkikaḷ ciṟiyavi. ivi periya iṟakkikaḷuṭiya vaṇṇāttip pūccikaḷilum antup pūccikaḷilum pārkka uṟutiyākavum vēkamākavum paṟakkiṉṟaṉa. tēṉīkkaḷukku nāṉku iṟakkikaḷuṇṭu. iṟakkikaḷiṉ naṭuvil narampupōṉṟu melliya caṭṭaṅkaḷ uṇṭu. paṟakkumpōtu kāṟṟu nirampi avi iḻuttuk kaṭṭiya mēḷattiṉ vārkkaṭṭup pōṉṟirukkum. mēliṟakkiḷiraṇṭum kīḻiṟakkiḷōṭu cērntirukkum. kuḷavikaḷum tēṉīkkaḷum iṟakkikaḷi vēkamāka aṭittalāl virintu paṟakkiṉṟaṉa; pūntātiyum tēṉiyum eṭuttukkoṇṭu pārattōṭu kāṟṟukketirē eḷitiṟ paṟantu celkiṉṟaṉa. avi kūṭṭukkuḷ kāṟṟuc cellumpaṭi veyiṟ kālattil tamatu iṟakkikaḷi aṭittukkoṇṭirukkum. nām tēṉkūṭṭukkup pakkattē niṉṟāl oru vaki iriyum ōciyik kēṭkalām. oru niri tēṉīkkaḷ tēṉ kūṭṭiṉ vāyilil uṭpakkam tirumpiyiruntukoṇṭu vēkamāka iṟakkikaḷi aṭittu veppamāṉa kūṭṭukkuḷ kuḷirkāṟṟic celuttuvatāl ivvōci uṇṭākiṉṟatu.
kūṭṭukkuḷ yātu nikaḻkiṉṟatu? kūṭṭiṉuḷ irāṇi īyum, vēli ceyyum īkkaḷum, āṇ īkkaḷum irukkiṉṟaṉa. āṇṭiṉ perumpakuti yil irāṇi īyum, vēli ceyyum īkkaḷum aṅku kāṇappaṭum. kōṭi kālattil āṇ īkkaḷum kāṇappaṭum. irāṇi ī kūṭṭi āṭci purivatilli. atu muṭṭiyiṭum iyantiram pōla vēli ceykiṉṟatu. atu nāḷoṉṟukku irunūṟu muṭṭi vītam iraṇṭu mātaṅkaḷil 12000 muṭṭikaḷiṭum. vēli ceyyum īkkaḷ tēṉiyum pūttātiyum cērppatallāmal kūṭṭilum vēli ceykiṉṟaṉa.
tēṉīkkaḷ putiya iṭaṅkaḷukkuc cellumpōtu viyappāṉa tēṉ kūṭṭik kaṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. cila īkkaḷ maṟṟa īkkaḷ uṇpataṟkākat tēṉ cērkkiṉṟaṉa. irupattu nāṉku maṇi nērattil avi tēṉi meḻukāka māṟṟiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru iṟāttal meḻuki uṇṭākkuvataṟkup patiṉāṟu mutal irupatu iṟāttal tēṉ vēṇṭum. tēṉī vaḷarppōr kūṭṭukkuḷ meḻuki vikkiṉṟaṉar. itaṉāl tēṉīkkaḷ kūṭṭil atika tēṉiviṭa muṭikiṟatu. pala īkkaḷē muṟi muṟiyāka vēliceytu aṟikaḷik kaṭṭimuṭikkum. aṟikaḷ kaṭṭi muṭiya muṭiya ovvoru aṟiyum payaṉ paṭuttapaṭum. cēkarikkappaṭṭa tēṉoṭu vēli ceyyum īkkaḷ kūṭṭi ṉuḷ cellum. tēṉ nirappappaṭṭatum kūṭukaḷ aṭikkappaṭum. mārikāla uṇavukkākat tēṉ cēkarittu vikkappaṭukiṟatu. cila aṟikaḷil makarantap poṭi cēkarittu vikkappaṭum. itu iḷam puḻukkaḷukkuriya uṇavil oru pakutiyākum.
kīḻ aṟikaḷ kuñcu porikkum aṟikaḷ eṉappaṭum. avi iruvakiyiṉa. cila vēli ceyyum īkkaḷukkuriyavi; ciṟitu periya aṟikaḷ āṇ īkkaḷukkuriyaṉa. irāṇi ī ovvoru aṟiyilum ov voru muṭṭiyiṭukiṟatu. mūṉṟu nāṭkaḷiṉ piṉ avaṟṟiṉiṉṟu ciṟiya puḻukkaḷ veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi iḷam īkkaḷ eṭuttuc celkiṉ ṟaṉa. puḻukkaḷ vaḷarvatāl aintu nāṭkaḷil aṟikaḷ nirampiviṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi mēṟpārkkum īkkaḷ ciṟitu meḻukālum pūntātālum aṟiyi aṭittuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivvaṭipput tēṉaṟikaḷi aṭikkum aṭippup pōlallātu uḷ irukkum puḻukkaḷukkuk kāṟṟup paṭak kūṭiyatāka virukkum.
puḻukkaḷ iraṇṭuvāram aṟiyuḷ kiṭakkum. akkālattil viyap pāṉa māṟṟamuṇṭākiṟatu. mutalil utaviyaṟṟa niliyil kiṭanta puḻukkaḷ tammic cuṟṟiyirukkum kūṭṭiliruntu iṟakki muḷitta pūccikaḷāka veḷivarukiṉṟaṉa.
putiya īkkaḷ puḻukkaḷik kavaṉikkum īkkaḷāki avaṟṟip pātukākkiṉṟaṉa; piṉ vēli ceyyum īkkaḷ puriyum vēlikaḷip puri kiṉṟaṉa. īkkaḷ iṟakkikaḷuṭaṉ veḷivantatum vēli ceyyum īkkaḷ avvaṟikaḷic cuttañceykiṉṟaṉa. irāṇi ī avaṟṟuḷ ovvoru muṭṭiyiṭukiṟatu.
tēṉīkkaḷ ellām oru kūṭṭil irukkamuṭiyātapaṭi atikappaṭum kālam oṉṟu varukiṟatu. cila camayaṅkaḷil pōtiya muṭṭikaḷ iṭamuṭiyāta paṭi irāṇi ī kiḻam aṭikiṟatu. appoḻutu vēli ceyyum īkkaḷ, potuvāka muṭṭikaḷ porikkum aṟikaḷ pōlallātu periya cila aṟikaḷik kaṭṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi irāṇi īkkaḷiṉ aṟikaḷ eṉap paṭum. ivaṟṟuḷ porikkum puḻukkaḷ cātāraṇa tēṉīkkaḷāka māṟum puḻuk kaḷilum pārkka vēṟāṉaviyalla. ivaṟṟi vaḷarkkum īkkaḷ ivikkut tēṉum pūntātum kalanta oruvaki uṇavik koṭukkiṉṟaṉa.
irāṇi ī aṟikaḷ kaṭṭimuṭivataṉ muṉ paḻiya irāṇi īyik kūṭṭiṉiṉṟum eṭuttuviṭṭāl vēli ceyyum īkkaḷ cātāraṇa ī piṟakkum muṭṭiyiliruntu oru irāṇi īyi uṇṭākkikkoḷḷa muṭiyum. am muṭṭiyiliruntu uṇṭākum puḻukkaḷukkuc ciṟappāṉa muṟiyil uṇavaḷittalāl ivvāṟu uṇṭākiṉṟatu. kūṭṭil paḻiya irāṇi ī oṉṟu irukkumāyiṉ atu kōpaṅkoṇṭu iḷam irāṇi īyik kollamuyalum. vēlipuriyum īkkaḷ avvāṟu ceyvatit taṭuttuviṭum. appoḻutu atu cila īkkaḷuṭaṉ kūṭṭiviṭṭup putiya oru kūṭṭik kaṭṭac celkiṟatu.
oru nāḷilē pakal nērattil iḷam irāṇi ī ākāyattiṟ paṟantu celkiṉṟatu. ataṉit toṭarntu āṇīk kūṭṭamum paṟakkiṉṟatu. aṅku irāṇi ī avaṟṟil oṉṟic cērkiṟatu. piṉṉar atu kūṭṭukkut tirumpi vantu muṭṭiyiṭat toṭaṅkukiṟatu.
vaṇṭukaḷum kuḷavikaḷum tēṉīkkaḷip pōlallātu ovvoru āṇṭilum putiya kūṭukaṭṭip perukukiṉṟaṉa. kuḷavikkūṭu tēṉ kūṭṭip pōṉṟataṉṟu. atu marattic cappik kākitampōṟceyta marakkaḷiyiṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭuvatu. tēṉīkkaḷ kūṭṭiṉ pakkavaḻiyākak kūṭṭukkuḷ celkiṉ ṟaṉa. kuḷavikkūṭṭiṉ vāyil kīḻppakkattiluḷḷatu. mēṟpakkam taṭṭi yāka irukkum. nām tiṉamum paṭikkum ceytittāḷkaḷ marakkaḷiyiṉāṟ ceyyappaṭṭavi.
10. kākitattiṉ kati
makkaḷ ellōrum tamatu niṉivukaḷi eḻuti vikka virumpiṉārkaḷ.
nākarikamaṭiyāta mikap paḻaṅkālattil avarkaḷ tāmaṟintavum, vēṭṭiyāṭiyavum vilaṅkukaḷiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷit tām vāḻnta kuki kaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷil eḻutiṉārkaḷ. avaṟṟi nām iṉṟum kāṇalām.
āyiram āṇṭukaḷiṉ piṉṉar avarkaḷiṉ piṉṉōr ōraḷavu nākarikamaṭintaṉar. ippoḻutu avarkaḷ eḻuttu eḻutum muṟiyi aṟintu paṭaṅkaḷukkup patil eḻuttukkaḷi kaṟkaḷil veṭṭiṉārkaḷ. ivvāṟu veṭṭappaṭṭa paḻaṅkālak kuṟippukaḷ aracarkaḷ purinta ceyal kaḷip paṟṟiyavi. ak kālattil aracarē kavaṉikkappaṭat takkavarāka viruntaṉar. ivvāṟu kallil veṭṭappaṭṭa eḻuttukkaḷik koṇṭē nām paḻaṅkāla varalāṟukaḷi aṟikiṉṟōm.
kal nilikkak kūṭiya poruḷāyiṉum eḻutuvataṟku vāyppāṉa poruḷaṉṟu. ākavē pāpilōṉiya makkaḷ kalliviṭṭuk kaḷimaṇṇip payaṉpaṭuttiṉar. avarkaḷ kaḷimaṇṇāl taṭṭukaḷiyum uruḷikaḷi yum ceytu avaṟṟiṉ mītu eḻuttāṇiyāl eḻutiṉārkaḷ. kaḷimaṇ mirutuvāyiruntamiyiṉ kallilum pārkka itil virivākavum, ilaku vākavum, malivākavum eḻuttu vēli ceyyappaṭṭatu. eḻutteḻutap paṭṭa kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukaḷum uruḷikaḷum aṭuppil allatu veyilil vittu ularttappaṭṭaṉa.
kallileḻutuvatilum pārkka itu tiruntiya muṟiyākaviruntatu. ivvaki āyirakkaṇakkāṉa taṭṭukaḷum uruḷikaḷum iṉṟu variyum aḻiyātu nilipeṟṟuḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuṭ cila oruvar iṉṉoruvarukku eḻutiya kaṭitaṅkaḷ, cila nāṭkuṟippuk kaṇakkukaḷ, pala puttakaṅkaḷ, pāpilōṉ nāṭṭukkut talinakarāyirunta pāpilōṉ nakaril kaḷimaṇ taṭṭukaḷil eḻutappaṭṭa puttakaṅkaḷi makkaḷ iraval peṟṟuppaṭikkak kūṭiya periya puttakaniliyam iruntateṉa aṟiñar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
maṟṟiya nāṭṭiṉar vēṟu vakiyil tamatu kuṟippukaḷi vittirun taṉar. paḻiya ekiptiyar kallil eḻuttukkaḷi veṭṭiyatōṭu pappilacu eṉṉum tāḷilum eḻutiṉar. pappilacu eṉpatu nīla āṟṟaṅkariyil vaḷarum nāṇal taṇṭip piḷantu ceyyappaṭṭatu. meḻukiṉāṟ ceyyap paṭṭa takaṭum eḻutappayaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu.paḻiya urōmilum pappilacu payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. marappalakikaḷ mītu meḻukit taṭavi aḻuttañ ceytu ataṉmītum eḻuttāṇiyāl eḻutappaṭṭatu. pappilacut tāḷiviṭa eḻutuvataṟkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭa maṟṟiya poruḷkaḷ aḻakillāma liruntaṉa. meḻukil eḻutappaṭum eḻuttukaḷ virivil aḻintu pōyiṉa. piṉ paṭippaṭiyē tōl eḻutap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu. tōl iṉṟum mukkiya āvaṇaṅkaḷ eḻutuvataṟku mēṟku nāṭukaḷiṟ payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. pala nūṟu āṇṭukaḷāka itu eḻutuvataṟkēṟṟa mutaṉmiyāṉa poruḷāka iruntu vantatu. cila nūlkaḷ “pārc meṇṭ” eṉappaṭṭa ivvakit tōlil eḻutappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
eḻutuvataṟku ipporuḷkaḷip payaṉpaṭuttiyavarkaḷ tirupti yaṭiyavilli. ivvaṟṟilum pārkka nalla eḻutum poruḷ kaṇṭupiṭik kappaṭāviṭṭāl ceṉṟa cila nūṟṟāṇṭukaḷil acciṭappaṭṭa kōṭik kaṇakkāṉa nūlkaḷ veḷivantirukkamāṭṭā. acciṭutaṟku ēṟṟa tāḷ atikam kiṭiyāviṭṭāl in nūlkaḷil ciṟupakuti māttiram veḷivan tirukkum. tōl oṉṟu māttiram eḻutum, acciṭum poruḷākaviruntāl cila nūlkaḷ māttiram veḷivantirukkum; putiṉattāḷkaḷ tōṉṟiyirukka māṭṭā. ulakil naṭattappaṭum ceytittāḷkaḷ ellām tōlil acciṭap paṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu vittukkoṇṭāl iṉṟu oru āṭum uyirōṭu vāḻamuṭiyātu.
itaṉāl kākitam mika mukkiyamuṭiya poruḷ eṉa viḷaṅkukiṉ ṟatu. kākitam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭāviṭṭāl ulakam iṉṟirukkum niliyi viṭa vēṟuvakiyāka virukkum. kākitamum kākitattāl ceyyappaṭṭa puttakaṅkaḷum aṟiñar kaṇṭaṟinta aṟivukaḷiyum,kaṇṭupiṭitta putiyavikaḷiyum kāppāṟṟum karuvikaḷākavuḷḷaṉa. ulakil uḷḷa aṟivukaḷ ellām kākitattil kuṟikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa eṉṟu oruvaṉ kūṟalām. ik kuṟippukaḷ illāviṭil makkaḷiṉ putiya karuttukkaḷum kaṇṭupiṭippukaḷum maṟintuviṭum. kākitatti mutalil yār kaṇṭu piṭittār eṉap pārppōm. kākitattic ceyyak kaṇṭupiṭitta pukaḻ cīṉāvukkuriyatu. cīṉar ceyyak kaṇṭupiṭitta poruḷkaḷ piṟkālattil maṟu nāṭukaḷil potu upayōkattukkup payaṉpaṭṭaṉa. cīṉarē mutal mutal paṭṭuppuḻu vaḷarttup paṭṭu uṟpatti ceytaṉar; tēyiliyip payiriṭṭaṉar. puttakaṅkaḷi acciṭṭaṉar.
kiṟistu piṟappataṟku iru nūṟāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ kākitam ceyyak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ivvitti toḷāyiramāṇṭukaḷāka mika irakaciya mākak kāppāṟṟappaṭṭuvantatu. camarkantu(Samarkand) eṉṉum nāṭṭi veṉṟu kippaṟṟiya arāpiyar mītu cīṉar 751ām āṇṭu paṭiyeṭut taṉar; paṭiyeṭuppil cīṉar tōlviyuṟṟaṉar. ciṟipiṭikkappaṭṭa cīṉariṭa miruntu arāpiyar kākitam ceyyum muṟiyik kaṟṟaṉar.
ivvitti arāpiyariṭamiruntu airōppiya nāṭukaḷiṟ paraviṟṟu. 
caṇal, pañcu, tumpu pōṉṟa tāvaravakikaḷiliruntu kākitam uṇṭākkappaṭṭatu. kantit tuṇikaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. aṇmiyilēyē marakkūḻiliruntu kākitam ceyyappaṭukiṉṟatu. marakkūḻ ceyvataṟku ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉa maraṅkaḷ taṟittu viḻuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. oru periya ceytittāḷ niliyattil paṉṉiraṇṭu mātaṅkaḷukkuc ceytittāḷ acciṭuvataṟkuk kākitam koṭukka āṇṭil 31,300 ēkkar kāṭu veṭṭappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu.
cila nāṭukaḷil aḻikkappaṭum kāṭukaḷil putiya maraṅkaḷ naṭappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa. cila nāṭukaḷil kāṭukaḷ atikam. ettaṉi maraṅkaḷi veṭṭi aḻittālum kāṭu muṉṉiruntatu pōlavē irukkiṉṟatu. ivvaki nāṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟu kaṉaṭā. aṅku kāṭṭup pāti vaḻiyākap payaṇam ceytāl palavāraṅkaḷukku makkaḷik kāṇamuṭiyātu.
kākitam ceyvataṟkup payaṉpaṭuvatu ispuṟūcu (Spruce) eṉṉum maram. periya āṟukaḷiṉ ayaliluḷḷa maraṅkaḷ perumpālum taṟikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. ili tuḷirkālam variyil maraṅkaḷ aṅkē kiṭakkum. āṟu paṉikkaṭṭiyiṉṟi irukkum kālattil avi āṟṟiṉ vaḻiyē ālikkuk koṇṭupōkappaṭum.
marattik kūḻāka arikkum āliyil mutalil maraṅkaḷ nālaṭi nīḷamuḷḷa tuṇṭukaḷāka veṭṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. piṉṉaravi piṟitōr iyantirattil iṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iyantiram maraṅkaḷiṉ paṭṭiyi uriñci eṭuttuviṭukiṟatu. piṉṉar avi kumpamāka aṭukki vikkap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. marakkūḻ iraṇṭu vakiyākac ceyyappaṭukiṉṟatu. maraṅkaḷit tuṇṭu tuṇṭukaḷāka uṭittut tuṇṭukaḷi oruvaki iracāyaṉap poruḷōṭu kalantu kūḻākum variyil aṭuppil vittu erippatu oru muṟi. itu iracāyaṉa muṟiyeṉappaṭum. marakkaṭṭikaḷip periya kaṟcakkaraṅkaḷukkiṭiyiliṭṭu eṭṭiloru aṅkulap parumaṉuḷḷa ciṟu cimpukaḷākumpaṭi aritum vēṟu pala muṟikaḷālum kūḻākkuvatu iṉṉoru muṟi.
innilimiyil marakkūḻ kākitam ceyyum paṭi ulakam muḻumikkum aṉuppappaṭukiṟatu. piṉpu itu pala iyantiraṅkaḷ vaḻiyākac ceṉṟu kākitamāka māṟukiṉṟatu. nām kāliyil atika āvalōṭu paṭik kum ceytit tāḷkaḷ ivvakiyil kiṭittaviyeṉa aṟiyumpōtu nām mika viyappu eytuvōm.
11. cīṉapperuñcuvar
cīṉāviluḷḷa peruñ cuvarip pattāyiram mila nīḷamuḷḷa cuvar eṉṟu cīṉar kūṟuvar. iccuvar maṉitaṉāl ceyyappaṭṭa viyappumikka vēlikaḷuḷ oṉṟu.
itupōṉṟa periya cuvar ulakam muḻumiyilum illi. iccuvar uṇmiyil patiṉāyiram mil nīḷamuṭiyataṉṟu. itaṉ nīḷam ēṟak kuṟiya 1500 mil.
iccuvar ēṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭatu? cīṉappēraracaṉ oruvaṉ vaṭapuṟattil tāttāriyariṉ paṭiyeṭuppit taṭuppataṟkāka oru niri kōṭṭikaḷik kaṭṭi avaṟṟil pōrvīrari niṟutta niṉittāṉ. ellākkōṭṭi kaḷilum vīrari niṟuttātapaṭiyāl tāttāriyap paṭiyayeṭuppit taṭukkac cīṉarāl muṭiyavilli.
cih-vāṅti eṉṉum pēraracaṉ ki.mu. 221il cimmācaṉamēṟiṉāṉ. itu kiṟittu nātar piṟappataṟku iru nūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ. iccīṉap pēraracaṉ mikap pukaḻ peṟṟavaṉ. ivaṉuṭiya araṇmaṉi maṇṭapam pattāyiram pēr irukkakkūṭiya vicālamuṭiyatāka viruntatu. pattāyiram makkaḷ irukkakkūṭiya maṇṭapameṉpatu avaṉukkuc ciṟu kāriyamāka viruntatu. avaṉ ki.mu.214il vaṭakkuc ciṉāviliruntu tipettu elli variyil cellum cuvarik kaṭṭa niṉittāṉ.
iccuvarik kaṭṭuvataṟkuc cīṉā muḻumiyilum vāḻnta āṇṭu kaḷil mūṉṟiloru pakutiyiṉar vēliyāṭkaḷākap piṭikkappaṭṭaṉar. taṇṭaṉi vitikkappaṭṭa kuṟṟavāḷikaḷum cuvar kaṭṭuvataṟku aṉuppap paṭṭārkaḷ. aracaṉ puttakaṅkaḷi veṟuttāṉ. ellāpputtakacālikaḷum tīkkiriyākkappaṭa vēṇṭumeṉṟum, avaṟṟi vittiruppōr cuvar kaṭṭutaṟku aṉuppappaṭutal vēṇṭumeṉṟum avaṉ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ.
ak kālattil periya nāṭṭi āḷvataṟku cih-vāṅti pōṉṟa palavāṉ vēṇṭappaṭṭāṉ. cuvar kaṭṭuvōril kaṭami ceyya muṭiyāta varkaḷum palamaṟṟavarkaḷumirukkavilli. vēliyāṭkaḷiṉ mutukil cavukkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. kaṇkāṇikaḷ avarkaḷ mītu tamatu pārvi yic celuttiṉārkaḷ. cumiyi iḻukka allatu cumakkamāṭṭātavaṉ koṉṟu attipārattil pōṭappaṭṭāṉ. ivvāṟu kollappaṭṭa ettaṉi āyiram pēr cuvariṉ kīḻ kiṭakkiṟārkaḷeṉpatu evarukkum teriyātu. iṉṟum iccuvar ulakiluḷḷa periya pirētakkuḻi eṉṟu collappaṭukiṉṟatu.
iraṇṭāyiramāṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ kaṭṭappaṭṭa iccuvar iṉṟum ulaka viyappukkaḷil oṉṟāka viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. cuvar kaṭṭa vēṇṭiya pala iṭaṅ kaḷil orē muṟiyil vēli toṭaṅkappaṭṭatu. cuvariṉ perumpakuti karuṅkaṟpāḷaṅkaḷiṉāṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. ataṉmēl kaḷimaṇ kaṟkaḷ variciyāka 15aṭi mutal 30 aṭi uyarattukku aṭukkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cuvariṉ aṭippapakuti 25 aṭik kaṉamuṭiyatu. mēlē cellac cella ciṟitu ciṟitākak kaṉaṅkuṟintu cellum ataṉ tali 15 aṭi kaṉamuṭiyatu. apāyamāṉa iṭaṅkaḷil ovvoru munnūṟu aṭikku oṉṟum maṟu iṭaṅ kaḷil 600 aṭikku oṉṟumākac ciṟukōṭṭikaḷum kāvaṟkōpuraṅkaḷum kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. matiliṉ oru puṟattiliruntu maṟupuṟam variyil 40,000 kōṭṭikaḷum kōpuraṅkaḷumuḷḷaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ uyaram 40 aṭi mutal 50 aṭi variyil. āpattuk kālaṅkaḷil pōrvīrar cuvarkaḷ mītu niṉṟu pōriṭṭu etirikaḷit turattiṉārkaḷ.
immatilik kaṭṭi muṭippataṟku evvaḷavu maṇṇum kallum karuṅkaṟpāḷaṅkaḷum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭumeṉa niṉittu oruvar viyappaṭiyalām. cīṉar mutalil kōpuraṅkaḷik kaṭṭip piṉ avaṟṟi matilkaḷāl iṇittārkaḷ eṉṟu cilar kūṟukiṉṟaṉar.
vaṉāntaraṅkaḷ, malikaḷ, paḷḷattākkukaḷ, āṟukaḷ, eṉpavaṟṟiṉ mēlāka iccuvarik kaṭṭavēṇṭiyiruntatu. kaṭṭaṭa vēliyāḷar camāḷikka vēṇṭiya pala tollikaḷ iruntaṉa. vaṉāntaraṅkaḷuk kūṭākac cuvar cellumpōtu maṇal mikka tolli viḷittatu. av viṭaṅkaḷil kāṟṟōṭu vanta maṇal kuvintatu. ākavē cuvar maṟintu pōkātiruk kumpaṭi cila iṭaṅkaḷil oṉṟiṉpiṉ oṉṟāka mūṉṟu cuvarkaḷ kaṭṭappaṭ ṭaṉa. nila aḷavikkārar kiṭṭiya vaḻiyik kāṇpataṟkup pirayācappaṭa villi. vaḻiyil oru maliniṉṟāl malikku mēlāl cuvar ceṉṟatu. pāṟikaḷil muppataṭi akalattukku akaḻkaḷ veṭṭi avaṟṟi ṉiṭiyē cuvar eḻuppappaṭṭatu. ivvāṟu kaṭṭappaṭṭa cila iṭaṅkaḷ kaṭal maṭṭattukku mēl 4000 aṭiyiluḷḷaṉa.
cila iṭaṅkaḷil malip pakkaṅkaḷiliruntu iruṇṭa paḷḷattākku vaḻiyāka matil celkiṉṟatu. pakivar orupōtum vara muṭiyāta ivvakip paḷḷattākkukaḷil ēṉ cuvar kaṭṭappaṭṭateṉṟu niṉittu oruvar viyap paṭiyalām. cīṉa irācciyattiṉ vaṭavelli matilāl kākkap paṭa vēṇṭumeṉa aracaṉ kaṭṭaḷiyiṭṭāṉ. aṟakkaṭṭaḷi evvakiyilum niṟivēṟṟappaṭavēṇṭiyiruntatu. ak kālattil vēliyāṭkaḷ mikap palar iruntaṉar. oruvaṉ virumpiṉālum virumpāviṭṭālum kaṭṭa ḷikkup paṇintu matil kaṭṭac ceṉṟāṉ. avaṉ iṟantupōkum vari yil avaṉukku vēli yiruntatu.
im matil cih-vāṅtiyāl ki.mu.214il toṭakkappaṭṭatu. cuvar kaṭṭi muṭivataṟku nīṇṭakālattiṉ muṉ ivvaracaṉ iṟantāṉ. ivaṉukkup piṉ vanta aracar ataṉik kaṭṭi muṭittaṉar. kaṭṭimuṭikkappaṭṭa piṉ immatil periya vēlippāṭākak karutappaṭṭiruttal vēṇṭum. cuvariṉ ucciyil pōr vīrar pavaṉi ceṉṟaṉar. āpattuk kālaṅkaḷil kōpuraṅkaḷ mītu neruppu erittu apāyam aṟivikkappaṭṭatu.
ippoḻutu immatilkaḷukkūṭākap pukivaṇṭip pātikaḷum vītikaḷum celkiṉṟaṉa. ippoḻutu atu araṇākap payaṉpaṭavilli; palaviṭaṅkaḷ aḻintu kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa.
12. kāntattiṉ viyappu
pala nūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ viññāṉi oruvar āciyā miṉarilē māknēciyā eṉṉumiṭattil pāramāṉa kaṟuppukkal oṉṟik kaṇṭeṭut tār. atu ciṟiya irumput tuṇṭukaḷi iḻukkum taṉmiyuṭiyatāka viruntatu.
ik kallik kaṇṭupiṭittavar yār eṉṟu oruvarum aṟiyār. urōmarum kirēkkarum ik kallip paṟṟi naṉku aṟintiruntārkaḷ. itu māknēciyaṉ kal allatu māknēs eṉappaṭṭatu. ip peyarkaḷiṉṟē mākneṭ eṉṉum peyar vantatu. āṅkilam pēcappaṭum nāṭukaḷil ik kal ippeyarāl aṟiyappaṭum.
kāntakkal eṉpatu uṇmiyiṟ kallaṉṟu; oruvaki irumpumaṇ tātu(Iron ore) atu ippoḻutu ulakiṉ pala pākaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭikkiṟatu. cuvīṭiṉ nāṭṭil kiṭikkum kāntak kallil kavarum āṟṟal atikamuṇṭu. itu kaṭaliṟ cellum kappalkaḷiluḷḷa ticiyaṟi karuviyiṉ ūciyi aciyac ceykiṉṟatu. kāntakkallukku iṉṉoru peyar ‘ulōṭ sṭōṉ” (Lodestone) vaḻikāṭṭum kal eṉpatu itaṉ poruḷ. ulaki luḷḷa iyaṟkik kāntak kaṟkaḷ ellām ivvulōkattālaṉavi. 
kāntakkalliṉ kavarumāṟṟal ataṉ iraṇṭu muṉikaḷilumuḷḷatu. kāntak kal oṉṟi irumput tūḷukkuḷ vittu eṭuttāl ataṉ iru muṉikaḷilum tūḷ oṭṭikkoṇṭiruppatiyum naṭuvil irumput tūḷ illātiruppatiyum kāṇalām. tūḷ oṭṭātirukkum veḷi kalliṉ accu eṉavum tūḷ oṭṭiyirukkum muṉikaḷ turuvaṅkaḷ eṉavum peyar peṟum.
kāntameṉpatu oru tuṇṭu kāntak kal allatu ceyaṟki muṟiyil kāntamūṭṭappaṭṭa nēriya urukkut tuṇṭu. ceyaṟki muṟi yil kāntamūṭṭappaṭṭa urukkut tuṇṭiṉ iru muṉikaḷum kavarumāṟṟa luṭiyaṉa. kāntattuṇṭu oṉṟi oru nūliṟ kaṭṭi antarattil toṅka viṭṭāl ataṉ muṉikaḷ vaṭakkiyum teṟkiyum nōkki niṟkum. vaṭakkunōkki niṟkum muṉi vaṭaturuvam eṉṟum, teṟku nōkki niṟkum muṉi teṉturuvam eṉavum peyarpeṟum.
muṉikaḷil oṉṟu vaṭakkilum oṉṟu teṟkilum tirumpi niṟkum kāntat tuṇṭit ticiyaṟi karuvi eṉṟu nām kūṟukiṉṟōm. atu uṇmiyē. ticiyaṟikaruvi eṉpatu cāyttuvittāl ūci ellāt ticikaḷukkum tirumpak kūṭiyatāka amikkappaṭṭa oru karuvi. evvāṟu āṭṭi acittālum ūciyiṉ oru muṉi eppoḻutum vaṭakku nōkkiyum maṟṟa muṉi teṟku nōkkiyum niṟkum.
kāntattiṉ ivviyalpu makkaḷukku aḷavukaṭanta payaṉaḷikkiṉṟatu. atu kappalōṭṭukiṟavaṉukkuk kaṭalukkūṭāka vaḻiyik kāṭṭa utavu kiṉṟatu. ticiyaṟi karuviyi vittukkoṇṭu oruvaṉ vaṉāntaraṅkaḷi ṉūṭāka allatu nīr uṟintu kiṭakkum teṉ turuvap pakutikaḷiṉūṭāka allatu peruṅkaṭaliṉūṭākac ceṉṟāl avaṉāl tāṉ niṉitta iṭattukkuc ceṉṟu tirumpa muṭiyum.
kāntattiṉ iyalpu ivvāṟiruppataṟkuk kāraṇatti evarum aṟiyār ceyaṟkik kāntam eṉak kūṟiṉōm. ceyaṟkik kāntatti iraṇṭu vakiyiṟ ceyyalām. miṉ ōṭṭattip (Electric current) pāyccic ceyvatu oru vaki. urukkuttuṇṭu oṉṟi eṭuttuk kāntakkallōṭu uriñcutalālum kāntamuṇṭākum.
urukkuttuṇṭik kāntamākkutaṟkuk kāntakkallil urukkut tuṇṭi vittu orē ticiyi nōkkip palamuṟi aḻutti iḻuttal vēṇṭum; muṉṉum piṉṉumāka iḻuttal kūṭātu. itaṉāl urukku eppaṭik kāntamākiṉṟatu? irumpu urukku, maram, uroṭṭi pōṉṟa ellāp poruḷkaḷum ciṟiya aṇukkaḷiṉ cērkkiyiṉālāṉavi. poruḷkaḷiṉ taṉmikaḷukkēṟpa avi oṉṟi oṉṟu iṟukkamākavum taḷarvākavum piṭittukkoṇṭirukkiṉṟaṉa. cātāraṇa kālaṅkaḷil icciṟiya aṇukkaḷiṉ muṉikaḷ orē pakkamākat tirumpiyirātu aṅkumiṅku mākat tirumpik kuḻappamākak kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. urukkil aṇukkaḷ ivvāṟu iruttalāl atil kāntamillātirukkiṉṟatu. kāntakkalliṉ mītu oru ticiyi nōkki urukki uriñcumpōtu aṇukkaḷiṉ muṉikaḷ ellām orē mukamākat tirumpi viṭukiṉṟaṉa. urukkiṉ ovvoru aṇu vum ciṟiya kāntamākum. aṇukkaḷiṉ vaṭaturuvam vaṭakkilum teṉ turuvam teṟkilumākat tirumpiyavuṭaṉ urukkuk kāntamāka māṟukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu ceyyappaṭum kāntam ticiyaṟikaruvi vakiyil allatu maṟuvakikaḷil payaṉpaṭuvatilli. miṉ ōṭṭattiṉ mūlam ceyyappaṭum kāntam aṟiyappaṭuvataṉ muṉ kāntattiṉ muḻuppayaṉiyum makkaḷ peṟa villi.
miṉ ōṭṭattiṉāl ceyyappaṭum kāntam periya kaṇṭupiṭip pākum. itu potuvāṉa kāntatti viṭa mikap payaṉuṭiyatu. vīṭṭil eriyum viḷakki eriyavum taṇiyavum ceyvatupōla nām ataṉi vēṇṭāta pōtu niṟuttiviṭalām. ivvakik kāntam miṉ (Electrictiy) āṟṟalik koṇṭu iyaṅkukiṟatu. ivvāṟṟal ceppuk kampiyiṉāl cuṟṟappaṭṭa irumpiliruntu varukiṉṟatu. ik kampi vaḻiyāka miṉōṭṭam celkiṉṟatu. miṉōṭṭam cellumpōtu irumpu kāntamāka māṟukiṉṟatu. miṉōṭṭatti niṟuttiyatum atu kāntat taṉmiyi iḻantuviṭukiṉṟatu; kānta āṟṟalip peṟṟu atu piṭittirunta irumpum nilattil viḻuntu viṭukiṟatu.
miṉ kāntattiṉ eṇṇillāta payaṉkaḷi virittuk kūṟiṉ atu mikap perukum. itaṉutaviyiṉāl palavaki iyantiraṅkaḷ ceyyak kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. nām pēcum paṭak kāṭcikkuceṉṟu miṉkaruvi (Electroorgan) yiliruntu varum toṉiyik kēṭkiṟōm. miṉkāntam illāviṭiṉ irēṭiyōk karuvi, telipōṉ. kāntacaktiyiṉāl iyaṅkum iyantiraṅkaḷ mutaliyaṉa ceyyappaṭṭirukkamāṭṭā.
miṉkāntam eppaṭi vēliceykiṉṟa teṉṟa ṟivataṟku ikkāla irumput toḻiṟcāli oṉṟukku nām pōkavēṇṭum. aṅku nām kaṭiyil vāṅkakkūṭiya kutiri ilāṭam vaṭivāṉa ciṟiya kāntaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭum eṉṟu nām niṉittal kūṭātu. nām oru miṉkāntattik kāṭṭumpaṭi kēṭṭāl ēṟakkuṟiya āṟaṭi kuṟukkaḷavuḷḷa periya vaṭṭa irumpu oṉṟu kāṭṭappaṭum. itu kīḻē tāḻttavum uyarttavum kūṭiyatākap pārantūkki (Crane) oṉṟil māṭṭappaṭṭirukkum.
ivvakik kāntaṅkaḷ pala tēvikaḷiṉ poruṭṭup payaṉ paṭuttappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. toḻiṟcālikaḷiṉ ōr iṭattiliruntu iṉṉoru iṭattukkup periya irumput takaṭukaḷik koṇṭu pōka vēṇṭiyiruk kum. nām pārttuk koṇṭu niṟkumpōtē tūkkavēṇṭiya takaṭṭi muṭṭumvariyil periya kāntam iṟakkappaṭum. kāntattic cuṟṟiyirukkum kampikaḷil ituvariyum kāntam celuttappaṭavilli. irumpu kānta millāmalirukkiṉṟatu. vēliceypavaṉ oru muḷiyi (cuvicci) iḻuk kiṟāṉ. miṉōṭṭam kampikaḷ vaḻiyē celkiṉṟatu. uṭaṉē periya irumpu kāntacakti aṭikiṉṟatu. pārantūkkiyil vēli ceypavaṉ kāntatti mēlē uyarttumpōtu periya takaṭum atōṭu mēlē eḻukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu irumput takaṭukaḷ oru iṭattiliruntu maṟu iṭattukkuk koṇṭu cellappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. miṉ ōṭṭatti niṟuttiyatum periya irumpu miṉ caktiyi iḻantu viṭukiṟatu. piṉṉar maṟu cumiyit tūkkuvataṟku atu koṇṭu cellappaṭukiṟatu.
paḻiya pāyilarkaḷiyum iyantiraṅkaḷiyum uṭippataṟkum kāntam payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivaṟṟi uṭikkum iṭaṅkaḷukkuc ceṉṟāl payaṅkaramāṉa periya cattattiṉāl namatu kātu aṭittuviṭum. ivaṟṟi uṭippatu ēṟakkuṟiya āṟutoṉ pāramuḷḷa periya urukkuk kuṇṭākum. paḻiya irumpi uṭippataṟkum ikkuṇṭu payaṉpaṭuttap paṭukiṟatu. irumpi oru kumpamākac cērttu vittu valuvuḷḷa miṉ kāntattiṉāl kuṇṭi uyarattūkki ataṉic caṭutiyil viḻaviṭuvatāl irumpukaḷ uṭintupōkiṉṟaṉa. paḻiya muṟiyil irumpi uṭippatilum pārkka itu ilakuvāṉa muṟiyākum.
miṉkāntam tūkkakkūṭiya pārattōṭu āṟutoṉ kuṇṭiṉ pāratti oppiṭiṉ atu ciṟiyatākum. āṟaṭik kuṟukkaḷavuḷḷa miṉkāntam muppatu toṉ pāramuḷḷa irumpi orē muṟiyil koḷuvi caṅkili kayiṟu pōṉṟa utavikaḷiṉṟit tūkkum. kāntam eṉṉum kaṇṇukkup pulappaṭāta itaṉ āṟṟal ivvakiyiṉatu.
kaṇṇil ēṟiya irumput tuṇṭukaḷi eṭuppataṟkuk kaṇ vittiya cālikaḷilum miṉkāntam payaṉpaṭuttappaṭukiṉṟatu. muṟkālat til ivvaki irumput tuṇṭukaḷ kaṇṇiliruntu piṭuṅki eṭukkappaṭ ṭaṉa. ippoḻutu ivvāṟu kaṇṇil irumput tuṇṭu ēṟiya oruvaṉ mutalutavi peṟavarumpōtu avaṉ miṉkāntattukku muṉṉāl nāṟkāli yil iruttappaṭukiṟāṉ. kāntattiṉ muṉi kāyampaṭṭa kaṇṇukku nērākap piṭikkappaṭṭapiṉ ataṟku miṉ pāyccappaṭukiṉṟatu. kāntam ulōkat tuṇṭi iḻuttuviṭukiṟatu.
13. paṟavik kuñcukaḷiṉ paḷḷikkūṭam
paṟavik kuñcukaḷiṉ vāḻkki mika viyappuṭiyatu. avi muṭṭiyiliruntu veḷiyē vantatu mutal tām tammip pātukākkattakka niliyaṭintu tamatu tāy tantikaḷi viṭumvari avaṟṟiṉ vāḻkki yil viyappāṉa ceyalkaḷ nikaḻkiṉṟaṉa. maṉitak kuḻantikaḷip pōlavē avi payilavēṇṭiya mukkiya pāṭaṅkaḷuṇṭu. ap pāṭaṅkaḷuṭ cila tāy tantikaḷāl kaṟpikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. cilavaṟṟi avi tamatu tiṉa vāḻkki nikaḻccikaḷikkoṇṭu aṟintukoḷḷukiṉṟaṉa. avi viḷiyāṭum nēramum vēliceyyum nēramum uṇṭu. avi kāṭukaḷil tappip piḻittu uyirvāḻum vāḻkkikku āyattam ceyyavēṇṭum. avaṟṟukku viḷiyāṭik kaḷippataṟku ēṟṟa nēraṅkaḷum uṇṭu.
mutalmutal paṟavikkuñcukaḷ paṭikkavēṇṭiyatu tamatu tāy tantikaḷ ceyyum vevvēṟu ōcikaḷiṉ poruḷi viḷakkikkoḷḷa vēṇṭiyatu. avi ceyyum apāyac cattattik kēṭṭavuṭaṉ ataṟkuk kīḻppaṭiyavēṇṭiyatu mutaṉmiyāṉatu. paṟakkamāṭṭātu kūṭṭilirukkum kuñcukaḷ apāyam vilakum variyil patuṅkikkoṇṭu aciyātum amitiyākavum iruttal vēṇṭum.
porittavuṭaṉ nilattil ōṭakkūṭiya ciṟiya paṟavikaḷ apā yattik kuṟittu atika kavaṉam celuttavēṇṭum. oru kūṭṭam kavutārik kuñcukaḷ tāyiṉ piṉṉāl ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkumpōtu tāy taṉatu ōr iṟakki muṟintuviṭṭatupōl nilattil ariyum paṭi ataṉit toṅka viṭṭukkoṇṭu taḷḷāṭi naṭantu celkiṟatu. uṭaṉē kuñcukaḷ ivvapāya aṟivippukkuk kīḻppaṭi vēṇṭumeṉṟu iyalpūkkattiṉāl uṇarntu ayan layuḷḷa putaril ōṭippatuṅkukiṉṟaṉa. aṅku avi aciviṉṟiyum amitiyuṭaṉum kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟiṉ niṟam cūḻniliyōṭu cērntatā yiruppatāl avi kāṇamuṭiyātaṉa vāyirukkiṉṟaṉa. taṉatu kuñcukaḷ pātukāppāka irukkiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu aṟintu makiḻcci aṭinta tāyk kavutāri eḷitil akappaṭṭuviṭakkūṭum eṉṉum vakiyil taṉatu etirikku etirē ceṭṭiyi aṭittukkoṇṭu ōṭukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu atu taṉatu etiriyik kuñcukaḷirukkumiṭattiliruntu tūrattukkuk koṇṭu pōy viṭukiṟatu; taṉṉit toṭarntu varum etiriyi vēṇṭiyaḷavutūram koṇṭuvantuviṭṭatāka niṉikkumpōtu atu ākāyattil paṟantu celkiṟatu. taṉṉip piṉtoṭarnta etiri turattuvati niṟuttiviṭṭatum atu maṟupaṭiyum kuñcukaḷiṭam paṟantu ceṉṟu āpattu nīṅkiviṭṭatāka avaṟṟukku aṟivikkiṉṟatu.
nīrppaṟavikaḷ kuñcukaḷ porittatum avaṟṟi nīrnilikkuk koṇṭu celkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟukku nīntuvatu iyaṟkik kuṇam. avi virivil kaḷittuviṭukiṉṟaṉa. appoḻutu avi tāyiṉ mutukil ēṟi irukkiṉṟaṉa. tāy avaṟṟik karikkuk koṇṭuvarukiṉṟatu. nīrkkōḻi kaḷ ivvāṟē tamatu kuñcukaḷi nīruḷ koṇṭucelkiṉṟaṉa. intiya nīrk kōḻik kuñcukaḷ tamatu tāykkuk kīḻ iruppataṟkup patil mutukiṉ mēl paṭuttu nittiri koḷkiṉṟaṉa. tārā taṉatu kuñcukaḷi nīril payamillā mal irukkumpaṭi tiṟamiyākap payiṟṟukiṟatu; nām viḷaṅkamuṭiyāta oru olikkuṟiyiṉāl kuñcukaḷi aḻittu avaṟṟi mutukiṉ mēl ēṟṟikkoṇṭu nīril celkiṉṟatu. atu kariyil āḻamillāta nīril ciṟitunēram nīntiviṭṭup piṟaku āḻamuḷḷa iṭattukkuc celkiṉṟaṉa. naṭakkappōvatu eṉṉa eṉṟu aṟiyum muṉ tārā nīruḷ caṭutiyil mūḻkic celkiṟatu. kuñcukaḷ ellām nīril viṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avi apāyattil akappaṭṭatāka niṉittut tiṭukkiṭṭapōtum nīntat toṭaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ciṟitunērattil avaṟṟiṉ tāy ciṟitu tūrattiliruntu varukiṉṟatu. tām nīnta aṟintiruppatip paṟṟip perumip paṭṭukkoḷvatupōla avi uṭaṉē tāyiṉ mutukil ēṟukiṉṟaṉa.
mīṉkottikaḷ tām nīruḷ mūḻkip piṭitta mīṉkaḷi uṇṭu vāḻ kiṉṟaṉa. paṟappatu eppaṭi mukkiyamō appaṭiyē nīruḷ mūḻkuvatum avaṟṟukku mukkiyam. āṉāl nīril mūḻkuvatu paḻakikkoḷḷa vēṇṭi yatu. mīṉkottik kuñcukuḷ tamatu tāy āṟu, kuḷam mutaliyavaṟṟil mīṉ piṭippatip palamuṟi pārttukkoṇṭirukkum. kūṭṭiviṭṭu veḷiyē varakkūṭiyaḷavu vaḷarntatum avi mīṉkottikaḷ uṇṇamuṭiyātu viṭṭa mīṉmuṭkumpattil vantu irukkum. itaṟku aṭuttapaṭiyil avi āṟu allatu vāykkālukkup pakkattiluḷḷa marakkiḷiyil paṟantu ceṉṟu iruppatu.
pāṭuvatu tāṉum paṭittup payila vēṇṭiyatu. ellāppaṟavikaḷum pāṭukiṉṟaṉa eṉpatu uṇmiyē. oru paṟavikkuñcu vayatumutirnta paṟavikaḷ pāṭuvatik kēṭṭālaṉṟic cariyākap pāṭamāṭṭātu. maṟṟap paṟavikaḷiṉiṉṟum pirittuk koṇṭuvantu taṉiyē vaḷarkkappaṭum kāṭṭuppaṟavikaḷ teḷivākap pāṭuvatilli. avi pāṭum vēṟupaṟavi kaḷiṉ ōciyip piṉpaṟṟip pāṭukiṉṟaṉa.
viyappāṉa paṟavikaḷuḷ oṉṟu kāṉāṉkōḻi eṉṉum nīrkkōḻi. eṅku ciṟiya nīrniliyum maṟivukkēṟṟa nāṇalkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉ ṟaṉavō aṅku ik kōḻik kuṭumpaṅkaḷi pārkkalām. ik kōḻikaḷ oru āṇṭil palamuṟi kuñcu porikkiṉṟaṉa. mutalvippuk kuñcukaḷ naṉṟāy vaḷarumuṉ iraṇṭāvatu vippuk kuñcukaḷ porikkiṉṟaṉa. mutalāvatu kuṭumpam iravil taṅkuvataṟku ēṟṟa kūṭṭi āṇpaṟavi kaṭṭukiṉṟatu. ik kuṭumpam iraṇṭāvatu poritta kuñcukaḷi mikak karut tākap pātukākkiṉṟatu. mutal vippuk kuñcukaḷ uṇavu tēṭikkoṇṭu vantu avaṟṟiṉ tāytantikaḷip pōla avaṟṟukkuk koṭukkiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi nīntuvataṟku aḻittuc ceṉṟu nīntumpōtu avaṟṟiṉ tāy tantikaḷip pōlak kavaṉikkiṉṟaṉa.
paṟavik kuñcukaḷ viḷiyāṭuvatik kavaṉittirukkiṟāyā? avi vinōtamākat tōṉṟum. kāṉāṉ kōḻik kuñcukaḷ ciṟiya kuñcu kaḷukku utavi yaḷittal viḷiyāṭṭup pōṉṟatākum. maraṅkottiyum vēṟu paṟavikaḷum maraṅkaḷuḷ oḷittu viḷiyāṭukiṉṟaṉa.
ellāp paṟavikaḷilum pārkkak kuyil mika viṉōtamuḷḷatu. kuyiṟkuñcu iṉṉoru paṟaviyiṉ kūṭṭil porittu veḷiyēṟuvatu ataṉ tavaṟaṉṟu. piḻittirukka vēṇṭumāṉāl atu maṟṟak kuñcukaḷik kūṭṭukku veḷiyē taḷḷiviṭavēṇṭum. ataṟku atika iṭamum atika uṇavum tēvi. porittuc cila vāraṅkaḷiṉ piṉ atu taṉimiyāka allatu vēṟu kuyilkaḷōṭu cērntu kaṭalik kaṭantu āppirikkā mutaliya nāṭukaḷukkuc celkiṉṟatu. vēṟu vaḷarnta paṟavikaḷ vaḻikāṭṭā viṭiṉum iyaṟki iyalpūkkattiṉāl avi toliviṭaṅkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟaṉa.
14. paḻaṅkāla intiya nāṇayaṅkaḷ
ārampa nākarika kālattil iṉṟu pōla nāṇayaṅkaḷum nāṇayaṅ kaḷiṉ vilimatippum vēṇṭappaṭavilli. makkaḷ kūṭṭaṅkaḷ, allatu kuṭumpaṅkaḷāka vāḻntārkaḷ. vēṭṭiyāṭum allatu manti mēykkum niliyil avarkaḷukku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷ avarkaḷiṭamē iruntaṉa.
paṇṭamāṟṟu eṉpatu oru kūṭṭattārukkum iṉṉoru kūṭṭat tārukkumiṭiyil naṭipeṟuvatākaviruntatu. iru kūṭṭattiṉar naṭpu muṟiyil oruvarōṭu oruvar kalakkumpōtu iru kūṭṭattiṉarum tattamakku aṉukūlamaḷikkum poruḷkaḷip paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytu koḷvar.
alintu tiriyum kūṭṭattiṉar ōriṭattil taṅki vēḷāṇmi ceyyat toṭaṅkiyapōtu toḻiṟpiriviṉ kaṭṭāyam uṇṭāyiṟṟu. ap poḻutu ovvoruvarum tattamakku vēṇṭiya poruḷkaḷip paṇṭa māṟṟu muṟiyil peṟa vēṇṭiya nerukkaṭi uṇṭāyiṟṟu. immuṟi nāḷaṭivil eṅkum paraviṟṟu.
maṟṟellā nāṭukaḷilum pōlavē intiyāvilum paḻiya makka ḷiṭiyē paṇṭamāṟṟu muṟi iruntu vantatu. acivuḷḷa poruḷkaḷ paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceyyappaṭṭu vantamipaṟṟi irukku vētam kūṟukiṉṟatu. nāṇayaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkattoṭaṅkiyapiṉṉum immuṟi kiviṭappaṭa villi. kuṭaliyariṉ artta cāttiram, cātakak katikaḷ eṉpaṉavum paṇṭamāṟṟip paṟṟik kūṟukiṉṟaṉa. iṉṟum intiya nāṭṭiṉ cila pākaṅkaḷil āciriyariṉ vētaṉam, paṭakukkūli, paṇṇivēliyāḷar kūli mutaliyaṉa tāṉiya vakiyil koṭukkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. paṇṭamāṟṟuk kālattilum poruḷkaḷiṉ vilimatippu uṇṭāyiruntatu. vēṭṭi yāṭum niliyil āyutaṅkaḷum vilaṅkiṉ tōlkaḷum vilimatippup peṟṟiruntaṉa. manti mēykkum niliyil vili matippu āṭumāṭu kutiri eṉṉum vakiyil iruntatu. vēḷāṇmi ceyyum kālattil tāṉiyam, vīṭu, ulōkaṅkaḷ vilimatippup peṟṟiruntaṉa. irukku vētat til intirak kaṭavuṭ ciliyiṉ vilikkup pattup pacukkaḷ pōtāmal iruntaṉa eṉṟu collappaṭṭuḷḷatu. cōmappūṇṭiṉ vili pacukkaḷ vakiyil vili matikkappaṭṭatu. aitirēyap pirāmaṇattil oruvaṉuṭiya celvam pacukkaḷiṉ eṇṇāl aḷaviṭṭuk kūṟappaṭukiṉṟatu. vēḷāṇmi ceyyum kālattil, tāṉiya matippi aṭippaṭiyākak koṇṭu poruḷkaḷ vili matikkappaṭṭaṉa. kirīcu nāṭṭil poruḷkaḷukku viliyākat taṅkam koṭukkappaṭṭatāyiṉum vilimatippu erutu eṉṟē kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. oru erutu eṉpataṟku iṉṉa niṟiyuḷḷa taṅkam eṉpatu poruḷ. nāṇayaṅkaḷ vaḻakkattilirunta kālattum pirāmaṇar puriyum kiriyikaḷukkuk kūliyāka ittaṉi pacukkaḷ koṭukkappaṭavēṇṭu meṉṉum vitikaḷ iruntaṉa.
niṣka eṉṉum collukku mūṉṟu poruḷkaḷ uṇṭu. mutalāvatu poruḷ kaḻuttaṇi; iraṇṭāvatu poruḷ nāṇayam; mūṉṟāvatu poruḷ niṟi. itu nāṇaya vaḷarcci evvāṟu uṇṭāṉatu eṉpataṟku eṭuttuk kāṭṭākum. kaḻuttaṇiyāka vaḻaṅkiya niṣka piṟkālattil nāṇayamāka māṟiṟṟu. muṟkāla makkaḷiṭiyē kaḻuttaṇi paṇampōla māṟṟakkūṭiya tākaviruntatu. kaḻuttaṇiyiṉ niṟiyuḷḷa poṇ nāṇayamāka māṟiṟṟu.
ellā nāṭukaḷilum vaḻaṅkum niṟi tāvara vitikaḷiliruntu toṭaṅkiṟṟu. mēṟku nāṭukaḷil karaṭ1 eṉṉum tāvarattiṉ vitiyik koṇṭu niṟi kaṇikkappaṭṭatu. itu oru avuṉcu tirāy2 niṟiyiṉ 1/24 pakuti. intiyāvil kuṉṟimaṇi mañcāṭi kaḻañcu eṉṉum vakiyil niṟi niṟukkappaṭṭatu. kuṟikkappaṭṭa niṟikaḷuḷḷa ulōkat tuṇṭukaḷ muṟ kālattil nāṇayaṅkaḷāka vaḻaṅkiṉa.
intiyāvil pāṇiṉikkumuṉ (ki.mu.400) nāṇayam iruntatu. intiya nāṇayaṅkaḷ viyappāṉa vaṭivum niṟiyum uṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. avi tuḷiyiṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. cempu veḷḷi taṅkam eṉpavaṟṟil nāṇayaṅkaḷ aṭikkappaṭṭaṉa. muṟkālattil aracariṉ āṇiyip peṟṟut taṉippaṭṭavarkaḷ nāṇayamaṭittārkaḷ. piṟkālattil nāṇayam aṭittal aracāṅkattiṉ taṉi urimiyākkappaṭṭatu. 
nāṭṭut taṅkamum kiṭikkakkūṭiyatāka viruntatu. 19ām nūṟ ṟāṇṭiṉ muṟpakuti variyum taṅka nāṇayaṅkaḷ vaḻaṅkiṉa. 1835il āṅkilēyar koṇṭu vanta caṭṭattiṉpaṭi intiyā muḻumikkum orē vaki rūpāy nāṇayam koṇṭuvarappaṭṭatu.
kiḻakkintiya kumpaṉiyāriṉ āṭcikkuṭpaṭṭa nāṭṭil taṅka nāṇayam vaḻaṅkuvatu caṭṭavirōtam eṉappaṭṭatu. cōkikaḷ nāṇayaṅ kaḷāka mikap paḻaṅkālam mutal vaḻaṅkiṉa. cātakak katikaḷil cōkikaḷ, cippikkāṉi eṉṟu kūṟappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. cōki, nāṇayamākap payaṉpaṭuttap paṭṭamiyāl cōki eṉṉum peyar nāṇayattik kuṟippatākaviruntatu.
tuṇittuṇṭukaḷum nāṇayaṅkaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa. pāṇiṉi ivaṟṟi vacaṉā eṉak kuṟippiṭukiṉṟār. cāmariyum nāṇaya mākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭatu. ki.pi. 5ām nūṟṟāṇṭil viḷaṅkiyavarum vināyapiṭakattiṉ pērpeṟṟa uriyāciriyarumāṉa puttakōcar, mācaki eṉṉum mūṉṟu vaki nāṇayaṅkaḷip paṟṟik kūṟiyirukkiṉṟār. artta cāttirattil kūṟappaṭṭirukkum mācaki eṉpatu oru veḷḷip paṇattiṉ 1/16 peṟumatippuḷḷa cempu nāṇayameṉat terikiṟatu. mācaki nāṇayam potuvākac cempāyiruntateṉṟum irumpu allatu vēṟu ulōkaṅ kaḷil aṭikkappaṭṭa mācaki nāṇayaṅkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa eṉṟum puttakōcar kūṟiyuḷḷār. mūṅkiliṉ veḷippaṭṭiyāl allatu paṉi ōliyiṉāl ceytu mēlē muttiri iṭappaṭṭa oru vaki mācaki nāṇayaṅkaḷum vaḻaṅkiṉa. meḻuku allatu piciṉ tuṇṭil muttiri iṭappaṭṭatu iṉṉoru vaki. elumpu, tōl, maraṅkaḷiṉ vitikaḷum nāṇayaṅ kaḷākap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭaṉa eṉṟu putta kōcar kūṟiyuḷḷār.
15. viṭṭamiṉum atu illāmiyāl tōṉṟum nōykaḷum
namatu uṭalvaḷarcci, toṭarpāṉa toḻiṟ pāṭṭukku ēṉiya vaḷarcci aḷikkum uṇavuc cattukkaḷōṭu viṭṭamiṉ eṉṉum oru vakic cīva cattum vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu. viṭṭamiṉ kuṟiviṉāl uṇ ṭākum nōyip paṟṟi makkaḷ mattiya kālam mutal aṟintuḷḷārkaḷ.
uṇavu vakiyil vevvēṟu ciṟanta cattukkaḷ iruppatu ceṉṟa irupattintu āṇṭukaḷāka viññāṉamuṟiyil paricōtittu aṟiyap paṭṭu varukiṉṟatu.
muṟkālak kaṭalōṭikaḷ muracāl irattam vaṭital, muracu vīṅkuta lākiya coṟikarappāṉ(Scurvy) eṉṉum nōyip paṟṟi naṉku aṟin tiruntārkaḷ. kariyi aṭintu putiya paḻaṅkaḷiyum putiya kāykaṟi kaḷiyum uṇṭāl in nōy kuṇamaṭiyumeṉpatum avarkaḷukkut terintiruntatu. viyātikkum uṇavukkum eṉṉa toṭarpu uṇṭu eṉpati avarkaḷ aṟiyavilli.
taviṭu pōkkiya ariciyi uṇavākakkoḷḷum makkaḷiṭiyē periperi eṉṉum nōy peritum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇ ṭil yappāṉiya pōrvīraril mūṉṟiloru pakutiyiṉar in nōyiṉāl pīṭikkappaṭṭiruntaṉar. 1900il iṭaccukkārariṉ irāṇuvattilirunta maruttuvaroruvar innōy uṇaviluḷḷa cattuk kuṟiviṉāl uṇṭā kiṉṟateṉpatik kaṇṭupiṭittār. ataṉ piṉ in nōyit taṭukkum muṟi ulakam muḻuvatilum aṟiyappaṭṭatu.
palavaki uṇavukaḷilum viṭṭamiṉkaḷ eṉṉum oruvakic cīvacattukkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. nām uṇṇum uṇavil ivi kuṟivatāl ovvoru vaki nōy uṇṭākiṉṟatu. namatu uṭali nōyiṉṟik kāppāṟṟuvataṟku ivi vēṇṭum.
1912il kacimīr vaṅk (Dr.Casimir Funk) eṉṉum maruttuvar uṇavu kaḷiluḷḷa iccīva cattukkaḷil amiṉkaḷ (Amiunes) eṉṉum niṭrōciṉ campantamāṉa iracāyaṉap poruḷkaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa eṉak karutiṉār. ivi uṭalukku avaciyam vēṇṭappaṭuvaṉa eṉṟu karutappaṭṭamiyāl amiṉkaḷ eṉpataṉ muṉ viṭ (Vit) eṉṉum col cērkkappaṭṭatu. ivvuyirccattup poruḷkaḷ amiṉkaḷ alla eṉṟu kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭapiṉ appeyar viṭṭamiṉ (Vitamin) eṉa māṟṟappaṭṭatu. viṭṭa miṉkaḷ eṉpaṉa uṭampiṟ cuvaṟi uṭal toṭarpāṉa cila iracāyaṉa māṟṟaṅ kaḷic ceyyum oru vakic cattukkaḷ. iviyiṉṟi ammāṟṟaṅkaḷ uṇṭākamāṭṭā. ivvuyirc cattukkaḷellām iracāyaṉa campantamāka oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu campantappaṭṭaviyalla. ivi (1) amiṉ poruḷkaḷ (2) koḻuppuḷḷa matuccāraṅkaḷ (Fatty Alchols) (3) carkkarip vakikaḷ (4) karaṭṭiṉ poruḷkaḷiliruntu peṟappaṭuvaṉa eṉṟu pirikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. uṭal nalattukku vēṇṭiya iccattukkaḷ ē,pi,ci,ṭi, i,ep, ec, kē,pi (A B C D E F H K) eṉap peyariṭappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa.
ic cattukkaḷ maṉitaṉukku uṇṭākum nōykaḷ ellāvaṟṟukkum maruntāka māṭṭā. ivi maṉitaṉ uṭal nalattōṭu vāḻvataṟku utavipuri kiṉṟaṉa. ovvoru viṭṭamiṉ cattum vevvēṟu kuṇam ceykiṉṟami yiṉ viṭṭamiṉ cattukkaḷit taṉittaṉi aḷavāka (Doses) uṭkoḷ vatil payaṉilli. vevvēṟu uṇavu vakikaḷil evvāṟu ivvuyirc cattukkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu aṟintu avaṟṟi uṭkoḷvatu payaṉaḷippatākum.
ē viṭṭamiṉ (A) ciṟuvariṉ uṭal vaḷarccikku vēṇṭappaṭuvatu. itu nuriyīral, ciṟunīrakam toṭarpāṉa toṟṟu nōykaḷi etirttu uṇavil viruppattiyum aḷavāṉa cīraṇippiyum uṇṭākkukiṉṟatu. ivvuyirc cattu ciṟitumillāviṭil kaṇṇil apāyam viḷikkum nōyuṇṭākiṟatu.
kaṇ campantamāṉa tacinārkaḷ paḻutaṭikiṉṟaṉa; avaṟṟukkup patil pōliyāṉa kaṇṇaṟi (Cells) uḷḷa taci (Abnormal Cellular Growth) vaḷarkiṉṟatu. mutalām ulakap pōriṉpōtu veṇṇeyum pālumiṉṟi vaḷarnta iṭeṉmārk nāṭṭuc ciṟuvarukku innōy kāṇappaṭṭatu. uṇa vuṭaṉ putiya veṇṇey cērkkappaṭṭatum innōy maṟintuviṭṭatu. ē uyirccattuc ciṟitu kuṟivatiṉāl uṭaṟpalavīṉam, ciṟunīrakam, īraliṟ kal uṇṭātal. mālikkaṇ, malaṭu mutaliya nōykaḷ uṇṭākum. ic cattu caraṭ eṉappaṭum mañcaḷ muḷḷaṅkic kiḻaṅkil mañcaḷ niṟatti uṇ ṭākkum karaṭṭiṉ (Carotene) eṉṉum iracāyaṉap poruḷōṭu campantap paṭṭatu. palavakip paccik kāykaṟikaḷ, apirikoṭ. taḷirvakikaḷ. muṭṭi veṇṇey, pāl pālāṭik kaṭṭi (Cheese) māṭṭīral. ciṟunīrakaṅ kaḷil uṇṭu.
pi viṭṭamiṉ (B) itil pala cattukkaḷ aṭaṅkiyuḷḷaṉa. pi viṭ ṭamiṉ oṉṟu, iraṇṭu, mūṉṟu, nāṉku aintu, āṟu eṉa āṟuvakippaṭum.
viṭṭamiṉ pi: 1. tacinārkaḷ toḻiṟpaṭuvataṟku vēṇṭiyatu. itu uṭal vaḷarcciyi atikappaṭuttukiṉṟatu. uṇavic cīraṇikkac ceyva taṟkum, uṇavil viruppam uṇṭākkuvataṟkum utavi purikiṉṟatu; toṟṟu nōykaḷit taṭukkiṉṟatu. itu ciṟitum illāviṭil periperi eṉṉum maraṇatti viḷikkum nōy uṇṭākiṉṟatu. in nōy kāl kikaḷil cōrvātattiyum irutayak kōḷāṟukaḷiyum naramput taḷarcciyiyum kuṭalil nīrkkoḷḷutalākiya turopci (Dropsy) eṉṉum nōyiyum uṇṭākkukiṟatu. taviṭu pōkkiya arici uṇavākak koḷḷum makka ḷiṭiyē paravum innōy taviṭu pōkkāta arici uṇavik koṭuppa tāl taṭukkappaṭukiṉṟatu. innōy peritum paraviyirunta pilippiṉ tīvukaḷil periperi vittiya cālikaḷ ippoḻutu mūṭappaṭṭuviṭṭaṉa. pi.1 viṭṭamiṉ cattu vitikaḷ, muḻut tāṉiyavakikaḷ, payaṟu, muntirikip paḻam, kīri, īral muṭṭi ākiya uṇavukaḷil uṇṭu.
pi.2 itu uṭal vaḷarcciyi atikappaṭuttukiṟatu. itu illāmi yāl kaḻuttil veṭippu uṇṭāvatākiya piḷavi nōy (Pellagra) uṇṭākiṟatu; ciṟuvar metuvāka vaḷarkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷiṉ tacinārkaḷ naṉku toḻiṟ paṭamāṭṭā. ivvuyirccattu ilivakikaḷ. īral , ciṟunīrakam, muṭṭi, iṟicci mutaliya uṇavukaḷil uṇṭu. maṟṟiya pi viṭṭa miṉkaḷ naṉṟāka ārāyappaṭavilli. ivaṟṟuḷ oṉṟu civappu irattatti luḷḷa ceṅkūṭukaḷi oḻuṅkāka uṇṭākkuvatilum. uṭalveḷiṟum cōki nōyit (Anemia) taṭuppatilum utavi purikiṉṟatu.
viṭṭamiṉ ci (C) elumpukaḷiyum paṟkaḷiyum uṟutip paṭuttik kākkiṉṟatu; iratta ācayaṅkaḷiṉ cukattik kāppāṟṟukiṉṟatu; nōykkirumikaḷ toṭarpāṉa toṟṟunōykaḷit taṭukkiṟatu. itu kuṟi vatāl potuvāṉa palavīṉam, taliyiṭi, kāyaṅkaḷ metuvāka āṟutal, cantunōy allatu uḷivu uṇṭātal mutaliyaṉa nikaḻum. itu muṟṟu millāviṭil coṟikarappaṉ (Scurvy) nōy uṇṭākiṟatu. innōy irattat tiraṭci. palavīṉam. veḷuveḷuppu, maṉaccōrvu. mayakkam mutaliyavaṟṟik koṭukkum viṭṭamiṉ ci eṉṉum uyirccattu tōṭampaḻam (ārēñcup paḻam) elumiccampaḻam kīri mutaliyavaṟṟiluṇṭu; tāvara vaṟṟalkaḷil itu kāṇappaṭamāṭṭātu.
viṭṭamiṉ ṭi(D) cila camayaṅkaḷil itu veyil viṭṭamiṉ eṉṟum collappaṭum. itu uṭalilē cūriya oḷiyiṉāluṇṭākum oru vakik koḻuppup poruḷ. uyiruḷḷa caṭapporuḷkaḷil veḷi ūtā (Ultraviolet) oḷikkatirkaḷ paṭutalāl ṭi viṭṭamiṉ uṇṭākiṉṟatu. iv vuyirccattu illāmiyāl malaṭṭut taṉmiyum, āṇkaḷukku āṇ taṉmi illāmiyum, peṇkaḷukkup peṇ taṉmi illāmiyum uṇṭākiṟatu. callāttuk kīri (Lettuce) ispiṉāk (Sinach) kīri vērkkaṭali pāl, muṭṭi, iṟicci mutaliyavaṟṟil iccattu uṇṭu.
ī viṭṭamiṉ (E) āṇ peṇ eṉṉum iru pālārukkum cantati viruttikku vēṇṭiyatu. ilikaḷ paruppukaḷuḷ irukkum muḷi, kōtumi muḷi, eṇṇey mutaliyavaṟṟil itu uṇṭu. makkaḷuḷ malaṭu kāṇappaṭuvataṟku mukkiya kāraṇam ī viṭṭamiṉ illāmiyākum.
viṭṭamiṉ kē (K) tāvara ilikaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭukiṟatu. itu illā miyāl kuḻantikaḷukku irattakkuḻāy campantamāṉa nōy uṇṭā kiṟatu; vaḷarntavarkaḷukku mayir utirtal pōṉṟa tōl campantamāṉa nōy uṇṭākiṟatu.
viṭṭamiṉ pi (P) illāmiyāl coṟikarappaṉ, tōl, muracukaḷil irattam vaṭital, kiviral nakaṅkaḷ veṭittal mutaliya nōykaḷ uṇṭākiṟatu.
16. uyirkaḷiṉ viyappumikka
taṟkāppu muṟikaḷ
ātiyil uyirkaḷ perukip parumiyaṭinta kālattil tamatu metuvāṉa uṭali apāyamiṉṟi eppaṭik kāppateṉṟum. ataṟkup pōtumāṉa pātukāppaḷippatu eppaṭiyeṉṟum avi aṟiyātiruntaṉa. mutaṟṟōṉṟiya uyirkaḷukku vayiramāṉa pakutikaḷ oṉṟēṉumillātirun tirukkalām. ellā uyirkaḷum tāvara uṇavaḷavil tiruptiyaṭinta kālattil avaṟṟukku uṭalil vayiramāṉa pakutikaḷ vēṇṭappaṭavilli.
kālam cellac cella avaṟṟuṭ cila ūṉ uṇavi virumpiṉa appoḻutu avi kavacam tāṅkat toṭaṅkiṉa. ak kālam mutal kavacam tāṅkum pōṭṭi naṭipeṟukiṉṟatu. pāṟikaḷil paḻaṅkāla uyirkaḷiṉ kaṟpaṭi uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. nūṟukōṭi allatu atikappaṭṭa āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ pala uyirkaḷ cuṇṇāmpiyum maṇaliyum kaṭal nīriliruntu iḻuttu avaṟṟāl tamatu pātukāppukku vēṇṭiya ōṭukaḷi ākkik koṇṭaṉa.
ivvāṟu ceyyum vaḻakkam viyappāṉa peṟupēṟṟi aḷittatu. avi tammip pātukākkum kavacaṅkaḷi amittukkoṇṭatōṭu piṟ kālattil poruttuḷḷa elumpukaḷiyum uṇṭākkikkoṇṭaṉa. iv vaki amippu illāviṭil nilattil allatu ākāyattil virivil iyaṅkuvatu muṭiyātu. tāvaraṅkaḷiṉ elumpupōṉṟa pakuti palattiyum etirttu niṟkum āṟṟaliyum koṭukkiṉṟatu. avaṟṟukkup porut tukkaḷ illi. mikac ciṟiya uyirkaḷē nīrilacintu tirintu uṇavip peṟa vēṇṭiyirukkiṉṟatu.
pulvakikaḷuṭ pala tammip palappaṭuttuvataṟkut taṇṭukaḷil maṇalic cērttu vikkiṉṟaṉa. naṉṟāka vaḷarnta oru kōtumi vikkōlip poṭṭācu uppōṭu kalantu urukkiṉāl atil oru kaṇṇāṭik kumiḻ uṇṭākak kūṭiya maṇal uṇṭu. maraṅkaḷ, ceṭikaḷiṉ uṭpakutikaḷil celulōcu eṉṉum poruḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. ipporuḷit tāvaraṅkaḷē uṇṭākkikkoḷkiṉṟaṉa.
uṭal muḻuvatiyum ōṭṭiṉāl mūṭikkoḷḷum upāyam teṉ ṉamerikkāvil vāḻntu maṟintupōṉa ārmaṭilō eṉṉum aḻuṅ kiṉāl kiyāḷappaṭṭateṉat terikiṉṟatu. ataṉ ōṭukaḷ cetilkaḷ pōlat tōliṉ mītiruntaṉa. apāyatti aṟintatum atu kavacamiṭṭa pantupōlac curuṇṭu kiṭantatu.
muṟkālattil avvaḻuṅkil mikapperiya pala iṉaṅkaḷ vāḻntaṉa. kiḷiptōṭaṉ (Glyptodon) eṉṉum vilaṅkiṉ nīḷam paṉṉiraṇṭaṭi. itaṟku iṉamuḷḷa cilamiṭōtirē (Chlamydothere) itaṉilum parumiyuṭiya tāyum ikkālak kāṇṭāmiruka maḷaviṉatāyumiruntatu. itu taṉatu mutukil mukaṭupōṉṟa periya ōṭukaḷik koṇṭu tirintatu. avvāṟiruntum atu mika muṟkālattil maṟintu pōyiṟṟu. atu maṟintu pōṉamikkuk kāraṇam yāteṉa aṟiyamuṭiyavilli. atu cumantukoṇṭu tiriya vēṇṭi yirunta paḷuvuḷḷa ōṭukaḷiṉ pārattiṉāl ciṟitu ciṟitākap palam kuṉṟi yirukkalām. iṟutiyil vāḻntatu nōyiṉāl caṭutiyil māṇṭirukkalām.
aḻuṅkukaḷiyum avaṟṟiṉ muṉṉatukaḷiyum viṭak kaṭalāmi yum nila āmiyum kavacam tāṅkiya uyirkaḷukkuc ciṟanta eṭuttukkāṭṭu kaḷākum. avaṟṟiṉ ōṭukaḷiṟ cila pakutikaḷ mutukelumpu vilā elumpu kaḷiṉ vaḷarcciyākum; cila tōliliruntu vaḷarvaṉavākum.
ōṭṭiṉ veḷippākam kompupōṉṟa poruḷālamintatu. ataṉ ōraṅkaḷiluḷḷa pakutikaḷ elumpiliruntu toṭaṅkivarum ōṭu pōṉṟatalla, āmikaḷukku elumpiliruntu vaḷarntuvarum ōṭu, veḷit tōliliruntu varum ōṭu eṉa iraṇṭu uṇṭu.
kaṭalāmikaḷum mutalikaḷum paḻiya orē muṉṉatiliruntu tōṉṟiṉa eṉṟu terikiṟatu. ivaṟṟiṉ paḻutaṭiyak kūṭiya uṟuppukaḷiṉ mēl kompupōṉṟu vayiramāṉa muḷikaḷuḷḷa tōlum ataṉkīḻ naṉku vaḷarcciyaṭinta elumpuppaṭiyum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kaṭalāmi aṅkumiṅkumākac citaṟiyirunta ōṭṭi vaḷarac ceytu peṭṭipōṉṟa kavacattic ceytukoṇṭu taliyiyum veḷiyuṟuppukaḷiyum uḷḷē iḻuttukkoṇṭu etirikku akappaṭāmal kiṭakkiṉṟatu.
viyappāṉa aḻuṅkukaḷukku mēlē mūṭikkoḷḷum cetilkaḷ pōṉṟa kavacamuṇṭu. kiviraliṉ nakam pōṉṟa cetilkaḷ vīṭuvēyum ōṭukaḷ pōla oḻuṅkupaṭṭuk kiṭakkiṉṟaṉa. naṇṭiṉaṅkaḷum kavacamaṇin tuḷḷaṉa. naṇṭukaḷ peṭṭipōṉṟa kavacattit tamatu pātukāppukku nampi yirukkiṉṟaṉa. ōyntirukkum pōtu tamatu kālkaḷi uṭaliṉ kīḻ maṭittu vittukkoḷkiṉṟaṉa. appoḻutu avaṟṟiṉ tōṟṟam taṇṇīraritta kūḻāṅkaṟkaḷ pōṉṟatu. ‘koṭ” pōṉṟa cila periya mīṉkaḷ naṇṭi muḻumiyāka viḻuṅkukiṉṟaṉa. avvaki etirikaḷiṉiṉṟu tappuvataṟku kūḻāṅkal pōṉṟa kavacam pātukāppaḷiyātu; kalpōṉṟa tōṟṟam pātu kāppaḷikkum.
nattikaḷ pōṉṟa uyirkaḷum peṭṭikaḷ pōṉṟa kavacattit tamatu pātukāppukkup payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa. peṭṭipōṉṟa kavacattukkuc cippi eṭuttukkāṭṭākum. ataṉ iraṇṭu ōṭukaḷum tacināriṉāl piṇikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. virintu kiṭakkum avviraṇṭu ōṭukaḷiṉiṭiyē oruvar kāli vikka nērntāl kaṭaṟperukku vantu avari mūḻkac ceyyum variyil avi iṟukappiṭikkiṉṟaṉa eṉṉum kaṟpaṉik katikaḷ vaḻaṅkukiṉṟaṉa. ikkatikaḷ uṇmiyi aṭippaṭiyāka uṭiyaṉavō eṉpatu aiyattukkuriyatu. periya cippikaḷ ivvakic ceyalip puriyak kūṭiya valimiyuṭiyaṉa eṉpatu uṇmiyē. iraṇṭu ōṭukaḷuṭiya periya cippikaḷ uḷḷaṉa. ivaṟṟuḷ mikapperiyatu mūṉṟaṭi nīḷamum 500 iṟāttal eṭiyumuṭiyatākak kāṇappaṭṭatu.
ōr ōṭuḷḷa cippikaḷiṉ ōṭukaḷil muḷpōṉṟa kūriya muṉikaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. itaṉik kaṭal muḷḷeli eṉṟu kūṟalām. nattikaḷ tamatu piṉpuṟattil vaṭṭamuḷḷa oru vayirap poruḷikkoṇṭu tirikiṉṟaṉa. itu ōṭṭiṉ vāyi mūṭakkūṭiya aḷaviṉatu. etiyēṉum kaṇṭu payappaṭumpōtu natti ōṭṭiṉuḷ curuṅkikkoḷkiṟatu. ōṭṭiṉ tuvāram tāṉē aṭipaṭṭu viṭukiṟatu. mūṭiyit tiṟakka evvaḷavu muyalap paṭukiṉṟatō avvaḷavukku atu uḷḷukku iḻukkappaṭukiṟatu. oru nattiyi uṇṇavēṇṭumāyiṉ ataṉ ōṭṭi uṭikkavēṇṭum allatu ataṉi muḻumiyāka viḻuṅkavēṇṭum. mikapperiya mīṉkaḷum ataṉi viḻuṅkat tayaṅkum.
cippikaḷiṭam tammip pātukākkum iṉṉoru upāyamuṇṭu. atu pāṟiyip piṭittirukkum piṭiyi viṭuvittal muṭiyātu. paṟitteṭukkil cippiyik kāyappaṭutta vēṇṭum. ataṉ piṭiyiṉ valu aṟupattiraṇṭu ṟāttal eṭiyaḷavu eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. itu oru cippiyiṉ eṭiyilum pārkka 1984 maṭaṅku atikam. cippi ōriṭattil taṅkiyirukkiṉṟatu eṉṟu niṉittal kūṭātu. atu uṇavit tēṭip pāṟikaḷil ūrntu tirikiṉṟatu. ataṉuṇavu palavakit tāvaraṅkaḷākum.
kaṭalil vaṟṟu uṇṭākumpōtu vaḷarkkum puṟā taṉatu kūṭṭi aṭivatu pōl atutāṉ iruppataṟkut terintuḷḷa iṭattukkuc cel kiṉṟatu. atu nīṇṭakālam ōriṭattiliruppatāl pāṟiyil ciṟiya paḷḷa muṇṭākiṉṟatu. ip paḷḷattukkuḷ atu ūrntu ceṉṟu pāṟiyip paṟṟikkoṇṭu kiṭakkiṟatu.
pāṟikaḷiyum maraṅkaḷiyum tuḷikkum nattivakikaḷumuṇṭu. pāṟikaḷit tuḷikkum natti veṇṇiṟamuṭiyatu. itu iḷami mutal pāṟiyic ciṟitu ciṟitākat tuḷittu uḷḷē celkiṉṟatu. taṉatu pātam pōṉṟa pakutiyi iṭiviṭātaḻuttiyum, ōṭṭit tuvārattiṉ pakkaṅkaḷil uriñciyum atu tuḷikkiṟatu. ciṟitu ciṟitāka vaḷarcci yaṭintu koṇṭirukkum kālattil natti pāṟikkuḷ ceṉṟu viṭu kiṟatu. atu vaḷarntatum tuḷiyiṉ mēṟpākattiṉ veḷi kīḻppākatti viṭac ciṟitākaviruppatāl atu avvaṟiyilēyē maṟiyaṟ paṭuttappaṭu kiṉṟatu. ataṉāl atu etirikaḷukku iriyākāmaṟ piḻikkiṟatu.
itaṟku iṉamuḷḷa iṉṉoru natti uṇṭu. itu kappaṟpuḻu (Shipworm) eṉappaṭum. itu nīruḷ amiḻntikkiṭakkum marattit tuḷik kiṉṟatu. irumpuk kappalkaḷ ceyyappaṭuvataṉ muṉ ulakil ellāk kappal kaḷum in nattikaḷukku añciṉa. atu kaṉatta karuvāli marattuṇṭu kaḷiyum ciṟitu nērattil tuḷittuviṭum āṟṟal vāyntatu. marakkalaṅ kaḷiṉ kīḻppuṟattē cemput takaṭukaḷ iṭappaṭṭapōtu itaṉāl avaṟ ṟukkuk keṭuti ceyyamuṭiyavilli. ollāntu nāṭṭil aṇikkaṭṭukkup payaṉpaṭuttappaṭṭuḷḷa maraṅkaḷi atu tuḷittu viṭutalāl palamuṟi kaḷil veḷḷam uḷḷē perukuvatuṇṭu.
maṇṇit tuḷittukkoṇṭu uḷḷē irukkum maṇpuḻuk kaḷip paṟṟic cila kūṟuvōm. puṟṟukaḷiṉ vāykaḷiṟ kāṇappaṭum ciṟu kumpam maṇṇikkoṇṭu aṅku maṇpuḻu irukkiṟateṉa aṟiyalām. puṟṟukaḷip pāriyāl kiṇṭiṉālallatu puḻukkaḷik kāṇpataritu. puṟṟuḷ vāl kiṭakkumpaṭi avi nilattil nimirntu kiṭappati nām irāk kālattiṟ kāṇalām. avi puṟṟit tōṇṭumpōtu maṇṇi iṭi viṭātu viḻuṅki maṟupuṟattāl ataṉi veḷippaṭuttikkoṇṭirukkum. avvāṟu ceyyumpōtu avi atiluḷḷa uṇavuc catti vāṅkikoḷ kiṉṟaṉa. avi aḻukum ilikaḷip puṟṟiṉuḷ iḻuttucceṉṟu uṇavākac cēmittu vikkiṉṟaṉa.
avi kīḻē irukkum maṇṇi mēlē koṇṭuvantu kāṟṟup paṭumpaṭi ceytu nilattic ceḻippaṭiyac ceyyum payaṉuṭiya vēli purikiṉṟaṉa. maṇ puḻukkaḷāl payircci ceykikku uṇṭākum vāyppu kaḷip paṟṟi iṭārviṉ eṭuttuk kāṭṭiyuḷḷār. oru ēkkar nilattil carācari 50,000 maṇpuḻukkaḷ vāḻkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum. avi oru āṇṭil mēlē koṇṭu varum maṇ 28 toṉ aḷaviṉateṉṟum avar kaṇakkiṭṭuk kūṟiyuḷḷār. ovvoru puḻuvum taṉittaṉi irupatu avuṉcu eṭi maṇṇi veḷiyē koṇṭuvarukiṟatu.
āstirēliyāvil kipslāntu eṉṉumiṭattil eṭṭaṭi nīḷa muḷḷa maṇ puḻukkaḷ kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. kōṭi kālattil avi pūmiyit tuḷittukkoṇṭu āḻac celkiṉṟaṉa. māri kālattil avi nilatti āḻat tuḷittukkoṇṭu kīḻē celkiṉṟaṉa. mārikālattil avi tari maṭṭattukku aṇmiyilirukkiṉṟaṉa. iṅkilāntilē tiṟacu (Thrush) eṉṉum paṟavi kaṇṇukkup pulappaṭāmal nilattuḷḷirukkum maṇpuḻu eppoḻutu veḷiyē varumeṉṟu ōciyiṉālaṟiyum. avvoci namatu kātukkuk kēṭkamāṭṭātu.
maṇpuḻukkaḷukku iṉṉoru paki oru vaki eli. itu maṇṇi atika virivil tuḷittukkoṇṭu cellum. akaḻāṉ kuṟikkappaṭṭa nērattil vēli ceytu kuṟikkappaṭṭa nērattil ōyntirukkiṉṟateṉa pirāṇikaḷip paṟṟi ārāyum aṟiñar kūṟuvar. nilattit tuḷikkum eli kāli 7 maṇi, 11 maṇi, māli 3 maṇi eṉṉum nēraṅkaḷil cuṟucuṟup pāka vēli ceykiṉṟatu. atu āṇṭiṉ perumpakutik kālattil nilap puḻukkaḷiyum pūccikaḷiyum tēṭi nilatti aṅkumiṅkum tuḷittuk koṇṭu celkiṉṟatu.
maraṅkaḷiṉ uṭpakuti vaḷiyaṅkaḷāka vaḷarkiṟatu. ovvoru āṇṭum paṭṭikkuk kīḻ aṭimarattiṉ ovvoru vaḷiyam vaḷarkiṟatu. marattit taṟittāl ivvarikaḷikkoṇṭu marattiṉ vayati aṟiyalām. kalipōrṉiyāvil taṟikkappaṭṭa cila maraṅkaḷiṉ vayatu 3000 variyil eṉṟu kaṇakkiṭappaṭṭuḷḷatu. aṅku 5000 āṇṭukaḷukku mēṟpaṭṭa vayatuṭiya maraṅkaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa.
17. ōṭum uyir āṟu
maṉita irattattil palavaki aṇu uyirkaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉa. ivi namatu uṭalukku naṉmipayakkum toḻilkaḷ purintuvarukiṉṟaṉa. iviyiṉṟi nām uyirvāḻa muṭiyātu.
oru kaṉa aṅkula maṉita irattittil 82,000,000,000 ceṅkūṭukaḷum, 16,400,000,000 veṇkūṭukaḷum, 13,000,000 takaṭṭu vaṭivāṉa kūṭukaḷum vēṟu cila poruḷkaḷumuḷḷaṉa. namatu uṭal nalam peṟṟiruppataṟku iviyellām iṉṟiyamiyātaṉa.
ivi viyappaḷippaṉavāyiṉum mika maṟivākayirukkiṉṟaṉa. kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa ivvaṇu uyirkaḷellām yātu ceykiṉṟaṉa eṉṟum ivi uṭali evvāṟu pala maṭiyac ceykiṉṟaṉaveṉṟum nīvir cintittatuṇṭā?
toṭakkattil irattattil nīrppakuti uḷḷatu. nām niṉippatu pōla ataṉ niṟam civappu aṉṟu; atu niṟamillāta oru poruḷ. irattam civappākat tōṉṟuvatu atil civappuk kūṭukaḷ iruppataṉālākum.
namatu uṭalil kōṭikkaṇakkāṉa ceṅkūṭukaḷ irukkiṉṟaṉaveṉṟu kūṟiṉōm. avi mirutuvākavum, vaḷiyakkūṭiyaṉavākavum irukkum. avaṟṟiṉ kuṟukkaḷavu mūvāyirattiloru aṅkulam. taṭippu oṉpatiṉāyirattiloru aṅkulam. im mikac ciṟiya kūṭukaḷ illāviṭil nām uyir vāḻa muṭiyātu.
namatu cukavāḻvukkup pirāṇa vāyu evvaḷavu mukkiyam eṉpati nām aṟivōm. pirāṇavāyu iṉṟi nām uyir vāḻamuṭiyātu. cuvācikkum pōtu nām kāṟṟi nuriyīralukkuḷ iḻukkiṟōm. uyir koṭukkum ivvāyu namatu nuriyīral oṉṟukku māttiram vēṇṭiyataṉṟu. nām uṭal nalattōṭu vāḻavēṇṭumāyiṉ namatu uṭaliṉ ovvoru pakutikkum pirāṇa vāyu cellutal vēṇṭum. nuriyīraliliruntu pirāṇavāyu maṟu pakutikaḷukkuc celvatu eppaṭi? atu ic ceṅkūṭukaḷ vaḻiyākac celkiṉṟatu. ivvaṇu uyirkaḷ namatu nuriyīralukkūṭākac cellumpōtu ovvoṉṟum ciṟitu pirāṇavāyuvi uṟiñcukiṉṟatu. nām uḷḷē iḻukkum pirāṇavāyu ivvāṟu ceṅkūṭukaḷ vaḻiyāka namatu uṭal muḻumikkum celkiṉṟatu.
ic ceṅkūṭukaḷiṉ virintu perukak kūṭiya taṉmi mika viyappuk kuriyatu. uṭalil vaḻakkamākavuḷḷa 12,000,000,000,000 ceṅkūṭukaḷirukkum pōtu oruvaṉ mali mītu ēṟukiṟāṉ eṉa vittukkoḷvōm. avaṉ maliucciyi aṭiyumpōtu avaṉatu irattattil 18,000,000,000,000 ceṅkūṭukaḷ irukkalām. ippoḻutu muṉṉirunta eṇṇil oru pāti atikarittuḷḷatu. mali uyaramākaviruntāl avaṉ ceṉṟu koṇṭirup pataṟku ivi tēvippaṭum. ēṉ? avaṉ evvaḷavu tūram kaṭal maṭṭat tukkumēl ēṟukiṟāṉō avvaḷavukkuk kāṟṟiluḷḷa pirāṇavāyu kuṟi kiṟatu. ākavē avaṉ ceṉṟu koṇṭirukka vēṇṭumāṉāl avaṉ uḷḷē iḻuttuc cuvācikkum ellāk kāṟṟum payaṉpaṭuttappaṭutal vēṇṭum. ataṟkēṟpa avaṉ mēlē cellaccella icceṅkūṭukaḷum perukip pirāṇavāyuvil evvaḷavum payaṉiṉṟik kaḻiyātapaṭi ataṉi payaṉpaṭuttukiṉṟaṉa.
ic ceṅkūṭukaḷ elumpiṉuḷ irukkum niṇattiliruntu uṇṭā kiṉṟaṉa eṉṟu viññāṉikaḷ karutukiṉṟaṉar. ovvoru ceṅkūṭum civappu niṟapporuḷkalanta puratattiṉāl āṉatu. kūṭukaḷiṉ civappuniṟamūṭṭum poruḷē pirāṇavāyuvi uṟiñci uṭal muḻumikkum koṇṭu celkiṉṟatu.
ceṅkūṭukaḷ eppaṭi namatu uyirvāḻkkikku vēṇṭappaṭu kiṉṟaṉavō avvaḷavukku veṇkūṭukaḷum vēṇṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. irat tattil veṇkūṭukaḷum kāṇappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. ivi ceṅkūṭukaḷilum pārk kapperiyavi; oru aṅkulattil 2500il oru pakuti kuṟukkaḷavuṭi yavi; kuṟikkappaṭṭa orē vaṭiviṉavalla; ivi illāviṭiṉ nām nōy vāyppaṭṭu iṟantuviṭuvōm.
veṇkūṭukaḷ namatu uṭalil pukum aḻukki akaṟṟuvaṉavum, nōykkirumikaḷi etirttup pōrpurivaṉavum ākum. namakkut toṟṟu nōy kaṇṭu nōykkirumikaḷ irattattuḷ pukuntāl veṇkūṭukaḷ uṭaṉē pāyntu avaṟṟi uṇṭuviṭum. nām nakattit turuvēṟiya irumpiṉāl kiḻittukkoṇṭāl nañcu uḷḷē celkiṉṟatu. appoḻutu irattatti luḷḷa veṇkūṭukaḷ nammik kākkum. avi uṭaṉē kāyam paṭṭa iṭat tukku virintu vantu naccuk kirumikaḷi viḻuṅkip paḻutaṭinta taci nārkaḷiyum uṇṭuviṭum. kāyam māṟik kuṇamaṭiyum variyilum. naccuk kirumikaḷ ellām nācamaṭiyum variyilum avi tamatu etirppi niṟutta māṭṭā.
iv veṇkūṭukaḷ kaṭiya nōyi etirkkum poruṭṭuc ceṅkūṭu kaḷip pōṉṟu atikam perukutalum kūṭum. namatu irattattiluḷḷa iv veṇkūṭukaḷē tamatu pōrāṭṭaṅkaḷil vaḻakkamāka veṟṟiyaṭikiṉṟaṉa. cila camayaṅkaḷil paṭi eṭuttuvarum nōykkirumikaḷ veṟṟiyaṭivatu muṇṭu. appoḻutu nām nōyvāyppaṭukiṉṟōm.
mūṉṟāvatāka uḷḷa viyappu irattattiluḷḷa takaṭupōṉṟa poruḷkaḷ. avi mikac ciṟiya uruviṉa. ākavē avaṟṟip paṟṟi atikam aṟiyappaṭavilli. avi taṭṭi allatu vaḷinta vaṭiviṉa. avi namatu iratta ācayaṅkaḷiṉ nalattip pēṇukiṉṟaṉa. iratta ācayattiṉ cuvar kāyappaṭṭāl ic ciṟutaṭṭi vaṭivaṅkaḷ uṭaṉē kāyamaṭinta iṭattil kuvikiṉṟaṉa. avi avviṭattil kaṭṭiyākat tiraṇṭu kāyatti aṭittu irattācayaṅkaḷiliruntu irattam veḷiyē peruki nām iṟantu pōkātapaṭi kākkiṉṟaṉa.
ivi namatu iratta ācayaṅkaḷukku eṉṉa naṉmi ceykiṉ ṟaṉavō avvāṟē uṟiyun taṉmiyuḷḷa iṉṉoru poruḷ namatu mēṟpuṟat tacikkum celkiṉṟatu. ipporuḷ illāviṭil nām uṭalil veṭṭumpōtu irattamellām veḷiyē peruki ōṭiviṭanām iṟantu pōvōm.
nām uṭalil veṭṭikkoṇṭāl allatu curaṇṭikkoṇṭāl mutalil kāyattiliruntu irattam vaṭikiṟatu; piṉṉar niṉṟu viṭukiṟatu. kāyattiṉ mītu kaṟuppuniṟak kaṭṭi oṉṟu mūṭi mēlum irattam vaṭiyāmal taṭukkiṉṟatu. nām ak kaṭṭiyit taṭṭiviṭṭāl kāyattiliruntu maṟupaṭi yum irattam vaṭikiṟatu. maṟupaṭiyum oru kaṭṭi tōṉṟi irattattit taṭi ceykiṉṟatu.
namatu uṭal kāyappaṭṭāl ceṅkūṭu, veṇkūṭu, irattatti uṟiyac ceyyum poruḷ eṉpaṉa vellām irattattōṭu veḷiyē celkiṉṟaṉa. irattatti uṟiyac ceyyum poruḷ kāṟṟōṭu campantap paṭumpōtu nuṇṇiya tumpukaḷ pōṉṟu māṟutalaṭikiṉṟatu. ivi oṉ ṟōṭu oṉṟu cērntu oṭṭikkoṇṭu veṇkūṭukaḷiyum ceṅkūṭukaḷi yum veḷiyē cellaviṭātapaṭi taṭiceytu kāyam muḻumiyilum paravi niṉṟu irattatti veḷiyē cellāmal taṭukkiṉṟaṉa. iyaṟki evvāṟu nammip pātukāttu varukiṉṟatu eṉṟu ippoḻutu namakku viḷaṅkukiṟa tallavā?
18. taṅkattiṉkati
āparaṇaṅkaḷ alaṅkāraṅkaḷ vakikaḷillātu nām taṅkattip pārāmal irukkalām. iviyallāta taṅkam ellām nilavaṟikaḷil maṟittu vittup pōrvīrarāl kāval kākkappaṭukiṉṟatu. ivvāṟu maṟintu kiṭantu pakal veḷiccattik kāṇāmal irukkiṉṟa taṅkam ulaka cātikaḷiṉ uyirāka irukkiṉṟatu. atu illāviṭil vāṇikam aḻintu pōkum.
uyirillāta keṭṭiyāṉa ōr ulōkam eppaṭi makkaḷiṭiyē mikap periya atikārattic celuttukiṉṟatu eṉṟa kēḷvi eḻalām. oruvarum itip paṟṟi aṟiyāmal irukkalām. oru paṇakkāraṉ allatu vaṅki naṭattukiṉṟavaṉ itaṟku maṟumoḻi kūṟalām. avaṉuṭiya maṟumoḻi perumpālum tavaṟuṭiyatāka virukkum. paṇappuḻakkañ ceyvōrum vaṅki mutalāḷikaḷum. vāḻnāḷ muḻuvatum taṅkattip paṟṟi ārāycci ceytavarkaḷum taṅkam ulakil ēṉ ivvaḷavu āṭci celuttukiṟatu eṉṉum kēḷvikku viṭi aḷikkamāṭṭātu taṭumāṟukiṉṟaṉar. taṅkam oru ariya ulōkam. kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭa kālam mutal ataṉi maṉitaṉ virumpukiṟāṉ. eṇṇillāta kālam atu maṉitaṉuṭiya maṉōkaṟpaṉi yil kavarcci aḷittamiyāl avaṉ ataṉi illātu vāḻa muṭiyātu eṉa nām kūṟalām. 
taṅkattip paṟṟiya varalāṟṟi eḻutuvatāṉāl atu oru puttaka māka viriyum. taṅkam aḷavillāta makiḻcciyit taruvatu. atu aḷavil lāta tuṉpattiyum ulakukkuk koṭukkiṉṟatu. makkaḷ tuṉpattip paṟṟi niṉippatilli; atu koṭukkum makiḻcciyiyum atikārattiyum paṟṟiyē niṉikkiṟārkaḷ. putiya ōriṭattil taṅkam kiṭikkumāṉāl tāmum taṅkattip peṟum nampikkiyōṭu āyirakkaṇakkāṉa makkaḷ avviṭattukkuc celkiṉṟaṉar. avvāṟu taṅkattip peṟa makkaḷ virintu cellum celavu taṅka ōṭṭam (Gold rush) eṉappaṭum. ivvaki ōṭṭaṅkaḷ ceṉṟa nūṟṟāṇṭil nikaḻntaṉa.
amerikkāvilē kalipōrṉiyāvil 1849ām āṇṭil taṅka vayal kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. ulakil ellāp pakutikaḷiliruntum makkaḷ kalipōrṉiyāvukkuc ceṉṟaṉar. iṅkilāntu, airōppā, āstirēliyā. cīṉā mutaliya nāṭukaḷiliruntu palar ceṉṟaṉar. amerikkāvil āyirak kaṇakkāṉa āṇkaḷum peṇkaḷum tamatu cottukkaḷi viṟṟuviṭṭuk kōvēṟu kaḻutikaḷiyum kūṭāra vaṇṭikaḷiyum payaṉpaṭuttikkoṇṭu ceṉṟārkaḷ. peṇkaḷ. kuḻantikaḷ ciṟuvar mutaliya ellōrum veyi liyum, kuḷiriyum periya malikaḷ āṟukaḷ mūrkkamāṉa cevvintiyar mutaliya āpattukkaḷiyum poruṭpaṭuttātu taṅkattip peṟum nōkkattiyē ilakkākak koṇṭu ceṉṟaṉar. ivvāṟu ceṉṟavarkaḷil ettaṉipēr tamatu apāyaṅkaḷukkum tuṉpaṅkaḷukkum kimmāṟākat taṅkattip peṟṟaṉar. ivvāṟu ceṉṟa varuṭ cilarukkē taṅkam kiṭittatu.
iṉṟum kalipōrṉiyāvil taṅkam kiṭikkiṉṟatu. taṅkam peṟṟuc celvamaṭintōr ciṟu tokiyiṉarāvar. taṅkam peṟṟuc celvamaṭinta ovvoruvaṉukkum nūṟṟukkaṇakkāṉōr vāḻkkikkup paṟṟum paṟṟātu eṉṉum aḷaviṉatākiya taṅkattiyē peṟṟaṉar.
ak kālattil āṟṟiṉ kīḻp paṭikaḷilum ciṟitu akaḻappaṭṭa kuḻi kaḷilum taṅkam kiṭittatu. nīrūṟṟukkaḷil parukkik kaṟkaḷiṉiṭiyē taṅkappoṭi kiṭittatu. ākavē taṅkam aritteṭuppōr muḻaṅkāl maṟiyum nīril niṉṟu tiṉamum parukkikkaṟkaḷi alaciṉar. nāḷ oṉṟukku iraṇṭu veḷḷi (ṭālar) peṟumatiyuḷḷa taṅkam īṭṭiyavaṉ atiṭṭacāliyākak karutappaṭṭāṉ.
itaṟkup piṉ āstirēliyāvil taṅkam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu. aṭutta āṇṭil palar taṅkam eṭuppataṟku aṅku oṭiṉar. iṅkum atiṭṭam peṟṟavar mikac cilarēyāvar; atiṭṭam peṟātavar palar. atiṭṭam peṟṟavarkaḷip paṟṟik kēḷvippaṭumpōtu poṟami aṭiyātu iruttal muṭiyātu. oruvaṉ 2,280 avuṉcu eṭiyuḷḷa taṅkak kaṭṭiyi eṭuttāṉ. iṉṉoruvaṉukku 2217 avuṉcu niṟiyuḷḷa taṅkakkaṭṭi kiṭittatu. oru pāṟiyit taṟceyalāka uṭitta pōtu ataṉuḷ 112 iṟāttal taṅkam kāṇappaṭṭatu. taṅkam arikkappaṭṭa muṟ kālattil ivi pōṉṟa viyappāṉa pala taṅkakkaṭṭikaḷ eṭukkappaṭṭaṉa. palāṭ (Ballart) eṉṉum ciṟiya taṅka vayalil āḻamillāta kuḻi oṉṟil 1800 taṅka nāṇayam peṟumatiyāṉa taṅkam kiṭittatu. itē taṅkavayalil pattuppēr cērntu ceyta oru vāra vēli 10000 taṅka nāṇayam peṟumatiyuḷḷa taṅkatti oruvārattil aḷittatu. itaṟkumuṉ iṅku vēṟucilar 12800 taṅka nāṇayam peṟumatiyuḷḷa taṅkam eṭuttaṉar. itaṟkuppiṉ iv vayal iṉṉoruvarukkup peruntokikku viṟkap paṭṭatu. avarukku muṟiyē 14,400; 9,000; 5,000 taṅkanāṇayam peṟumati yāṉa taṅkam kiṭittatu. ivvāṟu cilarukku atiṭṭam kiṭṭayamiyiṉ ilaṭcakkaṇakkāṉōr tamakkum atiṭṭam kiṭṭumeṉa nampiyatu viyappaṉṟu.
ippoḻutu ivvaḷavu viyakkattakka aḷavil taṅkam kiṭippa tilli. ippoḻutu ulakiluḷḷa ellāt taṅka vayalkaḷilum kūṭṭu vāṇika caṅkaṅkaḷāl taṅkam arikkum toḻil naṭattappaṭukiṉṟatu. iv vaṇika caṅkattiṉar taṅkam arikkum curaṅkaṅkaḷ mika āḻac celkiṉṟaṉa. āṇṭu oṉṟil 30,000,000 avuṉcu taṅkam eṭukkappaṭukiṉṟatu. itaṉ vili 200,000,000 taṅka nāṇayam. it taṅkam ellām eṉṉa ākiṉṟatu? itu muḻuvatum nāṇayaṅkaḷāka aṭikkappaṭuvatilli. itil oru ciṟu pakuti āparaṇañ ceyvōrukku viṟkappaṭukiṉṟatu. āṇṭutōṟum eṭuk kappaṭum taṅkattiṉ perumpakuti pāḷaṅkāḷakkappaṭṭup pala nāṭukaḷi luḷḷa 1vaṅkikaḷukku viṟkappaṭukiṉṟatu. aṅkē appāḷaṅkaḷ kāppum uṟutiyumuḷḷa aṟikaḷil maṟittu vikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. avaṟṟi maṟupaṭiyum makkaḷ kāṇpatu aritu. itu oru viyappāṉa vāṇikamākat tōṉṟukiṉṟatu.
iṉṟu ulakil mikac ceḻippuḷḷa taṅkac curaṅkaṅkaṅkaḷ teṉṉāp pirikkāvilē tirāṉcuvālil uḷḷaṉa. iṅku taṅkam 1885l kaṇṭupiṭikkap paṭṭatu. taṅkam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatu mutal aṅku 1,000,000,000 taṅka nāṇayam viliyuḷḷa taṅkam eṭukkappaṭṭatu. aṅku iṉit taṅkam irukka muṭiyātu eṉṟu cilar niṉikkalām. iṉṉum aimpatu āṇṭukaḷ kaḻintālum aṅku muṉpōl taṅkam kiṭikkum. aṅku āṇṭutōṟum 10,500,000 avuṉcu allatu ulakattil kiṭikkum taṅkattil mūṉṟil oru pakuti taṅkam kiṭikkiṟatu.
1855il taṅkam kaṇṭupiṭikkappaṭṭatiliruntu taṅkam arikkum muṟiyil māṟutal uṇṭāyiṟṟu. muṟkālattil curaṅkavēli ceyvōr akaḻntu nilattiṉ kīḻ ceṉṟu poṉ kalanta maṇṇi allatu pāṟikaḷik kuntāliyāl uṭittu, kiṇaṟṟiliruntu nīraḷḷuvatupōla avaṟṟik kūṭiyiliṭṭu mēlē eṭuttaṉar. ivvāṟu ceyyum potu vāṉa muṟi pōtāteṉat terintu poṟivallār (eñciṉiyarkaḷ) iyan tiraṅkaḷiṉutaviyāl irumpuk kampikaḷik celutti āḻat tuḷittup poṉpāṟikaḷ kiṭakkumiṭaṅkaḷi aṟintu avviṭaṅkaḷil curaṅkam tōṇṭip pāṟikaḷi noṟukki avaṟṟi veḷiyē eṭuttaṉar.
iṉṟu poṉ kiṭikkum pāṟikaḷ veṭimaruntiṉutaviyāl uṭikkappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. curaṅkam akaḻvōr turappaṇattāl pāṟikaḷit tuḷiyiṭṭut tuḷikaḷil “ṭiṉamiṭ” eṉṉum veṭimarunti nirappuvar. avarkaḷ eṭṭattil ceṉṟapiṉ maruntukku neruppu mūṭṭappaṭum. ivvāṟu uṭikkappaṭṭa pāṟit tuṇṭukaḷ veḷiyē eṭukkappaṭu kiṉṟaṉa.
taṅkam aḷikkum pāṟi oru aṅkulam mutal irupataṭivari kaṉamuṭiyatāka virukkum. oru pāṟiyi uṭittapiṉ ataṉ kīḻuḷḷa pāṟiyi uṭippataṟku nilatti akaḻa vēṇṭum. ivvāṟu akaḻap paṭṭa curaṅkam oṉṟiṉ āḻam 7640 aṭi.allatu oṉṟari mil. taṅkam aritteṭuppatil pala muṟikaḷ kiyāḷappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. poṉ tūḷ uḷḷa mutaliyavaṟṟi vāyakaṉṟa irumpu miṭākkaḷil iṭṭu maṇ kaḻuvuṇṭu pōkum vakiyil arippatu oru muṟi.
tirāṉcuvāl curaṅkaṅkaḷil uṭikkappaṭum taṅkap pāṟikaḷ ciṟu ciṟu tuṇṭukaḷākkappaṭṭu oruvaki iracāyaṉap poruḷ nirappiya tēkkaṅkaḷil iṭappaṭukiṉṟaṉa. iracāyaṉap poruḷ taṅkattik karit teṭuttuviṭukiṟatu. it taṅkam palavāṟu cutti ceyyappaṭṭapiṉ kaṭṭikaḷāka aṭikkappaṭukiṉṟatu. taṅkam mikap pāramuḷḷatum ilakuvil vēli ceyyak kūṭiyatumākum. poṉmulām pūcap payaṉpaṭuttappaṭum mika mel liya poṉtāḷkaḷ cuttat taṅkattiliruntu kiyiṉālaṭittuc ceyyap paṭukiṉṟaṉa. irumpum urukkum vayiramuṭiyaṉa vāyiṉum avi turu ēṟi aḻintupōkum iyalpiṉa. taṅkam tēyviṉṟi eppoḻutum orē vakiyāka virukkum.
19. tēyiliyiṉ kati
ellōrum tēnīri ēṉ kuṭikkiṟārkaḷ? etu ellāppāṉaṅ kaḷilum malivāṉatu?
munnūṟu āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ iṅkilāntil oru iṟāttal tēyiliyiṉ vili pattut taṅka nāṇayamāka viruntatu. tēyiliyiṉ varalāṟu itaṟku 4,300 āṇṭukaḷiṉ muṉ toṭaṅkukiṉṟatu.
